## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:54

> **Your Lord knows you best. He may have mercy on you if He wills, or punish you if He wills. We have not sent you ˹O Prophet˺ as a keeper over them.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Your Lord Knows You Best**

Rabbukum aʿlamu bikum — your Lord knows you best, more knowing about you. Rabbukum, your Lord, from r-b-b. Aʿlamu, knows best, more knowing, from ʿ-l-m, to know, as aʿlamu knows best, as verse 25 Lord knows best what is within selves, and verse 47 We know best how they listen, and verse 55 Lord knows best those in heavens and earth. Bikum, with you, about you, you all. Al-Ṭabarī explains that your Lord knows you best, knows your states, whether you deserve mercy or punishment, whether you will believe or disbelieve, as He is All-Aware All-Seeing of servants, knows best, as He knows what is within selves, knows best how they listen, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says rabbukum aʿlamu bikum means Lord knows you best, knows your inner, outer, whether you will be guided or astray, whether mercy or punishment appropriate.

**He May Have Mercy If He Wills Or Punish If He Wills**

In yasha' yarḥamkum aw in yasha' yuʿadhdhibkum — if He wills He will have mercy on you or if He wills He will punish you. In, if. Yasha' from sh-y-ʾ, to will, wish, from shā'a, to will. Yarḥamkum from r-ḥ-m, to have mercy, mercy, from raḥima, to have mercy, as raḥmah mercy. Aw, or. In yasha', if He wills. Yuʿadhdhibkum from ʿ-dh-b, to punish, torment, from ʿadhdhaba, to punish, as ʿadhāb punishment, painful punishment. Al-Ṭabarī explains that He may have mercy on you if He wills, if you repent, believe, follow most upright, or punish you if He wills, if you persist in disbelief, disbelief in Hereafter, outrageous claims, etc., as mercy and punishment by will, with wisdom, knowledge, as He knows you best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but also severe. Al-Rāzī says in yasha' yarḥamkum aw in yasha' yuʿadhdhibkum indicates will, not arbitrary, but based on knowledge, wisdom, as He knows best, and mercy and punishment by will, as test.

**We Have Not Sent You As Keeper Over Them**

Wa-mā arsalnāka ʿalayhim wakīlan — and We have not sent you over them as keeper, guardian, disposer. Wa-mā, and not. Arsalnāka from r-s-l, to send, dispatch, as rasūl messenger, We sent you, O Prophet. ʿAlayhim, over them, upon them, over disbelievers, polytheists. Wakīlan from w-k-l, keeper, guardian, disposer, one entrusted, from wakala, to entrust, from wakīl, keeper, guardian, trustee, disposer of affairs, as Trustee of Affairs in verse 2. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We have not sent you, O Prophet, as keeper over them, guardian, to compel them to believe, to be responsible for their guidance, as guidance is from Allah, not from you, you are deliverer of good news and warner, as verse 105 says We sent you only as deliverer of good news and warner, not keeper, guardian. Al-Qurṭubī says wakīlan means keeper, guardian, disposer, one who disposes affairs, responsible, as Prophet not keeper over them to compel, guide, but warner, deliverer.

**Why Lord Knows Best And Mercy Punishment By Will And Not Keeper**

Why Lord knows best, mercy punishment by will, not keeper? Because disbelievers have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, say bewitched man, call names, far astray cannot find Right Way, deny resurrection bones and ashes new creation, stones, iron, whatever harder, shake heads when, perhaps soon, Day call, etc., and Prophet tells servants to say best to prevent Satan discord, Satan sworn enemy, so Lord knows best, mercy punishment by will, Prophet not keeper over them, not responsible for their guidance, as guidance from Allah, He knows best, may have mercy if wills, punish if wills, as All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving.

**Connection To Lord Knows Best What Is Within Selves And All-Aware All-Seeing**

Verse 25 Lord knows best what is within selves, if righteous All-Forgiving to constantly turning, verse 30 Lord gives abundant or limited provisions, All-Aware All-Seeing of servants, verse 17 sufficient Lord as All-Aware All-Seeing of sins, verse 47 We know best how they listen, verse 54 Lord knows you best, may have mercy or punish if wills, not sent you as keeper, verse 55 Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, favoured some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms. The connection is knowledge theme: Lord knows best what is within selves, how they listen, you best, those in heavens and earth, All-Aware All-Seeing, so mercy punishment by will based on knowledge, not arbitrary, and Prophet not keeper, as knowledge and guidance from Allah.

**Link To Prophet As Warmer Not Keeper And Mercy Punishment**

The sūrah's theme of Prophet as warner, deliverer of good news and warner, not keeper, appears in verse 54 and 105, indicating role: deliver good news to believers who do good mighty reward, warn those who do not believe painful punishment, but not keeper, guardian, to compel, as guidance from Allah, Lord knows best, may have mercy or punish if wills, as will, wisdom.

**Linguistic Note On A'lamu Bikum And Yarhamkum And Yu'adhdhibkum And Wakilan**

Rabb from r-b-b, Lord. Aʿlam from ʿ-l-m, knows best, more knowing. Bikum, with you, about you. Yasha' from sh-y-ʾ, to will. Yarḥam from r-ḥ-m, to have mercy. Yuʿadhdhib from ʿ-dh-b, to punish. Arsalnā from r-s-l, to send. Wakīl from w-k-l, keeper, guardian, trustee, disposer, from wakala. The verse's structure — your Lord knows you best, if He wills He will have mercy on you or if He wills He will punish you, and We have not sent you over them as keeper — with knowledge best, mercy punishment by will, and not sent as keeper, shows that Lord knows best, mercy punishment by will based on knowledge, and Prophet not keeper over them to compel, but warner, deliverer.

**How This Verse Teaches Lord Knows Best And Mercy Punishment By Will**

This verse teaches that Lord knows you best, knows your states, inner, outer, whether mercy or punishment appropriate, may have mercy if wills, if you repent, believe, follow most upright, or punish if wills, if persist in disbelief, disbelief in Hereafter, outrageous claims, etc., as will, wisdom, knowledge, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but also severe, and Prophet not sent as keeper over them, guardian, to compel, guide, as guidance from Allah, not from Prophet, Prophet is deliverer of good news and warner, not keeper. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know Lord knows best, may have mercy if wills, punish if wills, so seek mercy via repentance, belief, most upright, and know Prophet not keeper over disbelievers to compel, but warner, deliverer, so follow guidance, not rely on Prophet to compel, as guidance from Allah.

**A Reflection On Knowing Best And Mercy And Punishment**

Rabbukum aʿlamu bikum — your Lord knows you best — is comforting and warning: comforting that Lord knows you best, knows your struggles, intentions, whether righteous, awwāb, constantly turning, and warning that He knows best whether mercy or punishment appropriate, may have mercy if wills, punish if wills, as will, wisdom, knowledge, All-Aware All-Seeing. Wa-mā arsalnāka ʿalayhim wakīlan — We have not sent you as keeper over them — indicates Prophet's role limited to delivering good news and warning, not keeper, guardian, to compel, as guidance from Allah, Lord knows best, may have mercy or punish, as will. The believer who reflects will seek mercy via being righteous, awwāb, constantly turning, following most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, and avoid punishment, painful punishment, Hell, by believing, not disbelieving in Hereafter, not making outrageous claims, not having barrier, veils, deafness, aversion.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:55

> **Your Lord knows best all those in the heavens and the earth. And We have surely favoured some prophets above others, and to David We gave the Psalms. Other Gods Besides Allah?**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Lord Knows Best All Those In Heavens And Earth**

Wa-rabbuka aʿlamu bi-man fī al-samāwāti wa-al-arḍi — and your Lord knows best who is in heavens and earth. Wa-rabbuka, and your Lord, your Lord, from r-b-b. Aʿlamu, knows best, more knowing, from ʿ-l-m, as previous aʿlamu knows best. Bi-man fī al-samāwāti wa-al-arḍi, with who is in heavens and earth, about those in heavens and earth, all those in heavens and earth, from m-n, who, and s-m-w, heavens, and a-r-ḍ, earth, as seven heavens, earth, all those in them glorify Him, as verse 44 says. Al-Ṭabarī explains that your Lord knows best all those in heavens and earth, who is in them, angels, humans, jinn, etc., knows their states, who deserves mercy, punishment, favouring, etc., as All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best what is within selves, how they listen, you best, those in heavens and earth, all. Al-Qurṭubī says rabbuka aʿlamu bi-man fī al-samāwāti wa-al-arḍi means Lord knows best all those in heavens and earth, knows their inner, outer, who is righteous, who deserves favouring, etc.

**We Favoured Some Prophets Above Others, To David Gave Psalms**

Wa-la-qad faḍḍalnā baʿḍa al-nabiyyīna ʿalā baʿḍin wa-ātaynā Dāwūda zabūran — and We have surely favoured some prophets above others and We gave David Psalms. Wa-la-qad, and surely, indeed, certainly, emphasis. Faḍḍalnā from f-ḍ-l, to favour, prefer, give superiority, as faḍḍalnā some over others in world, and akbaru tafḍīlan greater in favour in Hereafter, and faḍḍalnā some prophets above others, favouring prophets. Baʿḍa al-nabiyyīna, some prophets, some of prophets, from n-b-ʾ, prophets, plural of nabī, prophet, from naba', news. ʿAlā baʿḍin, over others, above others, some above others, in rank, favour, as some prophets favoured above others, as verse 21 some favoured over others in this life but Hereafter far greater in rank and favour, and prophets favoured some above others in rank, favour, as Muhammad favoured, etc. Wa-ātaynā from a-t-y, to give, bring, as ātā give, We gave. Dāwūda, David, prophet David, Dāwūd, from d-w-d, David. Zabūran from z-b-r, Psalms, Scripture, from zabūr, Psalms, Scripture given to David, as Zabūr, Psalms, as Qur'an mentions. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We favoured some prophets above others, some above others in rank, favour, as some prophets given more, and to David We gave Psalms, Zabūr, as example of favouring, as David favoured with Psalms, Scripture, as well as kingdom, etc., as favouring. Al-Qurṭubī says faḍḍalnā baʿḍa al-nabiyyīna ʿalā baʿḍin indicates some prophets favoured above others, as Muhammad favoured above all, etc., and to David gave Psalms as example, as David favoured with Zabūr.

**Other Gods Besides Allah Heading**

Other Gods Besides Allah? appears as editorial heading in translation indicating next section about other gods besides Allah, invoking those claimed besides Him, etc., not part of verse? Actually translation line includes "Other Gods Besides Allah?" at end of line 55, as section marker, but included in verbatim. So blockquote must include exactly as translation line: includes that heading. The phrase Other Gods Besides Allah? is part of translation line as section marker, but included in verbatim, so keep.

**Why Lord Knows Best Those In Heavens Earth And Favoured Prophets**

Why Lord knows best those in heavens and earth and favoured some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms? Because previous verses said Lord knows you best, may have mercy or punish if wills, not sent you as keeper, and now Lord knows best all those in heavens and earth, favoured some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms, indicating that favouring some over others, including prophets, is by knowledge, will, wisdom, as He knows best those in heavens and earth, who deserves favouring, as He favoured some prophets above others, some over others in world, Hereafter far greater in rank and favour, so favouring prophets is part of favouring some over others, by knowledge, will.

**Connection To See How We Favoured Some Over Others And Hereafter Greater**

Verse 21 said see how We favoured some over others in this life but Hereafter far greater in rank and favour. Verse 55 says Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, We favoured some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms, showing that favouring some over others includes prophets, some prophets favoured above others in rank, favour, as part of favouring, and Hereafter far greater in rank and favour, so prophets favoured in world and Hereafter greater. The connection is favouring theme: some favoured over others in world, prophets favoured, Hereafter greater.

**Link To David Psalms And Wisdom And Other Gods**

To David We gave Psalms, Zabūr, as Scripture, wisdom, as part of wisdom revealed, as verse 39 said this is part of wisdom revealed to you, O Prophet, and David given Psalms as wisdom, Scripture, as example of favouring prophets, and next section Other Gods Besides Allah? indicates invoking those claimed besides Him, they have no power, etc., refuting other gods, as monotheism theme.

**Linguistic Note On A'lamu Bi-man And Faddalna And Nabiyyin And Zaburan**

Rabb from r-b-b, Lord. Aʿlam from ʿ-l-m, knows best. Man fī, who is in, those in. Samāwāt from s-m-w, heavens. Arḍ from a-r-ḍ, earth. Faḍḍalnā from f-ḍ-l, to favour, prefer, from faḍala. Baʿḍ from b-ʿ-ḍ, some. Nabiyyīn from n-b-ʾ, prophets, plural of nabī. Ātaynā from a-t-y, to give. Dāwūd from d-w-d, David. Zabūr from z-b-r, Psalms, Scripture, from zabara, to write. The verse's structure — your Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, and We favoured some prophets above others and gave David Psalms — with knowledge best about those in heavens earth and favouring some prophets above others and giving David Psalms as example, shows that Lord knows best, favours some over others by knowledge, including prophets, as wisdom, favouring.

**How This Verse Teaches Lord Knows Best And Favoured Prophets**

This verse teaches that Lord knows best all those in heavens and earth, knows their states, who deserves mercy, punishment, favouring, etc., All-Aware All-Seeing, and that We favoured some prophets above others, some above others in rank, favour, as part of favouring some over others in world, but Hereafter far greater in rank and favour, and to David We gave Psalms, Zabūr, as example of favouring, Scripture, wisdom. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, favours some prophets above others by knowledge, wisdom, and to David gave Psalms, as favouring, and should not claim other gods besides Allah, as next section says, as other gods have no power, etc. The practical lesson is to believe Lord knows best, favours some over others, prophets favoured, Hereafter greater, and to follow favoured Prophet Muhammad, given Qur'an, wisdom, most upright, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving.

**A Reflection On Favoured Prophets And Psalms**

Faḍḍalnā baʿḍa al-nabiyyīna ʿalā baʿḍin — We favoured some prophets above others — indicates that prophets not equal in rank, favour, some favoured above others, as Muhammad favoured above all, as Seal, etc., as part of favouring some over others, by knowledge, will, wisdom, as Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, who deserves favouring. Wa-ātaynā Dāwūda zabūran — We gave David Psalms — as example of favouring, as David given Zabūr, Psalms, Scripture, kingdom, wisdom, as favoured, as well as other prophets given Scriptures, as Moses given Scripture guide for Children of Isra'il, etc., all favouring. The believer who reflects will know Lord knows best, favours some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms, and will follow most favoured Prophet, Muhammad, given Qur'an, wisdom, most upright, guides to most upright, explains everything in detail, varies signs so mindful, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour in Hereafter, and will not invoke other gods besides Allah, as they have no power, etc., as next verses say.

---

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:56

> **Say, ˹O Prophet,˺ “Invoke those you claim ˹to be divine˺ besides Him—they do not have the power to undo harm from you or transfer it ˹to someone else˺.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Invoke Those You Claim Besides Him**

Qul idʿū al-ladhīna zaʿamtum min dūnihi — say, call, invoke those you claimed besides Him, from besides Him. Qul from q-w-l, to say, command to Prophet. Idʿū from d-ʿ-w, to call, invoke, pray, supplicate, as yadʿu human prays, calls, as yadʿūkum He will call you. Al-ladhīna zaʿamtum, those you claimed, asserted, alleged, from z-ʿ-m, to claim, assert, allege, from zaʿama, to claim. Min dūnihi, from besides Him, besides Allah, other than Him, from d-w-n, besides, other than, besides Him. Al-Ṭabarī explains that say, O Prophet, invoke, call those you claim to be divine besides Him, besides Allah, those you claim are gods, like idols, angels as daughters, etc., invoke them, call them, to see if they can help. Al-Qurṭubī says idʿū al-ladhīna zaʿamtum min dūnihi means invoke those you claim besides Him, besides Allah, to be divine, gods, to see if they have power.

**They Have No Power To Undo Harm Or Transfer It**

Fa-lā yamlikūna kashfa al-ḍurri ʿankum wa-lā taḥwīlan — then they do not possess removal of harm from you nor transfer. Fa-lā, then not, so not. Yamlikūna from m-l-k, to possess, own, have power, control, from malaka, to possess, own, have power, as mālik owner. Kashfa from k-sh-f, removal, uncovering, removing, from kashafa, to remove, uncover, as kashf, removal. Al-ḍurri from ḍ-r-r, harm, adversity, affliction, from ḍarra, to harm, from ḍurr, harm. ʿAnkum, from you, away from you. Wa-lā taḥwīlan from ḥ-w-l, transfer, transformation, changing, from ḥawwala, to transfer, transform, change, from taḥwīl, transfer, transformation. Al-Ṭabarī explains that they, those claimed besides Him, do not have power, do not possess ability to remove harm, adversity, affliction from you, nor to transfer it to someone else, change it, transform it, meaning they have no power to help, no power to remove harm nor transfer, so why invoke them, claim them divine? Al-Rāzī says lā yamlikūna kashfa al-ḍurri ʿankum wa-lā taḥwīlan indicates they have no power, no ownership, no control over harm removal or transfer, so they are not divine, not gods, only Allah has power, as He provides, gives abundant or limited, All-Aware All-Seeing, removes harm, etc.

**Why Invoke Those Claimed Besides Him Have No Power**

Why invoke those claimed besides Him have no power? Because polytheists claimed idols, angels as daughters, other gods besides Allah have power to intercede, remove harm, transfer, etc., but they have no power, no ownership, no control, as only Allah has power, as Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, favoured some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms, etc., and seven heavens, earth, all those in them glorify Him, not a thing except glorifies with praise, etc., and Allah Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, etc., so only Allah has power to remove harm, transfer, etc., not those claimed besides Him. The argument refutes other gods besides Allah, as Other Gods Besides Allah? heading indicates.

**Connection To Other Gods Argument And Glorified Exalted And Universal Glorification**

Verses 42-44 had there been other gods they would have sought way to Lord of Throne, Glorified Highly Exalted above what they claim, seven heavens, earth, all those in them glorify Him, not a thing except glorifies with praise, but you cannot comprehend, He is Forbearing Forgiving. Verse 56 says invoke those you claim besides Him they have no power to remove harm or transfer, showing they have no power, not divine, only Allah glorified, exalted, glorified by all creation, has power, so invoke Him alone, not other gods.

**Link To Closest Of Those Invoked Seek Way To Lord Hoping Mercy Fearing Punishment**

Next verse 57 says even closest of those invoked would be seeking way to their Lord, hoping for mercy, fearing punishment, indeed Lord's torment fearsome, indicating that even those invoked, closest to Allah, like angels, prophets, etc., themselves seek way to Lord, hope mercy, fear punishment, so they are not divine, not gods, but servants seeking Lord, so how can they be invoked as gods? They themselves seek Lord.

**Linguistic Note On Id'u And Za'amtum And Yamlikuna And Kashf And Tahwil**

Qul from q-w-l, to say. Idʿū from d-ʿ-w, to call, invoke. Ladhīna zaʿamtum, those you claimed, from z-ʿ-m, to claim. Min dūnihi, besides Him, from d-w-n. Yamlikūna from m-l-k, to possess, own, have power. Kashf from k-sh-f, removal, uncovering, from kashafa. Ḍurr from ḍ-r-r, harm, adversity. ʿAnkum, from you. Taḥwīl from ḥ-w-l, transfer, transformation, from ḥawwala. The verse's structure — say invoke those you claimed besides Him, then they do not possess removal of harm from you nor transfer — with command to invoke those claimed besides Him and statement they have no power to remove harm or transfer, shows refutation of other gods besides Allah, as they have no power, no ownership, no control, so not divine, only Allah has power.

**How This Verse Teaches Other Gods Have No Power**

This verse teaches that other gods claimed besides Allah have no power to remove harm from you or transfer it to someone else, no ownership, no control, so they are not divine, not gods, only Allah has power, as He provides, gives abundant or limited, All-Aware All-Seeing, removes harm, etc., and seven heavens, earth, all those in them glorify Him, etc., He is Forbearing Forgiving, Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should not invoke those claimed besides Him, besides Allah, as they have no power to remove harm or transfer, no ownership, no control, so invoke Allah alone, who has power, who provides, removes harm, transfers, etc., as most upright, monotheism, wisdom, part of wisdom revealed, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving. The practical lesson is to invoke Allah alone, not other gods claimed besides Him, as they have no power, no ownership, to remove harm or transfer, so trust Allah alone, who is Lord, knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving.

**A Reflection On No Power To Remove Harm Or Transfer**

Lā yamlikūna kashfa al-ḍurri ʿankum wa-lā taḥwīlan — they do not possess removal of harm from you nor transfer — indicates complete lack of power, ownership, control: they cannot remove harm, adversity, affliction, from you, nor transfer it to someone else, change it, transform it, so they are powerless, not divine, not gods, only Allah has power, ownership, control, to remove harm, transfer, etc., as He gives abundant or limited provisions, All-Aware All-Seeing, bounty never withheld, etc. The believer who reflects will invoke Allah alone, who has power to remove harm and transfer, not those claimed besides Him who have no power, no ownership, as they are powerless, not divine, as even closest of those invoked seek way to Lord, hope mercy, fear punishment, Lord's torment fearsome, as next verse says, so they themselves are servants, not gods.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:57

> **˹Even˺ the closest ˹to Allah˺ of those invoked would be seeking a way to their Lord, hoping for His mercy, and fearing His punishment. Indeed, your Lord’s torment is fearsome.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Closest Of Those Invoked Seek Way To Lord**

Ulā'ika al-ladhīna yadʿūna yabtaghūna ilā rabbihim al-wasīlata ayyuhum aqrabu — those whom they invoke seek to their Lord means, way, which of them is closest. Ulā'ika, those, those invoked, those claimed besides Him, like angels, prophets, etc., closest to Allah. Al-ladhīna yadʿūna from d-ʿ-w, those whom they invoke, call, as idʿū invoke those you claimed. Yabtaghūna from b-gh-y, to seek, desire, as ibtaghā seeking bounty, seeking way, as previous ibtaghaw sought way to Lord of Throne, and yabtaghūna seek means to Lord. Ilā rabbihim, to their Lord, to their Lord, from r-b-b. Al-wasīlata from w-s-l, means, way, means of nearness, from wasīlah, means, way, means to approach, nearness. Ayyuhum aqrabu, which of them is closest, nearer, from q-r-b, close, near, as aqrab closest. Al-Ṭabarī explains that even closest to Allah of those invoked, those whom polytheists invoke besides Allah, like angels, prophets, etc., would be seeking a way, means, to their Lord, which of them is closest, competing to be closest to Lord, seeking nearness, as they themselves are servants seeking Lord, not gods. Al-Qurṭubī says ulā'ika al-ladhīna yadʿūna yabtaghūna ilā rabbihim al-wasīlata ayyuhum aqrabu means those invoked themselves seek means to their Lord, which is closest, as they are servants, not gods, seeking nearness to Lord, competing.

**Hoping For Mercy And Fearing Punishment**

Wa-yarjūna raḥmatahu wa-yakhāfūna ʿadhābahu — and they hope His mercy and fear His punishment. Wa-yarjūna from r-j-w, to hope, expect, as tarjūhā you hope, hoping for Lord's bounty, mercy. Raḥmatahu from r-ḥ-m, His mercy, from raḥmah, mercy. Wa-yakhāfūna from kh-w-f, to fear, dread, from khāfa, to fear. ʿAdhābahu from ʿ-dh-b, His punishment, torment, from ʿadhāb, punishment. Al-Ṭabarī explains that those invoked, even closest, hope for His mercy, expect mercy, and fear His punishment, dread punishment, as they are servants, not gods, fearing, hoping, as all servants hope mercy, fear punishment, as Lord knows best, may have mercy if wills or punish if wills, as will, wisdom. Al-Rāzī says yarjūna raḥmatahu wa-yakhāfūna ʿadhābahu indicates they themselves are in need of mercy, fearing punishment, so how can they be gods, divine, invoked besides Allah? They themselves need Lord.

**Lord's Torment Fearsome**

Inna ʿadhāba rabbika kāna maḥdhūran — indeed punishment of your Lord is feared, fearsome, to be feared, guarded against. Inna, indeed, surely. ʿAdhāba from ʿ-dh-b, punishment, torment, as ʿadhāban alīman painful punishment, and ʿadhābahu His punishment. Rabbika, your Lord, from r-b-b. Kāna, is, was, indicates it is. Maḥdhūran from ḥ-dh-r, feared, to be feared, guarded against, from ḥadhira, to fear, beware, from maḥdhūr, feared, to be feared, guarded against, as fearsome, to be feared. Al-Ṭabarī explains that indeed punishment, torment of your Lord is fearsome, to be feared, guarded against, feared, as it is severe, painful, as Hell, painful punishment prepared for those who do not believe in Hereafter, as torment fearsome. Al-Qurṭubī says maḥdhūran means feared, to be feared, guarded against, as Lord's torment fearsome, severe, should be feared.

**Why Closest Invoked Seek Way And Hope Mercy Fear Punishment**

Why closest of those invoked seek way to Lord, hope mercy, fear punishment, torment fearsome? Because those invoked, like angels, prophets, etc., even closest to Allah, are servants, not gods, not divine, themselves need Lord, seek means, way, to be closest, hope mercy, fear punishment, as all servants, as they themselves glorify Him, seven heavens, earth, all those in them glorify Him, etc., and not a thing except glorifies with praise, but you cannot comprehend, He is Forbearing Forgiving, but torment fearsome, so they seek mercy, fear punishment, as servants.

**Connection To Invoke Those Claimed Besides Him No Power**

Verse 56 said invoke those you claim besides Him they have no power to remove harm or transfer. Verse 57 says even closest of those invoked would be seeking way to their Lord, hoping mercy, fearing punishment, indeed Lord's torment fearsome, showing that those invoked themselves are servants seeking Lord, not gods, no power, need mercy, fear punishment, so how can they be invoked as gods? They themselves seek Lord, hope mercy, fear punishment, torment fearsome, so invoke Lord alone, not them.

**Link To Lord Knows Best Those In Heavens Earth And Favoured Prophets**

Verse 55 Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, favoured some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms. Verse 57 closest of those invoked seek way to Lord, hoping mercy, fearing punishment, torment fearsome, showing that those in heavens and earth, including closest, like angels, prophets, etc., seek Lord, know best, favoured, but still servants, not gods, need mercy, fear punishment, as Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, favoured some prophets above others, but all servants.

**Linguistic Note On Yabtaghuna And Wasilah And Yarjuna And Yakhaafuna And Mahdhuran**

Ulā'ika, those. Ladhīna yadʿūna, those whom they invoke, from d-ʿ-w. Yabtaghūna from b-gh-y, to seek, desire, from ibtaghā. Rabb from r-b-b, Lord. Wasīlah from w-s-l, means, way, means of nearness, from wasala, to be near. Ayyuhum aqrabu, which of them is closest, from q-r-b, close. Yarjūna from r-j-w, to hope, expect. Raḥmah from r-ḥ-m, mercy. Yakhāfūna from kh-w-f, to fear. ʿAdhāb from ʿ-dh-b, punishment. Maḥdhūr from ḥ-dh-r, feared, to be feared, from ḥadhira. The verse's structure — those whom they invoke seek to their Lord means which is closest and hope His mercy and fear His punishment, indeed punishment of your Lord is fearsome — with seeking means, hoping mercy, fearing punishment, and torment fearsome, shows that those invoked themselves are servants seeking Lord, not gods, need mercy, fear punishment, torment fearsome, so invoke Lord alone.

**How This Verse Teaches Those Invoked Are Servants Not Gods**

This verse teaches that even closest to Allah of those invoked, those claimed besides Him, like angels, prophets, etc., would be seeking way, means, to their Lord, which of them is closest, competing to be closest, hoping for His mercy, expecting mercy, and fearing His punishment, dreading punishment, indeed Lord's torment is fearsome, to be feared, guarded against, so they themselves are servants, not gods, not divine, need Lord, seek nearness, hope mercy, fear punishment, so how can they be invoked as gods besides Allah? They themselves seek Lord. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know those invoked besides Allah, even closest, like angels, prophets, are servants seeking Lord, hoping mercy, fearing punishment, torment fearsome, so invoke Allah alone, not them, as they have no power to remove harm or transfer, no power, as previous verse, and they themselves seek Lord.

**A Reflection On Seeking Means And Hoping Mercy Fearing Punishment**

Yabtaghūna ilā rabbihim al-wasīlata ayyuhum aqrabu — seek to their Lord means which is closest — indicates competition among closest invoked to be closest to Lord, seeking means, nearness, as servants, not gods, as all servants seek nearness to Lord, compete, as believers should compete for good, for ranks far greater in Hereafter, as Hereafter far greater in rank and favour. Wa-yarjūna raḥmatahu wa-yakhāfūna ʿadhābahu — hope His mercy and fear His punishment — indicates balance of hope and fear, as servants hope mercy, fear punishment, as Lord may have mercy if wills or punish if wills, as will, wisdom, knowledge, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, maḥdhūran, to be feared, guarded against, so hope mercy, fear punishment, as servants, not gods, so invoke Lord alone, who has mercy, punishment, torment fearsome, but also Forbearing Forgiving.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:58

> **There is not a ˹wicked˺ society that We will not destroy or punish with a severe torment before the Day of Judgment. That is written in the Record.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Not A Wicked Society That We Will Not Destroy Or Punish Before Day Of Judgment**

Wa-in min qaryatin illā naḥnu muhlikūhā qabla yawmi al-qiyāmati aw muʿadhdhibūhā ʿadhāban shadīdan — and there is not a town, society, except We will destroy it or punish it with severe torment before Day of Resurrection. Wa-in, and not, there is not. Min qaryatin, a town, society, settlement, from q-r-y, town, as qaryah town, society, as previous qaryah when intend to destroy society, command elite but they rebel, decree justified, destroy utterly, and how many generations destroyed after Noah, etc. Illā, except. Naḥnu, We, Allah. Muhlikūhā from h-l-k, destroyers, will destroy, from ahlaka, to destroy, as nuhlika destroy, ahlaknā destroyed, muhlikūhā destroyers, will destroy. Qabla yawmi al-qiyāmati, before Day of Resurrection, Day of Standing, from q-b-l, before, and y-w-m, Day, and q-w-m, Resurrection. Aw muʿadhdhibūhā, or punish it, torment it, from ʿ-dh-b, to punish, torment, as muʿadhdhibīn punishers, muʿadhdhibūhā punishers, will punish. ʿAdhāban shadīdan, punishment severe, torment severe, from ʿ-dh-b and sh-d-d, severe, strong, from shadīdan severe. Al-Ṭabarī explains that there is not a wicked society, town, that We will not destroy or punish with severe torment before Day of Judgment, meaning every society, town, that is wicked, disbelieving, will be destroyed or punished with severe torment before Day of Judgment, either destruction or severe punishment, as divine law, as many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., as qaryah when intend to destroy, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says qaryah here means society, town, settlement, wicked, disbelieving, that will be destroyed or punished severely before Day of Judgment, as either destruction utterly or severe torment, as test, punishment, as history shows.

**That Is Written In Record**

Kāna dhālika fī al-kitābi masṭūran — that is in Book, Record, written, inscribed. Kāna, is, was, indicates it is. Dhālika, that, that decree, destruction or severe punishment before Day. Fī al-kitābi, in Book, Record, in Record, Preserved Tablet, Book, as kitāb Book, Record, as verse 13 record laid open, and verse 58 Record, Book. Masṭūran from s-ṭ-r, written, inscribed, lined, from saṭara, to write, inscribe, line, from masṭūr, written, inscribed, lined, as Record written. Al-Ṭabarī explains that that, destruction or severe punishment of wicked societies before Day of Judgment, is written in Record, Book, Preserved Tablet, inscribed, decreed, as divine decree, written, as everything explained in detail, as everything in Record, as destiny bound to neck, etc. Al-Rāzī says kāna dhālika fī al-kitābi masṭūran indicates that destruction or severe punishment before Day is written in Record, Book, Preserved Tablet, as divine decree, preordained, as part of divine knowledge, will, as Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, etc.

**Why Every Wicked Society Destroyed Or Punished Severely Before Day**

Why every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day? Because divine law: when intention to destroy society, command elite but they rebel, decree justified, destroy utterly, as verse 16 says, and how many generations destroyed after Noah, as verse 17 says, and Lord All-Aware All-Seeing of sins, sufficient, etc., and provision abundant or limited, etc., but wicked societies that rebel, corrupt, become extremely arrogant, as Children of Isra'il twice, etc., will be destroyed or punished severely before Day, as either destruction utterly or severe torment, as either via servants of great might, or via other punishments, as history shows, as warning.

**Connection To Destroying Society When Elite Rebel And How Many Destroyed After Noah**

Verse 16 whenever We intend to destroy society We command its elite but they rebel so decree justified destroy utterly, verse 17 how many peoples destroyed after Noah, sufficient Lord as All-Aware All-Seeing of sins, verse 58 there is not wicked society that We will not destroy or punish severely before Day, that is written in Record. The connection is that destruction or severe punishment of wicked societies before Day is divine law, illustrated by many generations destroyed after Noah, and by principle when intend to destroy society command elite but they rebel, decree justified, destroy utterly, and is written in Record, Book, as decree, preordained, as part of divine knowledge, will.

**Link To Record Laid Open And Record Written And Detailed Explanation**

Verse 13 record laid open, bound destiny to neck, record wide open, verse 14 read your book, sufficient self as reckoner, verse 58 that is written in Record, masṭūran, written, inscribed, as Record, Book, Preserved Tablet, where destruction or severe punishment written, as everything explained in detail, as verse 12 everything explained in detail, and as destiny bound to neck, etc., and as Record, Book, will be laid open on Day, read, etc., so destruction or severe punishment written in Record as part of detailed explanation.

**Linguistic Note On Qaryah And Muhlikuhā And Mu'adhdhibuhā And Kitab And Masturan**

Qaryah from q-r-y, town, society, settlement, from qarā, to gather. Muhlikūhā from h-l-k, destroyers, will destroy, from ahlaka, to destroy, from halaka, to perish. Qabla from q-b-l, before. Yawm from y-w-m, Day. Qiyāmah from q-w-m, Resurrection, Standing. Muʿadhdhibūhā from ʿ-dh-b, punishers, will punish, from ʿadhdhaba, to punish. ʿAdhāb from ʿ-dh-b, punishment. Shadīd from sh-d-d, severe, strong, from shadda, to be strong, severe. Kitāb from k-t-b, Book, Record, from kataba, to write. Masṭūr from s-ṭ-r, written, inscribed, from saṭara, to write, inscribe. The verse's structure — there is not a town except We will destroy it or punish it severely before Day of Resurrection, that is in Book written — with universal statement no town except destroyed or punished severely before Day and statement that is written in Record, shows divine law of destruction or severe punishment for wicked societies before Day, written in Record, preordained, as part of divine knowledge, will.

**How This Verse Teaches Divine Law Of Destruction Or Severe Punishment**

This verse teaches divine law: there is not a wicked society, town, that We will not destroy or punish with severe torment before Day of Judgment, that is written in Record, Book, inscribed, as decree, preordained, as part of divine knowledge, will, as Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, All-Aware All-Seeing of sins, sufficient, etc., and as many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., and as when intend to destroy society command elite but they rebel, decree justified, destroy utterly. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know every wicked society will be destroyed or punished severely before Day, as divine law, written in Record, so avoid wickedness, corruption, arrogance, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding destruction, severe punishment, painful punishment, Hell. The practical lesson is to avoid being wicked society, corrupt, arrogant, follow most upright, be righteous, awwāb, constantly turning, as Lord Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and destruction or severe punishment written in Record for wicked.

**A Reflection On Written In Record**

Masṭūran fī al-kitābi — written in Record, Book, inscribed — indicates that destruction or severe punishment of wicked societies before Day is not random, not arbitrary, but written, inscribed, in Record, Book, Preserved Tablet, as divine decree, preordained, as part of divine knowledge, will, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, knows sins, sufficient, and as everything explained in detail, detailed explanation, and as destiny bound to neck, record laid open, read book, sufficient self as reckoner, etc., and as Record will be laid open on Day, read, etc., so destruction or severe punishment written in Record as part of detailed explanation, divine law, warning, so avoid wickedness, follow most upright, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding destruction, severe punishment, painful punishment, Hell, as torment fearsome, but Lord Most Forbearing All-Forgiving to those who constantly turn.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:59

> **Nothing keeps Us from sending the ˹demanded˺ signs except that they had ˹already˺ been denied by earlier peoples. And We gave Thamûd the shecamel as a clear sign, but they wrongfully rejected it. We only send the signs as a warning.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Nothing Keeps Us From Sending Demanded Signs Except They Were Denied By Earlier**

Wa-mā manaʿanā an nursila bi-l-āyāti illā an kadhdhaba bihā al-awwalūna — and nothing prevented Us that We send with signs except that they were denied by earlier, first peoples. Wa-mā, and not, nothing. Manaʿanā from m-n-ʿ, to prevent, keep, withhold, from manaʿa, to prevent, keep. An nursila from r-s-l, to send, dispatch, as rasūl messenger, We send, as nursila We send with signs. Bi-l-āyāti from a-y-y, signs, proofs, miracles, from āyah, sign, as āyatayni two signs night and day, etc. Illā an kadhdhaba, except that they denied, disbelieved, from k-dh-b, to deny, lie, disbelieve, as kadhdhaba denied. Bihā, with them, about them, signs. Al-awwalūna from a-w-l, earlier, first peoples, earlier generations, from awwal, first, earlier. Al-Ṭabarī explains that nothing keeps Us, prevents Us from sending demanded signs, miracles, that disbelievers demand, except that they, signs, had already been denied by earlier peoples, first generations, who demanded signs then denied them, so were destroyed, as divine law, as many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., and as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, so if We send demanded signs and they deny, they will be destroyed, punished severely, as earlier peoples, so We do not send demanded signs to avoid hastening destruction, as forbearance, as He is Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome.

**We Gave Thamud Shecamel As Clear Sign But They Wrongfully Rejected**

Wa-ātaynā Thamūda al-nāqata mubṣiratan fa-ẓalamū bihā — and We gave Thamud shecamel as clear sign, bright, giving sight, but they wronged with it, wrongfully rejected it. Wa-ātaynā from a-t-y, to give, bring, as ātaynā We gave, as ātaynā David Psalms, etc. Thamūda, Thamud, people of Thamud, tribe of Thamud, who denied prophet Ṣāliḥ. Al-nāqata from n-w-q, shecamel, female camel, as nāqah, shecamel, as sign for Thamud, shecamel as clear sign, miracle. Mubṣiratan from b-ṣ-r, clear, bright, giving sight, making visible, as mubṣiratan bright sign of day, giving sight, as clear sign, visible, clear, as mubṣiratan clear sign. Fa-ẓalamū bihā from ẓ-l-m, to wrong, oppress, do injustice, wrongfully reject, from ẓalama, to wrong, oppress, as ẓālimūn wrongdoers. Bihā, with it, about it, shecamel. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We gave Thamud shecamel as clear sign, bright, visible, clear miracle, as sign, but they wronged with it, wrongfully rejected it, oppressed, did injustice, by hamstringing shecamel, killing it, denying sign, so were destroyed, punished severely, as example of earlier peoples who denied signs, demanded signs then denied, destroyed.

**We Only Send Signs As Warning**

Wa-mā nursilu bi-l-āyāti illā takhwīfan — and We do not send with signs except as warning, fear, to frighten. Wa-mā nursilu, and We do not send, We send not. Bi-l-āyāti, with signs, with miracles, signs. Illā takhwīfan from kh-w-f, warning, fear, to frighten, make fear, from khawwafa, to frighten, warn, from takhwīf, warning, frightening, making fear. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We only send signs, miracles, as warning, to frighten, warn, make fear, so that people may be mindful, remember, take heed, fear punishment, as signs are warning, not for entertainment, not to be demanded mockingly, but as warning, to frighten, so that they may believe, repent, as varying signs so they may be mindful, but drives farther away for disbelievers.

**Why No Demanded Signs Because Earlier Denied And Thamud Shecamel Example**

Why nothing keeps Us from sending demanded signs except they were denied by earlier peoples, and Thamud shecamel clear sign wrongfully rejected, We only send signs as warning? Because divine law: if demanded signs sent and denied, destruction, severe punishment follows, as earlier peoples denied signs, destroyed, as many generations destroyed after Noah, every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, so to avoid hastening destruction, forbearance, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, We do not send demanded signs that disbelievers demand mockingly, as they would deny and be destroyed, as Thamud given shecamel clear sign but wrongfully rejected, hamstrung, destroyed, as example. We only send signs as warning, to frighten, not as demanded entertainment, mocking, but as warning, so they may be mindful, fear.

**Connection To Varying Signs So They May Be Mindful But Drives Farther Away**

Verse 41 varied signs in Qur'an so they may be mindful but drives farther away, nufūran, aversion. Verse 59 nothing keeps Us from sending demanded signs except they were denied by earlier peoples, We gave Thamud shecamel clear sign but they wrongfully rejected, We only send signs as warning, showing that varying signs in Qur'an is warning, not demanded signs that would lead to destruction if denied, as earlier peoples denied and destroyed, as Thamud shecamel example, so Qur'an signs are warning, not demanded miracles that would hasten destruction, as forbearance.

**Link To Seven Heavens Earth Glorify And Hidden Barrier And Veils**

Verse 44 seven heavens, earth, all those in them glorify Him, not a thing except glorifies with praise, but you cannot comprehend, He is Forbearing Forgiving. Verse 45-46 hidden barrier, veils, deafness, aversion when Lord alone mentioned. Verse 59 signs as warning, not demanded, as earlier denied, Thamud shecamel, showing that signs are warning, forbearance, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, so warning, not demanded signs that would lead to destruction if denied, as forbearance.

**Linguistic Note On Mana'ana And Ayat And Kadhdhaba And Naqah And Mubshirah And Zalamu And Takhwif**

Manaʿa from m-n-ʿ, to prevent, keep, withhold, from manaʿa. Nursila from r-s-l, to send. Āyāt from a-y-y, signs, miracles, plural of āyah. Kadhdhaba from k-dh-b, to deny, disbelieve, from kadhdhaba. Awwalūn from a-w-l, earlier, first peoples, plural of awwal. Ātaynā from a-t-y, to give. Thamūd, Thamud, people name. Nāqah from n-w-q, shecamel, from nāqah. Mubṣirah from b-ṣ-r, clear, bright, giving sight, from abṣara, to make see. Ẓalamū from ẓ-l-m, to wrong, oppress, from ẓalama. Takhwīf from kh-w-f, warning, frightening, from khawwafa. The verse's structure — nothing prevented Us that We send with signs except that they were denied by earlier, We gave Thamud shecamel clear sign but they wronged with it, We send not with signs except warning — with reason no demanded signs because earlier denied, example Thamud shecamel clear sign wrongfully rejected, and purpose signs as warning, shows divine law, forbearance, warning, not demanded entertainment.

**How This Verse Teaches Signs As Warning Not Demanded Entertainment**

This verse teaches that nothing keeps Allah from sending demanded signs, miracles, that disbelievers demand mockingly, except that they, signs, had already been denied by earlier peoples, first generations, who demanded then denied, destroyed, punished severely, as many generations destroyed after Noah, every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, so to avoid hastening destruction, forbearance, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, He does not send demanded signs that would lead to destruction if denied, as Thamud given shecamel clear sign, bright, but they wrongfully rejected, wronged with it, hamstrung, destroyed, as example, and We only send signs as warning, to frighten, warn, make fear, so they may be mindful, remember, take heed, not as demanded entertainment, mocking, but as warning. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know signs, miracles, are warning, not demanded entertainment, should be mindful, fear, believe, not demand signs mockingly, not deny as earlier peoples, Thamud, etc., who denied and destroyed.

**A Reflection On Thamud Shecamel And Warning**

Al-nāqata mubṣiratan — shecamel clear sign, bright, giving sight, visible, clear miracle — for Thamud, people of Ṣāliḥ, as clear sign, miracle, shecamel from rock, etc., but they wronged with it, ẓalamū bihā, wrongfully rejected, oppressed, hamstrung, killed, denied, so destroyed, punished severely, as example of earlier peoples who denied signs, demanded signs then denied, destroyed, as divine law, every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record. Wa-mā nursilu bi-l-āyāti illā takhwīfan — We only send signs as warning, to frighten, warn — indicates purpose of signs, miracles, is warning, frightening, not entertainment, mocking, demanded, but warning, so they may be mindful, fear, believe, repent, as varying signs in Qur'an so they may be mindful, but drives farther away for disbelievers with barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way. The believer who reflects will see signs as warning, be mindful, fear, believe, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding destruction, severe punishment, painful punishment, Hell, torment fearsome.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:60

> **And ˹remember, O Prophet˺ when We told you, “Certainly your Lord encompasses the people.” And We have made what We brought you to see as well as the cursed tree ˹mentioned˺ in the Quran only as a test for the people. We keep warning them, but it only increases them greatly in defiance.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Remember When We Told You Lord Encompasses People**

Wa-idh qulnā laka inna rabbaka aḥāṭa bi-l-nāsi — and when We said to you, indeed your Lord encompasses people, surrounds people. Wa-idh, and when, remember when. Qulnā from q-w-l, to say, We said. Laka, to you, O Prophet. Inna rabbaka, indeed your Lord, your Lord, from r-b-b. Aḥāṭa from ḥ-w-ṭ, to encompass, surround, enclose, from aḥāṭa, to encompass, surround, enclose, as encompasses, surrounds, knows, controls, as Lord encompasses people, surrounds, knows, controls, as He knows best those in heavens and earth, etc. Bi-l-nāsi, with people, about people, people, from n-w-s, people, as nās people, humankind. Al-Ṭabarī explains that remember when We told you, O Prophet, certainly your Lord encompasses people, surrounds, encloses, knows, controls people, meaning He encompasses them with knowledge, power, protection, so do not fear, as He encompasses, surrounds, protects, as He knows best, etc., as He told Prophet Lord encompasses people, so be steadfast, as He encompasses. Al-Qurṭubī says aḥāṭa bi-l-nāsi means encompasses people with knowledge, power, protection, surrounds, encloses, so Prophet protected, as Lord encompasses.

**We Made What We Brought You To See And Cursed Tree As Test**

Wa-mā jaʿalnā al-ru'yā allatī araynāka illā fitnatan li-l-nāsi wa-al-shajarata al-malʿūnata fī al-Qur'āni — and We did not make vision which We showed you except as test for people and tree cursed in Qur'an. Wa-mā jaʿalnā, and We did not make, We made not. Al-ru'yā from r-ʾ-y, vision, what was seen, dream, vision, from ra'ā, to see, as ru'yā vision, what was brought to see, as Night Journey vision, what Prophet was brought to see, as Isra and Mi'raj, vision of signs, as verse 1 took servant by night to show signs, and as what We brought you to see, vision. Allatī araynāka, which We showed you, made you see, from r-ʾ-y, to show, make see, as araynāka We showed you, made you see. Illā fitnatan, except as test, trial, temptation, from f-t-n, test, trial, from fitnah, test, trial. Li-l-nāsi, for people, to people, as test for people. Wa-al-shajarata al-malʿūnata, and tree cursed, accursed tree, from sh-j-r, tree, and l-ʿ-n, cursed, accursed, from laʿana, to curse, as malʿūnah cursed. Fī al-Qur'āni, in Qur'an, mentioned in Qur'an, as cursed tree mentioned in Qur'an, as Zaqqūm tree, cursed tree, etc., as in 37:62-64, 44:43-44, etc., cursed tree. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We did not make vision which We showed you, Night Journey, Isra, what Prophet saw, signs, etc., except as test for people, trial, to test who believes, who disbelieves, as some believed Night Journey, some disbelieved, mocked, increased in defiance, as test, and tree cursed mentioned in Qur'an, Zaqqūm, cursed tree, as test for people, as disbelievers said how can tree grow in Hell, etc., as test, trial.

**We Keep Warning Them But Increases Greatly In Defiance**

Wa-nukhawwifuhum fa-mā yazīduhum illā ṭughyānan kabīran — and We warn them, frighten them, but it increases them not except in great defiance, transgression. Wa-nukhawwifuhum from kh-w-f, to warn, frighten, make fear, as takhwīfan warning, and nukhawwifuhum We warn them, frighten them, as We only send signs as warning, and We keep warning them. Fa-mā yazīduhum, but it increases them not, does not increase them, from z-y-d, to increase, as yazīduhum increases them, as verse 41 it only drives them farther away, nufūran, aversion, and verse 60 increases greatly in defiance. Illā ṭughyānan kabīran, except defiance great, transgression great, from ṭ-gh-y, defiance, transgression, exceeding limits, from ṭaghā, to defy, transgress, exceed, from ṭughyān, defiance, transgression, and kabīran, great, big, from k-b-r, great, as ṭughyānan kabīran great defiance, great transgression. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We keep warning them, frighten them, with signs, warnings, etc., but it only increases them greatly in defiance, transgression, exceeding limits, great defiance, as they have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, deny resurrection, etc., and warning increases defiance, as varying signs drives farther away, nufūran.

**Why Lord Encompasses People And Vision And Cursed Tree As Test And Warning Increases Defiance**

Why Lord encompasses people, vision and cursed tree as test, warning increases greatly in defiance? Because Prophet told Lord encompasses people, so be steadfast, not fear, as Lord encompasses, surrounds, knows, controls, protects, and vision, Night Journey, what Prophet saw, and cursed tree mentioned in Qur'an, Zaqqūm, are test for people, trial, to test who believes, who disbelieves, as some believed, some disbelieved, mocked, and warning, signs, only increase disbelievers greatly in defiance, transgression, as they have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray, etc., and varying signs drives farther away, nufūran, aversion, and warning increases defiance.

**Connection To Night Journey And Signs As Warning And Varying Signs Drives Farther Away**

Verse 1 Night Journey, Glory be to One Who took servant by night to show signs. Verse 60 vision which We showed you, Night Journey, as test, and cursed tree in Qur'an as test, We keep warning them but increases greatly in defiance, showing that Night Journey vision and cursed tree are test, warning, but disbelievers increase in defiance, as varying signs in Qur'an so they may be mindful but drives farther away, nufūran, aversion, and We only send signs as warning, takhwīfan, and We keep warning them but increases greatly in defiance, ṭughyānan kabīran, great defiance, showing that warning, signs, test, increase defiance for disbelievers with barrier, veils, deafness, aversion.

**Link To Lord Encompasses People And Lord Knows Best And Forbearing Forgiving**

Verse 54-55 Lord knows you best, may have mercy or punish if wills, not sent you as keeper, Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, favoured some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms. Verse 60 Lord encompasses people, surrounds, knows, controls, so be steadfast, as He encompasses, as He knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and vision and cursed tree as test, warning increases defiance, showing Lord encompasses, knows, tests, warns, but disbelievers increase defiance.

**Linguistic Note On Ahata And Ru'ya And Araynaka And Fitnah And Shajarah Mal'unah And Nukhawwifuhum And Tughyan**

Qulnā from q-w-l, to say. Rabb from r-b-b, Lord. Aḥāṭa from ḥ-w-ṭ, to encompass, surround, enclose, from aḥāṭa. Nās from n-w-s, people. Jaʿalnā from j-ʿ-l, to make. Ru'yā from r-ʾ-y, vision, what was seen, from ra'ā, to see. Araynāka from r-ʾ-y, to show, make see, from arā, to show. Fitnah from f-t-n, test, trial, from fatana, to test. Shajarah from sh-j-r, tree. Malʿūnah from l-ʿ-n, cursed, accursed, from laʿana, to curse. Qur'ān from q-r-ʾ, recitation. Nukhawwifuhum from kh-w-f, to warn, frighten, from khawwafa. Yazīdu from z-y-d, to increase. Ṭughyān from ṭ-gh-y, defiance, transgression, from ṭaghā, to defy, transgress. Kabīr from k-b-r, great. The verse's structure — when We said to you indeed your Lord encompasses people, and We did not make vision which We showed you except as test for people and tree cursed in Qur'an, and We warn them but it increases them not except great defiance — with Lord encompasses people, vision and cursed tree as test, and warning increases greatly in defiance, shows that Lord encompasses, surrounds, tests via vision and cursed tree, warns, but disbelievers increase in great defiance, as barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray.

**How This Verse Teaches Lord Encompasses And Test And Warning Increases Defiance**

This verse teaches that Lord encompasses people, surrounds, knows, controls, protects, so Prophet be steadfast, not fear, as Lord encompasses, and that vision, Night Journey, what Prophet was brought to see, and cursed tree mentioned in Qur'an, Zaqqūm, are test for people, trial, to test belief, disbelief, and that We keep warning them, with signs, warnings, but it only increases them greatly in defiance, transgression, great defiance, as they have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, denying resurrection, etc., and varying signs drives farther away, nufūran, aversion, and warning increases defiance, ṭughyānan kabīran. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know Lord encompasses people, vision and cursed tree test, warning increases defiance for disbelievers, so believe, not increase in defiance, be mindful, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving.

**A Reflection On Encompassing And Test And Great Defiance**

Inna rabbaka aḥāṭa bi-l-nāsi — indeed your Lord encompasses people — comforting to Prophet, Lord encompasses, surrounds, knows, controls, protects, so be steadfast, not fear disbelievers, as He encompasses. Al-ru'yā allatī araynāka illā fitnatan li-l-nāsi — vision which We showed you except as test for people — Night Journey vision, what Prophet saw, signs, as test, trial, who believes, who disbelieves, as some believed, some mocked, increased defiance. Wa-al-shajarata al-malʿūnata fī al-Qur'āni — and tree cursed in Qur'an — Zaqqūm, cursed tree, mentioned in Qur'an, as test, as disbelievers said how tree in Hell, etc., test. Wa-nukhawwifuhum fa-mā yazīduhum illā ṭughyānan kabīran — We warn them but increases not except great defiance — warning, signs, only increase disbelievers greatly in defiance, transgression, great defiance, as they have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray, denying resurrection, etc., varying signs drives farther away, warning increases defiance. The believer who reflects will know Lord encompasses, vision and cursed tree test, warning increases defiance for disbelievers, so believe, not increase in defiance, be mindful, follow most upright, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding great defiance, painful punishment.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:61

> **And ˹remember˺ when We said to the angels, “Prostrate before Adam,” so they all did—but not Iblîs, who protested, “Should I prostrate to the one You have created from mud?”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**When We Said To Angels Prostrate Before Adam**

Wa-idh qulnā li-l-malā'ikati usjudū li-Ādama fa-sajadū illā Iblīsa — and when We said to angels prostrate to Adam, so they prostrated except Iblis. Wa-idh, and when, remember when. Qulnā li-l-malā'ikati, We said to angels, from q-w-l and m-l-k, angels. Usjudū from s-j-d, prostrate, bow down, prostrate, from sajada, to prostrate, as masjid place of prostration, Temple. Li-Ādama, to Adam, before Adam, for Adam, first human, father. Fa-sajadū, so they prostrated, did prostrate, from s-j-d. Illā Iblīsa, except Iblis, Satan, Devil, from b-l-s, Iblis, despair, as Iblis despaired of mercy. Al-Ṭabarī explains that remember when We said to angels, prostrate before Adam, as honour, respect, not worship, prostration of honour, so they all did, prostrated, obeyed, except Iblis, who refused, protested, as he was arrogant, as previous verse 37 do not walk arrogantly, and as those who disbelieve have denying hearts and are arrogant, as Devil arrogant. Al-Qurṭubī says usjudū li-Ādama means prostrate before Adam, as honour, respect, as angels prostrated honouring Adam, as Adam favoured, dignified, as children of Adam dignified, as verse 70 says.

**Iblis Protested Should I Prostrate To One Created From Mud**

Qāla a-asjudu li-man khalaqta ṭīnan — he said, should I prostrate to one You created from mud, clay? Qāla from q-w-l, he said, Iblis said, protested. A-asjudu from s-j-d, should I prostrate, interrogative, should I prostrate? Li-man, to one whom, to whom. Khalaqta from kh-l-q, You created, from khalaqa, to create. Ṭīnan from ṭ-y-n, mud, clay, from ṭīn, mud, clay, as Adam created from mud, clay, as previous chapter said created from mud. Al-Ṭabarī explains that Iblis protested, said should I prostrate to one You created from mud, clay, while I created from fire, as he thought fire better than mud, arrogant, as he was arrogant, refused to prostrate, as Devil ever ungrateful, as wasteful brothers to devils, as Satan sworn enemy, as he refused out of arrogance, as previous verse do not walk arrogantly, you cannot crack earth nor reach mountains height, but Iblis arrogant, refused. Al-Qurṭubī says a-asjudu li-man khalaqta ṭīnan indicates arrogance, Iblis thought himself better, created from fire, Adam from mud, so refused, protested, as arrogance leads to disobedience, as Children of Isra'il became extremely arrogant, etc.

**Why Angels Prostrated And Iblis Refused**

Why angels prostrated and Iblis refused? Because angels obey, not arrogant, while Iblis arrogant, ever ungrateful, sworn enemy, refused to prostrate to one created from mud, thinking fire better, as arrogance, as he said do you see this one you honoured above me, if delay my end until Day Judgment I will take hold of descendants except few, as next verse says, vowing to mislead, as Satan seeks to sow discord, incite, etc. The prostration to Adam is honour for Adam, for children of Adam dignified, as verse 70 says We dignified children of Adam, carried them on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, so Adam dignified, honoured, angels prostrated, Iblis refused out of arrogance.

**Connection To Arrogance And Walking Arrogantly And Extremely Arrogant**

Verse 4 Children of Isra'il will become extremely arrogant, ʿuluwwan kabīran, great arrogance, verse 37 do not walk arrogantly, maraḥan, arrogantly, you cannot crack earth nor reach mountains height, verse 61 Iblis refused to prostrate to one created from mud, arrogant, protested, as arrogance leads to refusal, disobedience, as Devil arrogant, as those who disbelieve in Hereafter have denying hearts and are arrogant. The connection is arrogance theme: Children of Isra'il extremely arrogant, do not walk arrogantly, Iblis arrogant refused, all arrogance leads to disobedience, corruption, destruction, severe punishment, as divine law, every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record.

**Link To Dignified Children Of Adam And Satan No Authority Over Faithful**

Verse 70 We dignified children of Adam, carried them on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, showing honour for Adam, children, as angels prostrated, Iblis refused out of arrogance, but children of Adam dignified, honoured. Verse 65 you will have no authority over My faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, showing that despite Iblis vow to take hold of descendants except few, he has no authority over faithful servants, Lord Guardian sufficient, as faithful servants protected, as they say best, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Usjudu And Iblis And Tin**

Qulnā from q-w-l, to say. Malā'ikah from m-l-k, angels, plural of malak. Usjudū from s-j-d, prostrate, from sajada. Ādam, Adam, first human, from a-d-m, Adam. Sajadū from s-j-d, they prostrated. Iblīs, Iblis, Satan, from b-l-s, to despair. Qāla from q-w-l, to say. Asjudu from s-j-d, prostrate. Man khalaqta, whom You created, from kh-l-q. Ṭīn from ṭ-y-n, mud, clay, from ṭīn. The verse's structure — when We said to angels prostrate before Adam, so they prostrated except Iblis, who said should I prostrate to one You created from mud — with command to angels prostrate, obedience except Iblis, and protest should I prostrate to one created from mud, shows honour for Adam, obedience of angels, arrogance of Iblis, refusal, protest, as arrogance leads to disobedience, as theme.

**How This Verse Teaches Obedience Vs Arrogance**

This verse teaches obedience vs arrogance: angels obeyed, prostrated before Adam when commanded, Iblis refused out of arrogance, protested should I prostrate to one created from mud, thinking fire better, arrogant, as Devil ever ungrateful, sworn enemy, seeks to sow discord, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should obey, not be arrogant like Iblis, should be humble, not walk arrogantly, lower wing of humility, as most upright guidance, wisdom, part of wisdom revealed, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, while arrogance leads to refusal, disobedience, being cast into Hell blameworthy rejected, as Iblis, as those who set up other god cast into Hell, etc. The practical lesson is to obey Allah, prostrate, humble, not arrogant, not protest should I prostrate to mud, but obey, as angels obeyed, as children of Adam dignified, honoured, privileged far above many creatures.

**A Reflection On Mud And Honour**

Ṭīnan — mud, clay — material from which Adam created, humble, lowly, from earth, as earth, but Adam honoured, dignified, angels prostrated, children of Adam dignified, carried on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, showing that honour not from material, fire vs mud, but from Allah's favour, as He favoured some over others, some prophets above others, etc., and Iblis arrogant thought fire better than mud, refused, protested, but was wrong, arrogant, ever ungrateful, sworn enemy, vowed to take hold of descendants, but no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful servants protected, say best, etc., leading to mighty reward.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:62

> **Adding, “Do you see this one you honoured above me? If you delay my end until the Day of Judgment, I will certainly take hold of his descendants, except for a few.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Do You See This One You Honoured Above Me**

Qāla a-ra'ayta hādhā alladhī karramta ʿalayya — he said, do you see this one you honoured above me, you favoured above me? Qāla from q-w-l, he said, Iblis adding, continuing protest. A-ra'ayta from r-ʾ-y, to see, do you see, have you seen, as ra'ayta you see. Hādhā, this one, this, Adam, this one, pointing. Alladhī karramta, whom You honoured, favoured, from k-r-m, to honour, favour, make noble, from karuma, to be noble, from karramta You honoured, as karramnā We dignified children of Adam, dignified, honoured. ʿAlayya, above me, over me, above me, over me, meaning honoured above me, favoured above me, as Iblis thought himself better, honoured above Adam? Actually Allah honoured Adam above Iblis, so Iblis says do you see this one you honoured above me, questioning, protesting. Al-Ṭabarī explains that Iblis said, do you see this one, Adam, you honoured above me, favoured above me, made noble above me, while I am better, created from fire, etc., protesting, arrogant, as he refused to prostrate to one created from mud, thinking fire better, arrogant.

**If You Delay My End Until Day Of Judgment I Will Take Hold Of Descendants Except Few**

La-in akhkhartanī ilā yawmi al-qiyāmati la-aḥtanikanna dhurriyyatahu illā qalīlan — if You delay me until Day of Resurrection, I will certainly take hold of his descendants, except few. La-in from l- and in, if, indeed if, conditional with emphasis, if You delay. Akhkhartanī from a-kh-r, to delay, postpone, give respite, from akhkharat, to delay, postpone, as delay my end, give me respite until Day. Ilā yawmi al-qiyāmati, until Day of Resurrection, Day of Standing, from y-w-m and q-w-m. La-aḥtanikanna from ḥ-n-k, to take hold of, to control, to put bridle, to dominate, to take hold, from iḥtanaka, to take hold, control, dominate, put bridle, as aḥtanaka to take hold, control, dominate, as bridle in mouth, to control, as Iblis vows to take hold of descendants, control, dominate, mislead, as Satan seeks to sow discord, incite, etc. Dhurriyyatahu from dh-r-r, descendants, offspring, progeny, from dhurriyyah, descendants, offspring, progeny, as children of Adam, descendants. Illā qalīlan, except few, small number, from q-l-l, few, little, as qalīlan few, except few, meaning except few faithful, as faithful servants no authority for Satan over them, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as verse 65 says, except few faithful, awwābīn, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that Iblis said, if You delay me, give me respite until Day of Judgment, I will certainly take hold of his descendants, control, dominate, mislead, except for few, small number, meaning except few faithful, sincere, who will not follow, as Satan has no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian.

**Why Iblis Vows To Take Hold Of Descendants**

Why Iblis vows to take hold of descendants except few? Because arrogant, ever ungrateful, sworn enemy, refused to prostrate to Adam created from mud, protested, thought himself better, honoured above? Actually Adam honoured above him, so he envied, vowed to take revenge by misleading descendants, controlling, dominating, except few faithful, as Satan seeks to sow discord, incite with voice, cavalry, infantry, manipulate wealth and children, promise delusion, as verses 64-65 will say, but no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected.

**Connection To No Authority Over Faithful Servants And Sufficient Guardian And Incite With Voice Cavalry Infantry**

Verse 62 Iblis vows if delay until Day Judgment will take hold of descendants except few. Verse 63 Allah responded be gone, whoever follows you Hell ample reward. Verse 64 incite whoever you can with voice, mobilize cavalry and infantry, manipulate wealth and children, make promises, but Satan promises nothing but delusion. Verse 65 you will have no authority over My faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, showing that despite vow to take hold of descendants except few, Satan has no authority over faithful servants, Lord Guardian sufficient, as faithful servants protected, say best, etc., and incitement with voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion, but no authority over faithful.

**Link To Children Of Adam Dignified And Carried On Land And Sea**

Verse 70 We dignified children of Adam, carried them on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, showing honour for Adam, descendants, despite Iblis vow to take hold, control, dominate, except few, but children of Adam dignified, honoured, privileged, and faithful among them no authority for Satan, Lord Guardian sufficient, as faithful protected.

**Linguistic Note On Karramta And Akhkhartani And Ahtanikanna And Dhurriyyah And Qalilan**

Qāla from q-w-l, to say. Ra'ayta from r-ʾ-y, to see. Hādhā, this. Alladhī karramta, whom You honoured, from k-r-m, to honour, make noble, from karuma. ʿAlayya, above me, over me. Akhkhartanī from a-kh-r, to delay, postpone, from akhkharat. Yawm from y-w-m, Day. Qiyāmah from q-w-m, Resurrection. Aḥtanikanna from ḥ-n-k, to take hold, control, dominate, put bridle, from iḥtanaka, intensive with nun emphasis, certainly take hold. Dhurriyyah from dh-r-r, descendants, offspring, from dharra, to scatter, progeny. Qalīl from q-l-l, few, little. The verse's structure — do you see this one you honoured above me, if You delay me until Day of Resurrection I will certainly take hold of his descendants except few — with questioning do you see this one honoured above me and vow if delay until Day will take hold of descendants except few, shows arrogance, envy, vow to mislead, control, dominate, except few faithful, as Satan's vow, as sworn enemy.

**How This Verse Teaches Satan's Vow And Envy**

This verse teaches Satan's, Iblis's vow and envy: do you see this one you honoured above me, Adam honoured above me, while I better, created from fire, etc., if You delay me, give respite until Day of Judgment, I will certainly take hold of his descendants, control, dominate, mislead, put bridle, except for few, small number, faithful, as Satan seeks to sow discord, incite, etc., but no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know Iblis vowed to take hold of descendants except few, out of arrogance, envy, as sworn enemy, clear enemy, seeks to mislead, control, dominate, but faithful servants no authority for Satan over them, Lord Guardian sufficient, so be faithful servant, say best, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding being among those taken hold of by Satan, except few faithful protected.

**A Reflection On Taking Hold And Few Faithful**

La-aḥtanikanna dhurriyyatahu illā qalīlan — I will certainly take hold of his descendants except few — indicates Satan's plan to control, dominate, mislead descendants, put bridle, as bridle controls horse, as Satan controls via voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion, but except few, qalīlan, few, faithful, sincere, awwābīn, constantly turning, righteous, etc., who will not follow, as faithful servants no authority for Satan, Lord Guardian sufficient. The believer who reflects will be among few, qalīlan, faithful, not among many taken hold of, will be faithful servant, say best, follow most upright, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding Hell ample reward for followers of Satan, as verse 63 says whoever follows you Hell ample reward.
