## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:63

> **Allah responded, “Be gone! Whoever of them follows you, Hell will surely be the reward for all of you—an ample reward.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Be Gone Whoever Follows You Hell Reward Ample**

Qāla idhhab fa-man tabiʿaka minhum fa-inna jahannama jazā'ukum jazā'an mawfūran — He said, go, be gone, then whoever follows you from them, then indeed Hell is your reward, reward ample. Qāla from q-w-l, He said, Allah said, responded to Iblis vow. Idhhab from dh-h-b, to go, be gone, go away, from dhahaba, to go, imperative, be gone, go. Fa-man tabiʿaka from t-b-ʿ, to follow, whoever follows you, from tabiʿa, to follow, as tabiʿaka follows you. Minhum, from them, from descendants, children of Adam. Fa-inna jahannama, then indeed Hell, Gehenna, Hell, from j-h-n-m. Jazā'ukum from j-z-y, reward, recompense, from jazā', reward, recompense, as reward for following. Jazā'an mawfūran from w-f-r, ample, abundant, complete, from wafura, to be ample, abundant, complete, from mawfūr, ample, abundant, complete, as ample reward, abundant reward, complete recompense, as Hell ample reward for followers of Satan, as punishment ample, abundant, complete. Al-Ṭabarī explains that Allah responded to Iblis vow to take hold of descendants except few, saying be gone, go, whoever of them, descendants, follows you, follows Satan, Iblis, then indeed Hell will be reward for all of you, for you and followers, ample reward, abundant, complete recompense, as punishment, as Hell, as ample reward, as recompense for following Satan, as Satan promises delusion, but reward Hell ample.

**Why Be Gone And Hell Ample Reward**

Why be gone and Hell ample reward? Because Iblis arrogant, refused to prostrate to Adam created from mud, protested should I prostrate to mud, said do you see this one you honoured above me, if delay until Day Judgment will take hold of descendants except few, vowing to mislead, control, dominate, out of envy, arrogance, as sworn enemy, clear enemy, ever ungrateful, wasteful brothers to devils, seeks to sow discord, etc., so Allah responded be gone, go away, whoever follows you from descendants, Hell will be reward for all of you, you and followers, ample reward, abundant, complete, as punishment, as Hell, as ample recompense, as justice, as Lord knows best, may have mercy if wills or punish if wills, as will, wisdom, as faithful servants no authority for Satan over them, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected, but followers of Satan Hell ample reward.

**Connection To No Authority Over Faithful And Incite With Voice Cavalry Infantry**

Verse 62 Iblis vow to take hold of descendants except few if delayed until Day. Verse 63 Allah responded be gone, whoever follows you Hell ample reward. Verse 64 incite whoever you can with voice, mobilize cavalry and infantry, manipulate wealth and children, make promises, but Satan promises nothing but delusion. Verse 65 you will have no authority over My faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, showing that despite vow, Allah says be gone, followers Hell ample reward, but faithful servants no authority, Lord Guardian sufficient, and Satan can incite with voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion, but no authority over faithful, as faithful protected, say best, etc.

**Link To Hell For Fleeting Desirer Condemned Rejected And Blameworthy Rejected**

Verse 18 fleeting desirer hastened worldly then Hell condemned rejected, madhmūman madḥūran, condemned rejected. Verse 39 do not set up other god or cast into Hell blameworthy rejected, malūman madḥūran, blameworthy rejected. Verse 63 whoever follows you Hell ample reward, jazā'an mawfūran, ample reward, showing Hell as reward for followers of Satan, fleeting desirers, polytheists, etc., ample, abundant, complete, as recompense, as justice, as Lord knows best, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Idhhab And Tabi'aka And Jazā' And Mawfuran**

Qāla from q-w-l, to say. Idhhab from dh-h-b, to go, be gone, from dhahaba. Man tabiʿa from t-b-ʿ, to follow, whoever follows. Minhum, from them. Jahannam from j-h-n-m, Hell. Jazā' from j-z-y, reward, recompense, from jazā. Mawfūr from w-f-r, ample, abundant, complete, from wafura, to be ample. The verse's structure — He said be gone, then whoever follows you from them, then indeed Hell is your reward, reward ample — with command be gone and condition whoever follows you and result Hell reward ample, shows dismissal of Iblis, be gone, and warning that followers Hell ample reward, abundant, complete, as punishment, as justice, as Lord knows best, may have mercy or punish, as will.

**How This Verse Teaches Dismissal And Hell For Followers**

This verse teaches dismissal of Satan and Hell ample reward for followers: Allah responded to Iblis vow, be gone, go away, whoever of them, descendants, follows you, follows Satan, then indeed Hell will be reward for all of you, you and followers, ample reward, abundant, complete recompense, as punishment, as Hell, as ample, as justice, as Lord knows best, may have mercy if wills or punish if wills, as will, wisdom, as faithful servants no authority for Satan over them, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected, but followers Hell ample reward. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should not follow Satan, Iblis, who vowed to take hold of descendants except few, who incites with voice, cavalry, infantry, manipulates wealth and children, promises delusion, but promises nothing but delusion, and followers Hell ample reward, abundant, complete, as punishment, as Hell, as ample recompense, so be faithful servant, no authority for Satan, Lord Guardian sufficient, say best, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding Hell ample reward.

**A Reflection On Ample Reward Hell**

Jazā'an mawfūran — reward ample, abundant, complete — indicates Hell as ample reward, abundant, complete recompense for followers of Satan, as punishment ample, abundant, complete, not small, but ample, as justice, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and Hell ample reward for followers, as ample, abundant, complete, as recompense for following Satan, who promises delusion, but promises nothing but delusion, and incites with voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises, but no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected, say best, etc., leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, while followers Hell ample reward, abundant, complete, as punishment, as Hell, as ample, as justice.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:64

> **And incite whoever you can of them with your voice, mobilize against them all your cavalry and infantry, manipulate them in their wealth and children, and make them promises.” But Satan promises them nothing but delusion.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Incite Whoever You Can With Voice, Mobilize Cavalry Infantry, Manipulate Wealth Children, Make Promises**

Wa-istafziz man istaṭaʿta minhum bi-ṣawtika wa-ajlib ʿalayhim bi-khaylika wa-rajilika wa-shārikhum fī al-amwāli wa-al-awlādi wa-ʿidhum — and incite, entice whoever you can of them with your voice and mobilize against them with your cavalry and infantry and share with them in wealth and children and promise them. Wa-istafziz from f-z-z, to incite, entice, provoke, make to slip, from istafazza, to incite, entice, provoke, to make to tremble, to incite. Man istaṭaʿta, whoever you can, whoever you are able, from ṭ-w-ʿ, to be able, can, as istaṭaʿta you can, you are able. Minhum, from them, from descendants. Bi-ṣawtika from ṣ-w-t, with your voice, voice, from ṣawt, voice, as Satan's voice, incitement, as Satan seeks to sow discord, incites, etc. Wa-ajlib from j-l-b, to mobilize, bring, gather, from ajlaba, to mobilize, bring, gather, as mobilize against them. ʿAlayhim, against them. Bi-khaylika from kh-y-l, with your cavalry, horses, from khayl, cavalry, horses, plural of khayl, horses, cavalry. Wa-rajilika from r-j-l, and your infantry, foot soldiers, from rajil, infantry, foot, from rajila, foot. Wa-shārikhum from sh-r-k, to share, associate, manipulate, from shāraka, to share, associate, as share with them, manipulate, associate in wealth and children, as Satan shares, manipulates wealth and children, as via unlawful wealth, children, etc. Fī al-amwāli wa-al-awlādi, in wealth and children, in wealth and offspring, as amwāl wealth, awlād children, as wealth and children adornment, but Satan manipulates. Wa-ʿidhum from w-ʿ-d, to promise, make promises, from waʿada, to promise, as promise them, make promises. Al-Ṭabarī explains that Allah says to Satan, incite, entice whoever you can of them, descendants, with your voice, with incitement, deception, and mobilize against them with your cavalry and infantry, all your forces, horses and foot, and share with them, manipulate them in wealth and children, via unlawful wealth, unlawful children, etc., and make them promises, promise them, delude them, as Satan promises delusion, but promises nothing but delusion.

**But Satan Promises Nothing But Delusion**

Wa-mā yaʿiduhum al-shayṭānu illā ghurūran — and Satan promises them not except delusion, deception. Wa-mā, and not, but. Yaʿiduhum from w-ʿ-d, to promise, as ʿidhum promise them. Al-shayṭānu, Satan, Devil, Iblis. Illā ghurūran from gh-r-r, delusion, deception, vanity, from gharra, to deceive, delude, from ghurūr, delusion, deception, vanity, as delusion, deception, false promise. Al-Ṭabarī explains that Satan promises them nothing but delusion, deception, vanity, false promises, as he promises wealth, children, etc., but promises delusion, deception, not truth, as he has no power to fulfill, as he has no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected, but promises delusion to followers, as Hell ample reward.

**Why Incite With Voice Cavalry Infantry Wealth Children Promises Delusion**

Why incite with voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion? Because Satan, Iblis, vowed to take hold of descendants except few, if delayed until Day, and Allah said be gone, whoever follows you Hell ample reward, and now Allah says incite whoever you can with voice, mobilize cavalry and infantry, manipulate wealth and children, make promises, but promises nothing but delusion, showing Satan's methods: voice, incitement, deception, cavalry, infantry, forces, wealth, children, promises, all delusion, deception, not truth, as he has no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected, say best, etc., but followers deluded, Hell ample reward.

**Connection To No Authority Over Faithful And Sufficient Guardian And Voice And Wealth Children**

Verse 62 vow to take hold of descendants except few, verse 63 be gone, followers Hell ample reward, verse 64 incite whoever you can with voice, cavalry, infantry, manipulate wealth and children, make promises, but promises nothing but delusion, verse 65 you will have no authority over My faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, showing Satan's methods, voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion, but no authority over faithful servants, Lord Guardian sufficient, as faithful protected, say best, etc., and incitement is delusion, not truth.

**Link To Wasteful Brothers To Devils And Satan Sworn Enemy And Seeks Discord**

Verse 27 wasteful brothers to devils, Devil ever ungrateful, verse 53 Satan seeks to sow discord among them, Satan sworn enemy to humankind, clear enemy, verse 64 incite with voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion, showing Satan's methods to sow discord, incite, as sworn enemy, clear enemy, ever ungrateful, wasteful brothers to devils follow him, but faithful servants no authority, Lord Guardian sufficient, say best to prevent discord.

**Linguistic Note On Istafziz And Sawt And Ajlib And Khayl And Rajil And Sharik And Ghurur**

Istafziz from f-z-z, to incite, entice, provoke, from istafazza. Man istaṭaʿta, whoever you can, from ṭ-w-ʿ, to be able. Ṣawt from ṣ-w-t, voice, from ṣawt. Ajlib from j-l-b, to mobilize, bring, gather, from ajlaba. Khayl from kh-y-l, cavalry, horses, from khayl. Rajil from r-j-l, infantry, foot, from rajil. Shārik from sh-r-k, to share, associate, manipulate, from shāraka. Amwāl from m-w-l, wealth, plural of māl. Awlād from w-l-d, children, plural of walad. ʿIdh from w-ʿ-d, to promise, from waʿada. Yaʿidu from w-ʿ-d, to promise. Ghurūr from gh-r-r, delusion, deception, vanity, from gharra, to deceive. The verse's structure — incite whoever you can with voice and mobilize against them with cavalry and infantry and share with them in wealth and children and promise them, but Satan promises them nothing but delusion — with methods voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises, and statement promises nothing but delusion, shows Satan's methods are delusion, deception, not truth, no authority over faithful, Lord Guardian sufficient.

**How This Verse Teaches Satan's Methods Are Delusion**

This verse teaches Satan's methods are delusion, deception, not truth: incite whoever you can with voice, with incitement, deception, mobilize against them with cavalry and infantry, all forces, share, manipulate them in wealth and children, via unlawful wealth, unlawful children, etc., and make promises, promise them, but Satan promises them nothing but delusion, deception, vanity, false promises, not truth, as he has no power, no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected, but followers deluded, Hell ample reward, ample. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know Satan's methods, voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion, and should be faithful servant, no authority for Satan, Lord Guardian sufficient, say best, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding delusion, Hell ample reward.

**A Reflection On Voice Cavalry Infantry Wealth Children Delusion**

Bi-ṣawtika wa-khaylika wa-rajilika — with your voice and cavalry and infantry — indicates Satan's forces, voice, incitement, deception, cavalry, horses, infantry, foot, all forces, mobilized against descendants, to mislead, control, dominate, except few faithful, as vow to take hold, but no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, as faithful protected, say best, etc. Wa-shārikhum fī al-amwāli wa-al-awlādi — share with them in wealth and children, manipulate wealth and children — indicates Satan manipulates wealth and children, via unlawful wealth, usury, theft, etc., and unlawful children, adultery, etc., as wealth and children adornment but manipulated by Satan leads to evil, as previous verses about wealth, children, etc. Wa-ʿidhum — promise them — but promises nothing but delusion, ghurūran, delusion, deception, vanity, false, not truth, as Satan's promises delusion, as he promised delusion to followers, but Hell ample reward, ample, as recompense, as justice. The believer who reflects will not be incited by voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion, but be faithful servant, no authority for Satan, Lord Guardian sufficient, say best, follow most upright, wisdom, leading to mighty reward.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:65

> **˹Allah added,˺ “You will truly have no authority over My ˹faithful˺ servants.” And sufficient is your Lord as a Guardian.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**You Will Have No Authority Over My Faithful Servants**

Inna ʿibādī laysa laka ʿalayhim sulṭānun — indeed My servants, faithful servants, not for you over them authority, power. Inna, indeed, surely. ʿIbādī, My servants, My faithful servants, servants who are faithful, sincere, from ʿ-b-d, servants, as ʿibād, servants, as ʿibādī My servants, faithful, as in verse 53 My believing servants say best, etc., and verse 65 faithful servants. Laysa laka, not for you, not for you, Satan, Iblis. ʿAlayhim, over them, upon them, over My faithful servants. Sulṭānun from s-l-ṭ, authority, power, control, proof, from sulṭān, authority, power, as previous sulṭānan authority given to heir of unjustly killed, etc., and sulṭān authority, power, as Satan has no authority over faithful servants. Al-Ṭabarī explains that you, Satan, Iblis, will have no authority, power, control over My faithful servants, sincere servants, who are faithful, who say best, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, etc., as they are protected, as Lord Guardian sufficient, as they are faithful, not among those taken hold of except few, as few faithful, qalīlan, except few, as faithful servants protected. Al-Qurṭubī says ʿibādī here means faithful servants, sincere, believers, who are faithful, not all servants, but faithful, sincere, who have no authority for Satan over them.

**Sufficient Lord As Guardian**

Wa-kafā bi-rabbika wakīlan — and sufficient is your Lord as Guardian, Keeper, Trustee. Wa-kafā from k-f-y, to suffice, be enough, as kafā sufficient, as verse 14 kafā bi-nafsika sufficient yourself as reckoner, and verse 17 kafā bi-rabbika sufficient your Lord as All-Aware All-Seeing, and verse 65 sufficient Lord as Guardian. Bi-rabbika, with your Lord, by your Lord, your Lord, from r-b-b. Wakīlan from w-k-l, Guardian, Keeper, Trustee, Disposer, as wakīl, keeper, guardian, trustee, as verse 2 Trustee of Affairs, and verse 54 not sent you as keeper, wakīlan, over them, and verse 65 sufficient Lord as Guardian, wakīlan. Al-Ṭabarī explains that sufficient is your Lord as Guardian, Keeper, Trustee, Protector, for faithful servants, meaning Lord is enough as Guardian, Keeper, to protect faithful servants from Satan, Iblis, who has no authority over them, as Lord Guardian sufficient, protects, as He encompasses people, knows best, etc. Al-Rāzī says kafā bi-rabbika wakīlan indicates sufficiency of Lord as Guardian, Keeper, for faithful servants, no need for other guardian, as Lord sufficient.

**Why No Authority Over Faithful And Sufficient Guardian**

Why no authority over faithful servants and sufficient Lord as Guardian? Because Iblis vowed to take hold of descendants except few, if delayed until Day, Allah said be gone, followers Hell ample reward, incite whoever you can with voice, cavalry, infantry, manipulate wealth and children, make promises, but promises nothing but delusion, and now you will have no authority over My faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, showing that despite vow and methods, voice, cavalry, infantry, wealth, children, promises delusion, Satan has no authority, power, control over faithful servants, sincere, who say best, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, etc., as Lord Guardian sufficient, protects, as He encompasses people, knows best, etc., and faithful servants are few, qalīlan, except few, but protected, as Lord Guardian sufficient.

**Connection To Vow And Be Gone And Incite And Delusion**

Verse 62 vow to take hold of descendants except few, verse 63 be gone, followers Hell ample reward, verse 64 incite whoever you can with voice, cavalry, infantry, manipulate wealth and children, make promises, but promises nothing but delusion, verse 65 you will have no authority over My faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, showing sequence: vow, dismissal, methods delusion, no authority over faithful, sufficient Guardian, as faithful protected, as few, but sufficient Guardian.

**Link To Wasteful Brothers To Devils And Satan Sworn Enemy And Seeks Discord**

Verse 27 wasteful brothers to devils, Devil ever ungrateful, verse 53 Satan seeks to sow discord among them, Satan sworn enemy to humankind, clear enemy, verse 65 no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, showing that wasteful brothers to devils follow Satan, but faithful servants who say best, most excellent, have no authority for Satan over them, Lord Guardian sufficient, as faithful protected, say best to prevent discord, as Satan sworn enemy, clear enemy, seeks discord, but no authority over faithful.

**Linguistic Note On Ibadī And Sultan And Kafa And Wakilan**

ʿIbād from ʿ-b-d, servants, faithful servants, from ʿabada, to worship, serve. Laysa, not, is not. Laka, for you, to you. ʿAlayhim, over them, upon them. Sulṭān from s-l-ṭ, authority, power, control, from sulṭān. Kafā from k-f-y, to suffice, be enough, from kafā. Rabb from r-b-b, Lord. Wakīl from w-k-l, Guardian, Keeper, Trustee, Disposer, from wakala, to entrust. The verse's structure — indeed My servants not for you over them authority, and sufficient is your Lord as Guardian — with statement no authority over faithful servants and sufficiency of Lord as Guardian, shows protection of faithful servants from Satan, no authority, Lord sufficient as Guardian, Keeper, Protector.

**How This Verse Teaches Protection Of Faithful Servants**

This verse teaches protection of faithful servants: you, Satan, Iblis, will have no authority, power, control over My faithful servants, sincere servants, who say best, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, etc., and sufficient is your Lord as Guardian, Keeper, Trustee, Protector, for faithful servants, meaning Lord enough as Guardian to protect from Satan, who has no authority over them, as Lord encompasses people, knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and faithful servants protected, as few, qalīlan, except few, but sufficient Guardian, as Lord sufficient. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should be faithful servant, sincere, say best, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, so that Satan has no authority over him, Lord Guardian sufficient, protects, as faithful protected, as few, but sufficient, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding Hell ample reward for followers of Satan.

**A Reflection On No Authority And Sufficient Guardian**

Laysa laka ʿalayhim sulṭānun — not for you over them authority — indicates Satan, Iblis, has no authority, power, control, proof over faithful servants, sincere, faithful, who are My servants, as they are protected, as they say best, follow most upright, wisdom, etc., as they are few, qalīlan, except few, but protected. Wa-kafā bi-rabbika wakīlan — sufficient is your Lord as Guardian — indicates sufficiency of Lord as Guardian, Keeper, Trustee, Protector, for faithful servants, no need for other guardian, as Lord sufficient, encompasses people, knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and faithful servants protected, as Lord Guardian sufficient, as He steers ships through sea so you may seek bounty, ever Merciful, as next verse says, etc., leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, while followers of Satan Hell ample reward, ample, as recompense, as justice, as Lord knows best, may have mercy if wills or punish if wills, as will, wisdom.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:66

> **It is your Lord Who steers the ships for you through the sea, so that you may seek His bounty. Surely He is ever Merciful to you.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Lord Steers Ships Through Sea So You May Seek Bounty**

Rabbukumu alladhī yuzjī lakum al-fulka fī al-baḥri li-tabtaghū min faḍlihi — your Lord is Who drives, steers for you ships in sea so you may seek from His bounty. Rabbukumu, your Lord, your Lord, from r-b-b, Lord, as rabbukum your Lord. Alladhī yuzjī from z-j-y, to drive, steer, push gently, from azjā, to drive, steer, as yuzjī drives, steers ships. Lakum, for you, for your benefit. Al-fulka from f-l-k, ships, ship, from fulk, ship, ships, as fulk ships, as ark, as progeny of those carried with Noah in Ark, fulk, as ships. Fī al-baḥri from b-ḥ-r, in sea, sea, ocean, from baḥr, sea. Li-tabtaghū from b-gh-y, to seek, desire, so you may seek, as tabtaghū seek bounty, as verse 12 li-tabtaghū faḍlan, so you may seek bounty, and verse 20 bounty, etc. Min faḍlihi, from His bounty, from His favour, bounty, grace, from f-ḍ-l, bounty, favour, as faḍl bounty, as faḍlihi His bounty. Al-Ṭabarī explains that your Lord is Who steers, drives ships for you through sea, so that you may seek His bounty, provision, via trade, travel, etc., as ships steered through sea by Lord, as He subjected sea, etc., as He is ever Merciful, as He provides, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says yuzjī lakum al-fulka fī al-baḥri means drives, steers ships for you in sea, so you may seek bounty, provision, via sea travel, trade, etc., as sea subjected for seeking bounty, as previous chapter said He subjected for your benefit night and day, sun and moon, etc., and sea, etc.

**He Is Ever Merciful To You**

Innahu kāna bikum raḥīman — indeed He is with you, to you, ever Merciful. Innahu, indeed He, Allah, your Lord. Kāna, is, was, indicates He is. Bikum, with you, to you, about you, you all. Raḥīman from r-ḥ-m, Merciful, ever Merciful, from raḥima, to have mercy, from raḥmah, mercy, as Raḥīm Merciful, ever Merciful, as He is ever Merciful to you, as He steers ships for you to seek bounty, as mercy, as He is Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but also Merciful, as He provides, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that He is ever Merciful to you, meaning Lord ever Merciful to you, humans, as He steers ships for you to seek bounty, as mercy, as He is Merciful, not hastening punishment, as Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, but Merciful to you, as He provides, etc.

**Why Ships Through Sea To Seek Bounty And Merciful**

Why ships through sea to seek bounty and ever Merciful? Because Lord gives abundant or limited provisions, All-Aware All-Seeing, bounty never withheld, some favoured over others, provision, etc., and ships steered through sea so you may seek bounty, provision, via trade, travel, as sea subjected, as He dignified children of Adam, carried them on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, as verse 70 says, and ships are means to seek bounty, as mercy, ever Merciful to you, as He provides, etc., as He is Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but also Merciful, ever Merciful, as He steers ships for you, etc.

**Connection To Dignified Children Of Adam Carried On Land And Sea And Bounty**

Verse 66 Lord steers ships for you through sea so you may seek bounty, ever Merciful to you. Verse 70 We dignified children of Adam, carried them on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, showing that carrying on land and sea, ships, is part of dignifying children of Adam, honouring, as Lord steers ships for you to seek bounty, as mercy, ever Merciful, as He dignified, carried, granted provisions, privileged. The connection is ships through sea to seek bounty is part of dignifying, carrying, provisioning, privileging children of Adam, as mercy, ever Merciful.

Verse 20 bounty never withheld, We provide both former and latter from bounty. Verse 30 Lord gives abundant or limited provisions, All-Aware All-Seeing. **Linguistic Note On Yuzji And Fulk And Bahr And Tabtaghu And Fadl And Rahiman**

Rabb from r-b-b, Lord. Alladhī yuzjī from z-j-y, to drive, steer, push gently, from azjā. Lakum, for you. Fulk from f-l-k, ships, ship, from fulk. Baḥr from b-ḥ-r, sea, from baḥr. Tabtaghū from b-gh-y, to seek, desire, from ibtaghā. Faḍl from f-ḍ-l, bounty, favour, grace, from faḍl. Raḥīm from r-ḥ-m, Merciful, ever Merciful, from raḥima. The verse's structure — your Lord is Who drives for you ships in sea so you may seek from His bounty, indeed He is with you ever Merciful — with Lord drives ships for you in sea to seek bounty and He is ever Merciful to you, shows that ships through sea to seek bounty is from Lord, mercy, ever Merciful, as He dignified children of Adam, carried on land and sea, granted provisions, privileged.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:67

> **When you are touched with hardship at sea, you ˹totally˺ forget all ˹the gods˺ you ˹normally˺ invoke, except Him. But when He delivers you ˹safely˺ to shore, you turn away. Humankind is ever ungrateful.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**When Touched With Hardship At Sea You Forget All Gods Except Him**

Wa-idhā massakumu al-ḍurru fī al-baḥri ḍalla man tadʿūna illā iyyāhu — and when hardship touches you in sea, those you call besides Him go astray, you forget. Wa-idhā, and when. Massakum from m-s-s, to touch, afflict, befall, from massa, to touch, as massakumu touches you, afflicts you. Al-ḍurru from ḍ-r-r, hardship, adversity, harm, affliction, from ḍurr, harm, hardship, as ḍurr hardship at sea, storm, etc. Fī al-baḥri, in sea, at sea, as baḥr sea, as ships through sea. Ḍalla from ḍ-l-l, to go astray, be lost, forget, disappear, from ḍalla, to go astray, be lost, as ḍalla man tadʿūna, those you invoke go astray, you forget, disappear, as you forget all gods you normally invoke. Man tadʿūna from d-ʿ-w, those you call, invoke, you normally invoke, besides Him, as you invoke besides Him, idols, gods, etc., but at sea hardship you forget them. Illā iyyāhu, except Him, only Him, Allah alone, from iyyā, Him, only Him. Al-Ṭabarī explains that when hardship, adversity, harm touches you at sea, storm, drowning, etc., you totally forget all gods you normally invoke besides Him, idols, etc., except Him, Allah alone, meaning you invoke only Him, forget others, as hardship at sea makes you remember only Allah, as only He can remove harm, as those claimed besides Him have no power to remove harm or transfer, as verse 56 says, so at sea you forget them, invoke only Him.

**When Delivers You To Shore You Turn Away, Humankind Ever Ungrateful**

Fa-lammā najjākum ilā al-barri aʿraḍtum wa-kāna al-insānu kafūran — then when He delivers you to land, shore, you turn away, and human is ever ungrateful. Fa-lammā, then when, so when. Najjākum from n-j-w, to deliver, save, rescue, from najjā, to deliver, save, rescue, as najjākum delivers you, saves you, rescues you, from sea hardship to shore, land. Ilā al-barri from b-r-r, to land, shore, land, from barr, land, shore, opposite of baḥr sea, as land. Aʿraḍtum from ʿ-r-ḍ, to turn away, avoid, from aʿraḍa, to turn away, avoid, as turn away from Allah, from gratitude, after being delivered. Wa-kāna al-insānu kafūran, and human is ever ungrateful, very ungrateful, from k-f-r, ungrateful, disbelieving, very ungrateful, as kafūran ever ungrateful, very ungrateful, as Devil ever ungrateful to Lord, kafūran, and humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, as human ever hasty, ever ungrateful, as insān human, as in verse 11 human ever hasty, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that when He delivers you safely to shore, land, from sea hardship, you turn away, avoid, turn away from gratitude, from invoking Him alone, return to invoking other gods, etc., and humankind is ever ungrateful, very ungrateful, ungrateful to Lord, as they forget, turn away after being delivered, as ungratefulness.

**Why Forget Gods At Sea Except Him And Turn Away At Shore And Ever Ungrateful**

Why forget all gods except Him at sea hardship and turn away at shore and ever ungrateful? Because at sea hardship, storm, drowning, you realize only Allah can remove harm, as those claimed besides Him have no power to remove harm or transfer, as verse 56 says, they have no power, no ownership, so you forget them, invoke only Him, Allah alone, as only He can save, deliver, as He steers ships for you through sea to seek bounty, ever Merciful, as verse 66 says, and He delivers you to shore, land, saves you, rescues you, but when delivered safely to shore, you turn away, avoid, return to shirk, invoking other gods, etc., as humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, very ungrateful, as Devil ever ungrateful, kafūran, and human ever hasty, ever ungrateful, as insān, as human prays for evil as for good, ever hasty, etc.

**Connection To Lord Steers Ships To Seek Bounty Ever Merciful And No Power To Remove Harm**

Verse 66 Lord steers ships for you through sea so you may seek bounty, ever Merciful to you. Verse 67 when hardship touches you at sea you forget all gods you normally invoke except Him, when He delivers you to shore you turn away, humankind ever ungrateful, showing that ships through sea to seek bounty is mercy, ever Merciful, but at sea hardship you forget other gods, invoke only Him, as only He has power to remove harm, as those claimed besides Him have no power to remove harm or transfer, as verse 56 says, no power, so at sea you realize, forget others, invoke only Him, but at shore you turn away, ungrateful, as humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, as Devil ever ungrateful, kafūran, and human ever hasty.

**Link To Do You Feel Secure Land Swallow Storm Stones And Send Back To Sea Violent Storm**

Next verses 68-69 do you feel secure that He will not cause land to swallow you up or unleash storm of stones, then find none to protect you, or do you feel secure that He will not send you back to sea once again and send violent storm drowning you for denial, then find none to avenge you against Us, showing that after being delivered to shore, you turn away, ungrateful, but do you feel secure that land will not swallow you, storm of stones, or sea violent storm drowning, etc., as warning, as He is ever Merciful but also torment fearsome, as Lord's torment fearsome, maḥdhūran, to be feared.

**Linguistic Note On Massakum And Durr And Dalla And Najjakum And Barr And A'radtum And Kafuran**

Massakum from m-s-s, to touch, afflict, from massa. Ḍurr from ḍ-r-r, hardship, harm, adversity, from ḍarra. Baḥr from b-ḥ-r, sea. Ḍalla from ḍ-l-l, to go astray, be lost, forget, disappear, from ḍalla. Tadʿūna from d-ʿ-w, to call, invoke. Iyyāhu, Him, only Him. Najjā from n-j-w, to deliver, save, rescue, from najjā. Barr from b-r-r, land, shore, from barr. Aʿraḍtum from ʿ-r-ḍ, to turn away, avoid, from aʿraḍa. Insān from a-n-s, human, from insān. Kafūr from k-f-r, ever ungrateful, very ungrateful, from kafara, to be ungrateful, intensive kafūr. The verse's structure — when hardship touches you in sea, those you invoke besides Him go astray, you forget, except Him, then when He delivers you to land you turn away, and human is ever ungrateful — with hardship at sea forgetting other gods except Him and delivering to land turning away and human ever ungrateful, shows human nature at sea hardship invokes only Allah, but at shore turns away, ungrateful, ever ungrateful, as Devil ever ungrateful.

**How This Verse Teaches Human Nature At Sea And Ungratefulness**

This verse teaches human nature at sea hardship and ungratefulness: when hardship touches you at sea, storm, drowning, you totally forget all gods you normally invoke besides Him, idols, etc., except Him, Allah alone, invoke only Him, as only He can remove harm, as those claimed besides Him have no power, as verse 56 says, but when He delivers you safely to shore, land, you turn away, avoid, return to shirk, invoking other gods, etc., and humankind is ever ungrateful, very ungrateful, ungrateful to Lord, as Devil ever ungrateful, kafūran, and human ever hasty, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should be grateful, not ungrateful, should invoke Allah alone at sea and at shore, not forget, not turn away, should be grateful for being delivered, as Lord steers ships for you to seek bounty, ever Merciful, as He dignified children of Adam, carried on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, as mercy, ever Merciful, so be grateful, not ever ungrateful, as humankind ever ungrateful.

**A Reflection On Ever Ungrateful**

Kafūran — ever ungrateful, very ungrateful — as Devil ever ungrateful to Lord, kafūran, verse 27, and humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, verse 67, showing similarity between Devil and humankind in ungratefulness, as Devil ever ungrateful, human ever ungrateful, both kafūran, very ungrateful, as wasteful brothers to devils, brothers, companions, share ungratefulness, as human ever hasty, prays for evil as for good, ever hasty, ever ungrateful, as insān, human, ever hasty, ever ungrateful, so be grateful, not ungrateful, invoke Allah alone, not other gods, as only He has power to remove harm, transfer, steer ships, seek bounty, ever Merciful, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, so be grateful, not ever ungrateful, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding painful punishment, Hell, as torment fearsome.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:68

> **Do you feel secure that He will not cause the land to swallow you up, or unleash upon you a storm of stones? Then you will find none to protect you.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Do You Feel Secure Land Swallow You Or Storm Of Stones**

A-fa-amintum an yakhsifa bikum jāniba al-barri aw yursila ʿalaykum ḥāṣiban — do you feel secure that He will not cause land to swallow you, side of land, or send upon you storm of stones? A-fa-amintum from a-m-n, to feel secure, safe, from amina, to feel secure, safe, interrogative, do you feel secure, safe, as fa-amintum do you feel secure, as rhetorical, warning, do you feel secure? An yakhsifa from kh-s-f, to cause to swallow, to make sink, to cause land to swallow, from khasafa, to cause to swallow, sink, as yakhsifa cause to swallow, make sink, as earth swallows. Bikum, with you, you, you. Jāniba al-barri, side of land, side of shore, part of land, from j-n-b, side, and b-r-r, land, shore, as side of land, as land, shore, as after being delivered to shore, land, you turn away, ungrateful, do you feel secure that land will not swallow you? Aw yursila ʿalaykum ḥāṣiban, or send upon you storm of stones, from r-s-l, to send, and ḥ-ṣ-b, storm of stones, pebbles, from ḥāṣib, storm of stones, that throws stones, pebbles, as storm of stones, as punishment, as Thamud, etc., as ḥāṣib storm of stones. Al-Ṭabarī explains that do you feel secure, safe, that He will not cause land, side of land, shore, to swallow you up, sink you, after He delivered you to shore, you turned away, ungrateful, do you feel secure that land will not swallow you, as Qarun swallowed, etc., or unleash upon you storm of stones, ḥāṣib, storm that throws stones, pebbles, as punishment, as people of Lut, etc., as warning, as He is ever Merciful but also torment fearsome, as Lord's torment fearsome, maḥdhūran, to be feared, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record.

**Then You Will Find None To Protect You**

Thumma lā tajidū lakum wakīlan — then you will find not for you guardian, protector, keeper. Thumma, then, after land swallow or storm stones. Lā tajidū from w-j-d, to find, you will not find. Lakum, for you, for yourselves. Wakīlan from w-k-l, guardian, protector, keeper, trustee, as wakīl, keeper, guardian, as verse 2 Trustee of Affairs, verse 54 not sent you as keeper over them, verse 65 sufficient Lord as Guardian, wakīlan, and verse 68 no guardian, protector, keeper, to protect you from land swallow or storm stones, as only Allah Guardian sufficient, but if He punishes, no guardian to protect, as only He has power to remove harm, transfer, etc., as those claimed besides Him have no power, as verse 56 says, so if He causes land to swallow or sends storm stones, you will find no guardian, protector, to protect you.

**Why Land Swallow And Storm Stones Warning After Turning Away Ungrateful**

Why land swallow and storm stones warning after turning away, ungrateful, when delivered to shore? Because when hardship at sea you forget all gods except Him, invoke only Him, but when delivered to shore, land, you turn away, ungrateful, ever ungrateful, as humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, as Devil ever ungrateful, kafūran, so warning: do you feel secure, safe, that He will not cause land, side of land, shore, to swallow you up, sink you, after delivering you, or unleash upon you storm of stones, ḥāṣib, storm throwing stones, as punishment, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, as many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., as Qarun swallowed, people of Lut storm stones, etc., as examples, and then you will find no guardian, protector, keeper, to protect you, as only Allah Guardian sufficient, but if He punishes, no guardian, as only He has power.

**Connection To Lord Steers Ships Ever Merciful And Forget Gods At Sea Turn Away At Shore Ever Ungrateful**

Verse 66 Lord steers ships for you through sea so you may seek bounty, ever Merciful. Verse 67 when hardship at sea you forget all gods except Him, when delivers to shore you turn away, humankind ever ungrateful. Verse 68 do you feel secure that He will not cause land to swallow you or unleash storm of stones, then you will find none to protect you, showing that after being delivered to shore, turning away, ungrateful, do you feel secure that land will not swallow you, as Qarun, or storm stones, as Lut, etc., as warning, as He is ever Merciful but also torment fearsome, and no guardian to protect if He punishes, as only He Guardian sufficient, but if He punishes, no guardian.

**Link To Or Send Back To Sea Violent Storm Drowning For Denial No Avenger**

Next verse 69 poses another security question about returning to sea and a fierce gale sinking you for ingratitude, then finding no champion against Us, showing second warning: do you feel safe He will not return you to sea and unleash fierce gale sinking you for denial, as punishment, as sea dangerous, ships steered, but violent gale drowning for denial, as warning, as He is ever Merciful but torment fearsome, and no avenger against Us, as only Allah has power.

**Linguistic Note On Amintum And Yakhsifa And Janib And Barr And Hāsib And Wakilan**

Amintum from a-m-n, to feel secure, safe, from amina. Yakhsifa from kh-s-f, to cause to swallow, sink, from khasafa. Jānib from j-n-b, side, from jānib. Barr from b-r-r, land, shore, from barr. Yursila from r-s-l, to send. Ḥāṣib from ḥ-ṣ-b, storm of stones, from ḥaṣaba, to throw stones, from ḥāṣib, storm throwing stones. Tajidū from w-j-d, to find. Wakīl from w-k-l, guardian, protector, keeper, from wakala. The verse's structure — do you feel secure that He will not cause land to swallow you side of land or send upon you storm of stones, then you will find not for you guardian — with rhetorical question do you feel secure that land will not swallow or storm stones and statement then you will find no guardian, shows warning after turning away ungrateful at shore, do you feel secure that land will not swallow or storm stones, as punishment, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, and no guardian to protect if He punishes.

**How This Verse Teaches Warning After Ungratefulness**

This verse teaches warning after ungratefulness at shore: do you feel secure, safe, that He will not cause land, side of land, shore, to swallow you up, sink you, after delivering you to shore, you turned away, ungrateful, ever ungrateful, or unleash upon you storm of stones, ḥāṣib, storm throwing stones, pebbles, as punishment, as Qarun swallowed, people of Lut storm stones, etc., as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., then you will find none to protect you, no guardian, protector, keeper, to protect you from land swallow or storm stones, as only Allah Guardian sufficient, but if He punishes, no guardian, as only He has power to remove harm, transfer, etc., as those claimed besides Him have no power, as verse 56 says, so be grateful, not ungrateful, not turn away after being delivered, as humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, as Devil ever ungrateful, kafūran, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding destruction, severe punishment, painful punishment, Hell, torment fearsome.

**A Reflection On No Guardian To Protect**

Lā tajidū lakum wakīlan — you will find not for you guardian, protector, keeper — indicates that if Allah causes land to swallow you, side of land, or sends upon you storm of stones, ḥāṣib, as punishment after turning away ungrateful at shore, you will find no guardian, protector, keeper, to protect you, save you, as only Allah Guardian sufficient, wakīlan, sufficient Lord as Guardian, verse 65, but if He punishes, no guardian to protect, as only He has power, as those claimed besides Him have no power to remove harm or transfer, as verse 56 says, no power, so invoke Him alone, be grateful, not ungrateful, not turn away, as humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, follow most upright, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding land swallow, storm stones, no guardian, destruction, severe punishment.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:69

> **Or do you feel secure that He will not send you back to sea once again, and send upon you a violent storm, drowning you for your denial? Then you will find none to avenge you against Us.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Do You Feel Secure He Will Not Send You Back To Sea And Violent Storm Drowning For Denial**

Am a-mintum an yuʿīdakum fīhi tāratan ukhrā fa-yursila ʿalaykum qāṣifan mina al-rīḥi fa-yughriqakum bi-mā kafartum — or do you feel secure that He will not return you in it another time, send upon you violent storm wind, drown you for what you disbelieved, denied. Am, or, or do you feel secure, alternative to previous do you feel secure land swallow or storm stones. A-mintum from a-m-n, to feel secure, safe, as previous a-fa-amintum do you feel secure. An yuʿīdakum from ʿ-w-d, to return, bring back, repeat, as yuʿīdakum return you, bring you back, as yuʿīdunā will bring us back, return us, as resurrection, return. Fīhi, in it, in sea, in baḥr, sea, as ships through sea, hardship at sea, etc. Tāratan ukhrā from t-w-r, another time, once again, another turn, from tārah, time, turn, and ukhrā, other, another, from ā-kh-r, other. Fa-yursila from r-s-l, then send, and send, as yursila send. ʿAlaykum, upon you. Qāṣifan from q-ṣ-f, violent storm, that breaks, shatters, from qaṣafa, to break, shatter, violent, from qāṣif, violent storm, breaking, shattering, as violent storm wind that breaks ships, etc. Mina al-rīḥi from r-w-ḥ, from wind, storm wind, from rīḥ, wind, as rīḥ wind, storm. Fa-yughriqakum from gh-r-q, to drown, make drown, from aghraqa, to drown, make drown, as yughriqakum drown you, make you drown. Bi-mā kafartum from k-f-r, for what you disbelieved, denied, for your disbelief, denial, ungratefulness, as kafartum you disbelieved, denied, were ungrateful, from kafara, to disbelieve, deny, be ungrateful, as kafūran ever ungrateful, and kafartum disbelieved. Al-Ṭabarī explains that or do you feel secure, safe, that He will not send you back to sea once again, another time, after delivering you to shore, you turned away, ungrateful, and send upon you violent storm, qāṣif, wind that breaks, shatters, violent storm, drowning you, making you drown, for your denial, disbelief, ungratefulness, as punishment, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, as many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., as sea dangerous, ships steered, but violent storm drowning for denial, as warning, as He is ever Merciful but torment fearsome.

**Then You Will Find None To Avenge You Against Us**

Thumma lā tajidū lakum ʿalaynā tabīʿan — then you will find not for you against Us follower, avenger, pursuer. Thumma, then, after violent storm drowning for denial. Lā tajidū from w-j-d, to find, you will not find, as previous lā tajidū lakum wakīlan, no guardian. Lakum, for you. ʿAlaynā, against Us, upon Us, against Allah, Us. Tabīʿan from t-b-ʿ, follower, pursuer, avenger, one who follows, pursues, avenges, from tabiʿa, to follow, pursue, from tabīʿ, follower, pursuer, avenger, as one who avenges you against Us, follows to avenge. Al-Ṭabarī explains that then you will find none to avenge you against Us, no follower, pursuer, avenger, who will follow, pursue, avenge you against Us, against Allah, if He drowns you for denial, as only Allah has power, as those claimed besides Him have no power to remove harm or transfer, as verse 56 says, no power, so if He sends back to sea again and sends violent storm drowning you for denial, you will find none to avenge you against Us, no avenger, pursuer, follower, to avenge, as only Allah has power.

**Why Send Back To Sea Violent Storm Drowning For Denial And No Avenger**

Why send back to sea once again and violent storm drowning for denial and no avenger? Because when hardship at sea you forget all gods except Him, invoke only Him, but when delivered to shore, land, you turn away, ungrateful, ever ungrateful, as humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, as Devil ever ungrateful, kafūran, so do you feel secure that He will not send you back to sea once again, another time, and send violent storm, qāṣif, wind breaking, shattering, drowning you for your denial, disbelief, ungratefulness, as punishment, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, as many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., and then you will find none to avenge you against Us, no follower, pursuer, avenger, to avenge you against Allah, if He drowns you for denial, as only Allah has power, as those claimed besides Him have no power, as verse 56 says, no power, so be grateful, not ungrateful, not turn away, not deny.

**Connection To Land Swallow Storm Stones No Guardian And Sea Return Violent Storm No Avenger**

Verse 68 do you feel secure land swallow you side of land or storm stones, then find no guardian to protect you. Verse 69 or do you feel secure He will not send you back to sea once again and send violent storm drowning you for denial, then find none to avenge you against Us, showing two warnings after turning away ungrateful at shore: land warning, land swallow, storm stones, no guardian, and sea warning, return to sea again, violent storm drowning for denial, no avenger, both warnings, as He is ever Merciful but torment fearsome, and no guardian, no avenger, if He punishes, as only He Guardian sufficient, but if He punishes, no guardian, no avenger, as only He has power.

**Link To Lord Steers Ships Ever Merciful And Forget Gods At Sea Turn Away At Shore Ever Ungrateful And Dignified Children Of Adam**

Verse 66 Lord steers ships for you through sea to seek bounty, ever Merciful. Verse 67 hardship at sea forget all gods except Him, delivers to shore turn away, humankind ever ungrateful. Verse 68-69 do you feel secure land swallow or storm stones, no guardian, or send back to sea again violent storm drowning for denial, no avenger, warnings after ungratefulness, and verse 70 We dignified children of Adam, carried them on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, showing that despite warnings, children of Adam dignified, honoured, carried on land and sea, granted provisions, privileged, as mercy, ever Merciful, but warnings for ungratefulness, denial, turning away.

**Linguistic Note On Amintum And Yu'idukum And Tarah And Qasif And Rih And Yughriqum And Tabi'an**

Amintum from a-m-n, to feel secure, safe, from amina. Yuʿīdakum from ʿ-w-d, to return, bring back, from aʿāda. Fīhi, in it, in sea. Tārah from t-w-r, time, turn, from tārah. Ukhrā from ā-kh-r, other, another. Yursila from r-s-l, to send. Qāṣif from q-ṣ-f, violent storm, breaking, shattering, from qaṣafa, to break, shatter, from qāṣif, violent storm. Rīḥ from r-w-ḥ, wind, from rīḥ. Yughriqakum from gh-r-q, to drown, make drown, from aghraqa. Kafartum from k-f-r, to disbelieve, deny, be ungrateful, from kafara. Tajidū from w-j-d, to find. Tabīʿ from t-b-ʿ, follower, pursuer, avenger, from tabiʿa, to follow, pursue. The verse's structure — or do you feel secure that He will not return you in it another time and send upon you violent storm wind and drown you for what you disbelieved, then you will find not for you against Us follower, avenger — with rhetorical question do you feel secure return to sea again and violent storm drowning for denial and statement no avenger against Us, shows warning after turning away ungrateful at shore, do you feel secure return to sea again and violent storm drowning for denial, as punishment, and no avenger, as only Allah has power.

**How This Verse Teaches Warning Of Sea Return And Violent Storm**

This verse teaches warning of sea return and violent storm drowning for denial and no avenger: or do you feel secure, safe, that He will not send you back to sea once again, another time, after delivering you to shore, you turned away, ungrateful, ever ungrateful, and send upon you violent storm, qāṣif, wind breaking, shattering, violent storm, drowning you, making you drown, for your denial, disbelief, ungratefulness, for what you disbelieved, denied, as punishment, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., then you will find none to avenge you against Us, no follower, pursuer, avenger, to avenge you against Allah, if He drowns you for denial, as only Allah has power, as those claimed besides Him have no power to remove harm or transfer, as verse 56 says, no power, so be grateful, not ungrateful, not turn away, not deny, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding violent storm drowning for denial, no avenger, destruction, severe punishment.

**A Reflection On No Avenger Against Us**

Lā tajidū lakum ʿalaynā tabīʿan — you will find not for you against Us follower, avenger, pursuer — indicates that if Allah sends you back to sea again and sends violent storm drowning you for denial, disbelief, ungratefulness, you will find none to avenge you against Us, against Allah, no follower, pursuer, avenger, who will follow, pursue, avenge you against Us, as only Allah has power, as those claimed besides Him have no power, no ownership, to remove harm or transfer, as verse 56 says, no power, so invoke Him alone, be grateful, not ungrateful, not turn away, as humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, as Devil ever ungrateful, kafūran, follow most upright, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding violent storm drowning for denial, no avenger, destruction, severe punishment, painful punishment, Hell, torment fearsome, but Lord ever Merciful, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving to those who constantly turn.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:70

> **Indeed, We have dignified the children of Adam, carried them on land and sea, granted them good and lawful provisions, and privileged them far above many of Our creatures.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**We Dignified Children Of Adam**

Wa-la-qad karramnā banī Ādama — and We have surely dignified, honoured children of Adam, sons of Adam. Wa-la-qad, and surely, indeed, certainly, emphasis, as la-qad surely. Karramnā from k-r-m, to dignify, honour, make noble, generous, from karuma, to be noble, generous, from karramnā We dignified, honoured, as karramta You honoured above me, Iblis said do you see this one you honoured above me, and karramnā We dignified children of Adam, showing honour for Adam, children, as angels prostrated, Iblis refused out of arrogance, but children of Adam dignified, honoured, as honour, nobility. Banī Ādama from b-n-y, children, sons, offspring, and Ādam, Adam, first human, as children of Adam, sons of Adam, all humankind, descendants of Adam, as progeny of those carried with Noah, etc., as children of Adam, all humans. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We have surely dignified, honoured children of Adam, sons of Adam, all humankind, made noble, generous, honoured, with many honours, as intellect, speech, etc., as dignified, honoured, above many creatures. Al-Qurṭubī says karramnā banī Ādama means We dignified, honoured children of Adam with many honours: intellect, speech, writing, etc., made noble, honoured, above many creatures, privileged.

**Carried Them On Land And Sea**

Wa-ḥamalnāhum fī al-barri wa-al-baḥri — and We carried them in land and sea, on land and sea. Wa-ḥamalnāhum from ḥ-m-l, to carry, bear, transport, from ḥamala, to carry, bear, as ḥamalnāhum We carried them, transported them, as carrying on land and sea, as ships through sea, as animals on land, etc. Fī al-barri wa-al-baḥri, in land and sea, on land and sea, in land and sea, as barr land, shore, and baḥr sea, as land and sea, as Lord steers ships for you through sea so you may seek bounty, ever Merciful, as verse 66 says, and as We carried them on land and sea, as part of dignifying, honouring, as carrying, transporting, as honour, as privilege, as He subjected for your benefit night and day, sun and moon, etc., and land and sea, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We carried them on land and sea, transported them, on land via animals, vehicles, and on sea via ships, etc., as part of dignifying, honouring, as carrying, as privilege, as He steers ships for you through sea, etc.

**Granted Good And Lawful Provisions**

Wa-razaqnāhum mina al-ṭayyibāti — and We provided them from good, pure, lawful provisions, good things. Wa-razaqnāhum from r-z-q, to provide, provision, sustenance, as narzuquhum We provide for them, as yabsuṭu al-rizqa extends provision, gives abundant or limited, as provision, and razaqnāhum We provided them, granted provisions. Mina al-ṭayyibāti from ṭ-y-b, good, pure, lawful, wholesome, from ṭayyib, good, pure, lawful, wholesome, plural ṭayyibāt, good, pure, lawful things, provisions, as good and lawful provisions, wholesome, pure, lawful, as provision good, pure, lawful, not impure, unlawful, as bounty never withheld, some favoured over others, provision abundant or limited, but good provisions granted. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We granted them good and lawful provisions, good things, pure, lawful, wholesome provisions, as food, drink, etc., good, pure, lawful, as provision, as part of dignifying, honouring, as granting good provisions, as bounty, as mercy, ever Merciful.

**Privileged Far Above Many Creatures**

Wa-faḍḍalnāhum ʿalā kathīrin mimman khalaqnā tafḍīlan — and We privileged them, favoured them over many of those We created with great favour, privilege. Wa-faḍḍalnāhum from f-ḍ-l, to favour, privilege, prefer, give superiority, as faḍḍalnā some over others, favoured some over others, and faḍḍalnāhum We privileged them, favoured them, privileged. ʿAlā kathīrin, over many, above many, from k-th-r, many, much. Mimman khalaqnā, from those We created, of those We created, from m-n and kh-l-q, those We created, creatures, creation, as many creatures. Tafḍīlan from f-ḍ-l, with great favour, privilege, great favouring, privileging, with emphasis, great favour, privilege, from tafḍīl, favouring, privileging, as akbaru tafḍīlan greater in favour, and tafḍīlan great favour, privilege, as privileged far above many creatures with great favour. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We privileged them, favoured them far above many of Our creatures, many of those We created, with great favour, privilege, great favouring, meaning children of Adam privileged, favoured far above many creatures, many of creation, with great favour, privilege, as honour, dignity, as part of dignifying, carrying, provisioning, privileging, as children of Adam privileged far above many creatures, as honour, as He favoured some over others, some prophets above others, etc., but children of Adam privileged far above many.

**Why Dignified, Carried, Provided, Privileged**

Why dignified children of Adam, carried on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures? Because Adam honoured, angels prostrated, Iblis refused out of arrogance, but children of Adam dignified, honoured, despite Iblis vow to take hold of descendants except few, and despite ungratefulness, ever ungrateful, turning away at shore, etc., and despite warnings land swallow, storm stones, no guardian, sea return violent storm drowning, no avenger, etc., children of Adam dignified, honoured, carried, provided, privileged, as mercy, ever Merciful, as Lord steers ships for you through sea to seek bounty, ever Merciful, as He is Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and as part of favouring some over others, but Hereafter far greater in rank and favour, so dignified in world but Hereafter greater.

**Linguistic Note On Karramna And Hamalnahum And Barr And Bahr And Razaqnahum And Tayyibat And Faddalnahum And Tafdilan**

Karramnā from k-r-m, to dignify, honour, make noble, from karuma, to be noble. Banī from b-n-y, children, sons, from ibn, son. Ādam, Adam. Ḥamalnā from ḥ-m-l, to carry, bear, transport, from ḥamala. Barr from b-r-r, land, shore, from barr. Baḥr from b-ḥ-r, sea, from baḥr. Razaqnā from r-z-q, to provide, provision, from razaqa. Ṭayyibāt from ṭ-y-b, good, pure, lawful, wholesome, plural of ṭayyib, from ṭāba, to be good, pure. Faḍḍalnā from f-ḍ-l, to favour, privilege, prefer, from faḍala. Kathīr from k-th-r, many, much. Khalqnā from kh-l-q, We created, from khalaqa. Tafḍīl from f-ḍ-l, favouring, privileging, with great favour, from faḍḍala. The verse's structure — We dignified children of Adam and carried them in land and sea and provided them from good things and privileged them over many of those We created with great favour — with four honours, dignified, carried, provided good, privileged far above many with great favour, shows comprehensive honour for children of Adam, dignified, honoured, privileged, as mercy, ever Merciful, as part of favouring, but Hereafter far greater.

**How This Verse Teaches Dignity And Honour Of Children Of Adam**

This verse teaches dignity and honour of children of Adam: We have surely dignified, honoured children of Adam, all humankind, made noble, generous, honoured, with intellect, speech, etc., carried them on land and sea, transported them, on land via animals, vehicles, on sea via ships, so they may seek bounty, ever Merciful, granted them good and lawful provisions, good, pure, lawful, wholesome provisions, and privileged them far above many of Our creatures, many of those We created, with great favour, privilege, great favouring, as honour, dignity, privilege, as part of favouring some over others, but Hereafter far greater in rank and favour, so dignified in world but Hereafter greater, far greater. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know children of Adam dignified, honoured, carried on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, with great favour, as mercy, ever Merciful, so be grateful, not ever ungrateful, not turn away after being delivered, not feel secure land swallow or storm stones, sea return violent storm, but be grateful, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour in Hereafter, which is far greater than worldly dignity.

**A Reflection On Privileged Far Above Many Creatures With Great Favour**

Faḍḍalnāhum ʿalā kathīrin mimman khalaqnā tafḍīlan — privileged them over many of those We created with great favour, great privileging — indicates that children of Adam privileged, favoured far above many creatures, many of creation, with great favour, privilege, tremendous favour, not small, but great, tafḍīlan, great favour, as He favoured some over others in world, some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms, etc., but children of Adam privileged far above many creatures with great favour, as honour, dignity, as part of dignifying, carrying, provisioning, privileging, as mercy, ever Merciful, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome for those who are ungrateful, turn away, feel secure land swallow, storm stones, sea return violent storm, etc., and as Hereafter far greater in rank and favour, far greater than worldly privileging, so desire Hereafter, strive accordingly, be believer, appreciated striving, mighty reward, far greater rank and favour.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:71

> **˹Beware of˺ the Day We will summon every people with their leader. So whoever will be given their record in their right hand will read it ˹happily˺ and will not be wronged ˹even by the width of˺ the thread of a date stone.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Day We Will Summon Every People With Their Leader**

Yawma nadʿū kulla unāsin bi-imāmihim — Day We will call, summon every people with their leader, imam. Yawma from y-w-m, Day, Day of Resurrection, Beware of Day, Day We will summon. Nadʿū from d-ʿ-w, to call, summon, as yadʿūkum He will call you, Day He will call you respond praising Him, and nadʿū We will call, summon. Kulla unāsin from a-n-s, every people, every group, every community, from unās, people, plural of insān, human, as every people, every community. Bi-imāmihim from a-m-m, with their leader, imam, guide, from imām, leader, guide, imam, as leader, guide, book, record, etc., as every people summoned with their leader, imam, who led them, Prophet, Book, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that Beware of Day We will summon every people with their leader, imam, meaning Day of Judgment We will call every people, every community, with their leader, imam, who led them, Prophet, or Book, or leader, to be judged, as each people summoned with leader. Al-Qurṭubī says bi-imāmihim means with their leader, imam, guide, Prophet, or Book, or record, etc., as every people summoned with leader.

**Whoever Given Record In Right Hand Will Read Happily And Not Wronged Even Thread Of Date Stone**

Fa-man ūtiya kitābahu bi-yamīnihi fa-ulā'ika yaqra'ūna kitābahum wa-lā yuẓlamūna fatīlan — then whoever is given his book in his right hand, those will read their book and will not be wronged even thread of date stone. Fa-man, then whoever, who. Ūtiya from a-t-y, to be given, given, from ātā, to give, as ūtiya given, passive, given his book. Kitābahu from k-t-b, his book, record, as kitāb Book, Record, as record laid open, bound destiny to neck, record laid open, read your book, sufficient self as reckoner, etc. Bi-yamīnihi from y-m-n, in his right hand, right, from yamīn, right hand, right, as right hand, as right hand given book indicates success, as opposed to left, behind back. Fa-ulā'ika, then those, so those. Yaqra'ūna from q-r-ʾ, to read, recite, as iqra' read, read your book, etc., they will read their book, happily, as happily, as success. Kitābahum, their book, record, from k-t-b. Wa-lā yuẓlamūna from ẓ-l-m, to be wronged, oppressed, injustice, from ẓalama, to wrong, oppress, as yuẓlamūna will not be wronged, not oppressed. Fatīlan from f-t-l, thread of date stone, thin thread, small amount, from fatīl, thread, thin thread in groove of date stone, as even width of thread of date stone, smallest amount, as not wronged even by width of thread of date stone, smallest injustice, as justice, no injustice, as Lord does not wrong. Al-Ṭabarī explains that whoever given record in right hand, book in right hand, will read it happily, joyfully, and will not be wronged even by width of thread of date stone, smallest amount, meaning no injustice, full justice, reward, as mighty reward, appreciated striving, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says fatīlan means thread of date stone, thin thread in groove, smallest amount, indicating no injustice even smallest.

**Why Summon Every People With Leader And Right Hand Reading Happily No Wrong Even Thread**

Why summon every people with leader and right hand reading happily no wrong even thread? Because Day of Judgment, Day He will call you respond praising Him thinking remained only little while, and Day We will summon every people with their leader, to be judged, as each people summoned with leader, imam, Prophet, Book, etc., and whoever given record in right hand will read happily and not wronged even thread of date stone, showing justice, no injustice, mighty reward for believers who do good, appreciated striving, as Qur'an guides to most upright, gives good news to believers who do good mighty reward, warns those who do not believe painful punishment, and record laid open, bound destiny to neck, read book, sufficient self as reckoner, no bearer bears burden, etc., and now right hand reading happily, no wrong even thread, showing success, justice, as opposed to blind in this world blind in Hereafter far more astray, as next verse says.

**Connection To Record Laid Open And Read Book Sufficient Self And No Bearer Bears Burden**

Verse 13 bound destiny to neck, record laid open, verse 14 read your book sufficient self as reckoner, verse 15 no bearer bears burden, etc., verse 71 summon every people with leader, whoever given record in right hand will read happily and not wronged even thread, showing record theme: destiny bound to neck, record laid open, read book, sufficient self, no bearer bears burden, now summon every people with leader, given record right hand read happily, no wrong even thread, showing justice, success, as record laid open, reading, etc., and accountability.

Verse 70 dignified children of Adam, carried on land and sea, granted good provisions, privileged far above many creatures, showing honour for children of Adam, and verse 71 Day summon every people with leader, whoever given record right hand read happily no wrong even thread, showing that dignified children of Adam will be summoned, judged, some given right hand reading happily, no wrong, as success, as mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, while others blind in this world blind in Hereafter far more astray, as next verse.

**Linguistic Note On Nad'u And Unas And Imam And Utiya Kitabahu Bi-yaminihi And Fatilan**

Yawm from y-w-m, Day. Nadʿū from d-ʿ-w, to call, summon, from daʿā. Kull from k-l-l, every. Unās from a-n-s, people, plural of insān, human. Imām from a-m-m, leader, guide, from amma, to lead, from imām. Ūtiya from a-t-y, to be given, from ātā. Kitāb from k-t-b, book, record. Yamīn from y-m-n, right hand, right, from yamīn. Yaqra'ūna from q-r-ʾ, to read, recite. Yuẓlamūna from ẓ-l-m, to be wronged, oppressed, from ẓalama. Fatīl from f-t-l, thread of date stone, thin thread, from fatala, to twist, thread. The verse's structure — Day We will summon every people with their leader, then whoever given his book in right hand those will read their book and will not be wronged even thread — with Day summon every people with leader and whoever given right hand read happily no wrong even thread, shows Day of Judgment summoning with leader, success for right hand, justice no wrong even smallest thread, as mighty reward, appreciated striving.

**How This Verse Teaches Summoning With Leader And Right Hand Success No Injustice**

This verse teaches summoning every people with leader on Day and right hand success no injustice: Beware of Day We will summon every people with their leader, imam, guide, Prophet, Book, etc., to be judged, and whoever will be given their record, book, in right hand, right, will read it happily, joyfully, and will not be wronged even by width of thread of date stone, smallest amount, no injustice, full justice, mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour. **A Reflection On Right Hand And No Wrong Even Thread**

Bi-yamīnihi — in his right hand — indicates success, honour, as right hand given book indicates success, as opposed to left hand, behind back, indicating failure, as Qur'an says. Yaqra'ūna kitābahum — they will read their book, happily — indicates joy, success, reading record laid open happily, as record laid open, read your book, sufficient self as reckoner, etc., now reading happily, as success, mighty reward, appreciated striving. Wa-lā yuẓlamūna fatīlan — not wronged even thread of date stone, width of thread — indicates justice, no injustice even smallest, smallest amount, thread of date stone, thin thread in groove, smallest, as Lord does not wrong, as He is All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and justice, no wrong even thread, as mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, while blind in this world blind in Hereafter far more astray, as next verse, etc.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:72

> **But whoever is blind ˹to the truth˺ in this ˹world˺ will be blind in the Hereafter, and ˹even˺ far more astray from the ˹Right˺ Way.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Whoever Blind In This World Will Be Blind In Hereafter Far More Astray**

Wa-man kāna fī hādhihi aʿmā fa-huwa fī al-ākhirati aʿmā wa-aḍallu sabīlan — and whoever is in this, this world, blind, then he is in Hereafter blind and more astray way. Wa-man, and whoever, who. Kāna, is, was, is. Fī hādhihi, in this, in this world, this life, this world, as hādhihi this, this world, as worldly life, fleeting, ʿājilah. Aʿmā from ʿ-m-y, blind, blind to truth, from ʿamiya, to be blind, from aʿmā, blind, as blind to truth, blind to guidance, as blind to truth in this world, not seeing truth, guidance, most upright, as disbelievers have veils over hearts, deafness in ears, hidden barrier, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, etc., blind to truth. Fa-huwa, then he, so he. Fī al-ākhirati, in Hereafter, in Hereafter, Hereafter, as ākhirah Hereafter, opposite of hādhihi this world. Aʿmā, blind, blind in Hereafter, as blind to truth in Hereafter, blind in Hereafter, as punishment, as blind in this world blind in Hereafter. Wa-aḍallu from ḍ-l-l, more astray, farther astray, more misguided, from ḍalla, to go astray, as aḍall more astray, farther astray, as far more astray from Right Way. Sabīlan from s-b-l, way, path, Right Way, as sabīl way, path, Right Way, as far more astray from Right Way, more astray than blind in world. Al-Ṭabarī explains that whoever is blind to truth in this world, blind to guidance, most upright, Qur'an guides to most upright, but blind, not seeing, not following, then he will be blind in Hereafter, blind in Hereafter, and even far more astray from Right Way, more misguided, farther astray, as punishment, as blind in world blind in Hereafter, more astray.

**A Reflection On Blind And Far More Astray From Right Way**

Aʿmā fī hādhihi, blind in this, this world, blind to truth, guidance, most upright, Qur'an guides to most upright, but blind, not seeing, not following, due to barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, etc. Fa-huwa fī al-ākhirati aʿmā wa-aḍallu sabīlan — then he is in Hereafter blind and more astray way, far more astray from Right Way — indicates that blindness in world leads to blindness in Hereafter and even greater misguidance, farther astray, more astray from Right Way, as punishment, as justice, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and as record laid open, etc., and as summon every people with leader, right hand reading happily no wrong even thread, success, vs blind failure, far more astray. The believer who is not blind in this world, sees truth, guidance, most upright, follows, will not be blind in Hereafter, not far more astray, but guided, Right Way, right hand reading happily, no wrong even thread, mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour in Hereafter, which is far greater than worldly, as Hereafter far greater in rank and favour.
