## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:73

> **They definitely ˹thought they˺ were about to lure you away from what We have revealed to you ˹O Prophet˺, hoping that you would attribute something else to Us falsely—and then they would have certainly taken you as a close friend.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**They Were About To Lure You Away From What We Revealed**

Wa-in kādū la-yaftinūnaka ʿani alladhī awḥaynā ilayka — and indeed they were about to lure, tempt you away from what We revealed to you. Wa-in, and indeed, indeed, surely, emphasis, they were about to. Kādū from k-w-d, to be about to, almost, nearly, from kāda, to be about to, almost. La-yaftinūnaka from f-t-n, to lure, tempt, test, mislead, from fatana, to lure, tempt, test, mislead, as fitnah test, trial, temptation, as yaftinūnaka lure you, tempt you away. ʿAni alladhī, from what, away from what. Awḥaynā from w-ḥ-y, We revealed, inspired, as awḥā revealed, as awḥaynā We revealed to you, O Prophet, as revelation, Qur'an, wisdom, most upright, as this is part of wisdom revealed to you, as verse 39 says. Ilayka, to you, O Prophet. Al-Ṭabarī explains that they, disbelievers, polytheists, definitely thought they were about to lure you away, tempt you away from what We revealed to you, Qur'an, wisdom, most upright, hoping you would attribute something else to Us falsely, compromise, etc., as they tried to lure Prophet away from revelation, to compromise, as they wanted him to attribute something else to Allah falsely, to accept their gods, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says kādū la-yaftinūnaka means they were about to lure you, tempt you away from what We revealed to you, as they tried to make Prophet compromise, attribute something else to Allah falsely.

**Hoping You Would Attribute Something Else To Us Falsely And Then Taken As Close Friend**

Li-taftariya ʿalaynā ghayrahu wa-idhan la-ittakhadhūka khalīlan — so you would invent, attribute something else to Us falsely, other than it, and then they would have taken you as close friend. Li-taftariya from f-r-y, to invent, fabricate, attribute falsely, lie, from iftarā, to invent, fabricate, attribute falsely, as taftariya you would invent, attribute falsely, as attribute something else to Us falsely, other than what We revealed. ʿAlaynā, upon Us, against Us, to Us, to Allah, Us. Ghayrahu, other than it, something else, other than what We revealed, from gh-y-r, other, else. Wa-idhan, and then, in that case, then. La-ittakhadhūka from a-kh-dh, to take, adopt, take as, from ittakhadha, to take, adopt, as ittakhadhūka they would have taken you. Khalīlan from kh-l-l, close friend, intimate friend, from khalīl, close friend, intimate friend, as friend, close friend. Al-Ṭabarī explains that hoping that you would attribute something else to Us falsely, other than what We revealed, invent, fabricate, lie against Us, and then they would have certainly taken you as close friend, intimate friend, as they would have taken you as friend if you compromised, attributed something else falsely, accepted their gods, etc., as they wanted close friendship via compromise.

**Why Lure Away From Revelation And Attribute Falsely And Close Friend**

Why lure away from what We revealed, attribute something else falsely, taken as close friend? Because disbelievers, polytheists, wanted Prophet to compromise, to attribute something else to Allah falsely, to accept their gods, idols, etc., to invent, fabricate, lie against Allah, other than revelation, Qur'an, wisdom, most upright, and if he did, they would take him as close friend, intimate friend, as they wanted friendship via compromise, as they thought they were about to lure him away, tempt him away from revelation, as they tried, as they were about to, almost, as kādū, almost, about to, but Allah made him steadfast, as next verse says had We not made you steadfast you would have inclined to them a little, and then double punishment, etc.

**Connection To Made You Steadfast Would Have Inclined Little And Double Punishment And No Helper**

Verse 73 they were about to lure you away from what We revealed hoping you would attribute something else falsely and then taken as close friend. Verse 74 had We not made you steadfast you would have inclined to them a little. Verse 75 then We would have made you taste double punishment in this life and after death and you would have found no helper against Us, showing sequence: they were about to lure away, tempt, hoping attribute falsely, taken as close friend, but Allah made Prophet steadfast, so he did not incline, otherwise double punishment, no helper, as warning, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses people, knows best, etc., and as Our way with messengers sent before you, no change in Our way, as verse 77 says.

**Link To Our Way With Messengers And No Change And Intimidate To Drive Out Of Land**

Verse 76 they were about to intimidate you to drive you out of land of Mecca, but then they would not have survived after you left except little while. Verse 77 this has been Our way with messengers We sent before you, you will never find any change in Our way, showing that luring away, intimidating to drive out, are ways of disbelievers against messengers, but Our way with messengers is steadfast, no change, as messengers made steadfast, not lured, not intimidated, etc., as divine law, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Kadu And Yaftinunaka And Awḥayna And Taftariya And Khalilan**

Kādū from k-w-d, to be about to, almost, from kāda. Yaftinūnaka from f-t-n, to lure, tempt, test, mislead, from fatana. Alladhī awḥaynā, what We revealed, from w-ḥ-y, to reveal, inspire, from awḥā. Taftariya from f-r-y, to invent, fabricate, attribute falsely, from iftarā. Ghayr from gh-y-r, other, else. Ittakhadhūka from a-kh-dh, to take, adopt, from ittakhadha. Khalīl from kh-l-l, close friend, intimate friend, from khalīl. The verse's structure — they were about to lure you away from what We revealed to you so you would attribute something else to Us falsely and then they would have taken you as close friend — with about to lure away from revelation and hoping attribute falsely and then taken as close friend, shows attempt to make Prophet compromise, attribute falsely, to gain friendship, but Allah made steadfast, not inclined, otherwise double punishment, no helper.

**How This Verse Teaches Attempt To Lure Prophet And Compromise**

This verse teaches attempt to lure Prophet away from revelation and compromise: they, disbelievers, definitely thought they were about to lure you away, tempt you away from what We revealed to you, O Prophet, Qur'an, wisdom, most upright, hoping that you would attribute something else to Us falsely, invent, fabricate, other than revelation, and then they would have certainly taken you as close friend, intimate friend, as they wanted friendship via compromise, accepting their gods, etc., as they tried to lure, tempt, as they were about to, almost, as kādū, almost, about to, but Allah made steadfast, as next verse says, had We not made you steadfast you would have inclined a little, double punishment, no helper, as warning, as Prophet protected, steadfast. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should not be lured away from what Allah revealed, not attribute something else falsely to Allah, not compromise for close friendship with disbelievers, but be steadfast, as Prophet made steadfast, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding double punishment, no helper.

**A Reflection On Close Friend Via Compromise**

Khalīlan — close friend, intimate friend — they would have taken you as close friend if you attributed something else falsely to Us, compromised, accepted their gods, etc., showing that close friendship with disbelievers via compromise, attributing falsely to Allah, is not allowed, as it would lead to double punishment, no helper, as next verse says, and as Our way with messengers no change, as messengers made steadfast, not compromised, not taken as close friend via false attribution, but steadfast, as Lord encompasses people, knows best, etc., and as Prophet not keeper over them, etc., but warner, deliverer, steadfast, not lured, not inclined, otherwise double punishment. The believer who reflects will not be lured away from revelation, not attribute something else falsely to Allah for close friendship with disbelievers, but be steadfast, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding double punishment, no helper, Hell, as torment fearsome, but Lord Most Forbearing All-Forgiving to those who constantly turn, etc.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:74

> **Had We not made you steadfast, you probably would have inclined to them a little,**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Had We Not Made You Steadfast You Would Have Inclined Little**

Wa-lawlā an thabbatnāka la-qad kidta tarkanu ilayhim shay'an qalīlan — and had We not made you firm, steadfast, you would have almost inclined to them a little. Wa-lawlā, and had not, were it not that, had We not. An thabbatnāka from th-b-t, to make firm, steadfast, stable, from thabbata, to make firm, steadfast, stable, as thabbatnāka We made you firm, steadfast, stable, as Allah made Prophet steadfast, firm, stable, not lured, not inclined. La-qad kidta from k-w-d, you were about to, almost, nearly, as kidta you were about to, almost, as kādū they were about to, almost, as kidta you almost inclined. Tarkanu from r-k-n, to incline, lean, trust, rely, from rakana, to incline, lean, trust, rely, as tarkanu incline to them, lean towards them, trust them, rely on them. Ilayhim, to them, towards them, disbelievers, polytheists, who tried to lure away. Shay'an qalīlan, a little, little thing, small amount, from sh-y-ʾ, thing, and q-l-l, little, small, few, as little, small inclination, a little. Al-Ṭabarī explains that had We not made you steadfast, firm, stable, you, O Prophet, probably would have inclined to them a little, almost inclined to them a little, meaning you would have almost leaned towards them, compromised a little, due to their attempt to lure you away, tempt you away from what We revealed, hoping you would attribute something else falsely and taken as close friend, as previous verse, but Allah made you steadfast, firm, stable, so you did not incline, not even a little, as Prophet protected, steadfast.

**Why Steadfastness From Allah Prevented Little Inclination**

Why steadfastness from Allah prevented little inclination? Because disbelievers were about to lure Prophet away from revelation, tempt him to attribute something else falsely to Allah for close friendship, as previous verse says they were about to lure you away, hoping attribute something else falsely and taken as close friend, and had Allah not made him steadfast, firm, stable, he would have almost inclined to them a little, small inclination, due to human nature, ever hasty, etc., but Allah made steadfast, firm, stable, as Lord encompasses people, knows best, etc., as He made steadfast, as He is Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and as Our way with messengers no change, as messengers made steadfast, not inclined, etc., as divine law, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, etc., and as double punishment would have been if inclined, as next verse says, then dual torment in worldly life and after death, no supporter against Us, as warning, even for Prophet, double punishment if inclined, so steadfastness essential.

**Connection To Lure Away And Attribute Falsely And Double Punishment**

Verse 73 they were about to lure you away from what We revealed hoping attribute something else falsely and taken as close friend. Verse 74 had We not made you steadfast you would have inclined to them a little. Verse 75 then would have inflicted dual torment in worldly life and after death with no supporter against Us, showing sequence: they were about to lure away, hoping attribute falsely, taken as close friend, had not made steadfast would have inclined a little, then dual torment, no supporter, as warning, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses, knows best, etc., and as Our way with messengers no change, as messengers made steadfast.

**Link To Our Way With Messengers And Intimidate To Drive Out**

Verse 76 they were about to intimidate you to drive you out of land of Mecca, but then they would not have survived after you left except little while. Verse 77 this has been Our way with messengers We sent before you, you will never find any change in Our way, showing that luring away, inclining, intimidating to drive out are ways of disbelievers against messengers, but Our way with messengers is steadfast, no change, as messengers made steadfast, not lured, not inclined, not intimidated, etc., as divine law, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Lawla And Thabbatnaka And Kidta And Tarkanu And Shay'an Qalilan**

Lawlā, had not, were it not that, from l-w-l-ā, had not. Thabbatnāka from th-b-t, to make firm, steadfast, stable, from thabbata. Kidta from k-w-d, to be about to, almost, nearly, from kāda. Tarkanu from r-k-n, to incline, lean, trust, rely, from rakana. Ilayhim, to them. Shay' from sh-y-ʾ, thing. Qalīl from q-l-l, little, small, few. The verse's structure — had We not made you firm, you would have almost inclined to them a little — with condition had not made firm and result almost inclined a little, shows that steadfastness from Allah prevented even little inclination to disbelievers who tried to lure away, tempt, hoping attribute falsely, taken as close friend, but Allah made steadfast, firm, stable, so no inclination, not even little, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses, knows best, etc.

**How This Verse Teaches Steadfastness From Allah Prevents Inclination**

This verse teaches steadfastness from Allah prevents inclination to disbelievers: had We not made you steadfast, firm, stable, you, O Prophet, probably would have inclined to them, disbelievers who tried to lure away from revelation, hoping attribute something else falsely and taken as close friend, a little, small inclination, almost, as kādū, kidta, almost, about to, but Allah made steadfast, firm, stable, so no inclination, not even little, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses people, knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and as Our way with messengers no change, as messengers made steadfast, not inclined, etc., as divine law. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should seek steadfastness from Allah, be firm, stable, not incline to disbelievers, not be lured away from revelation, not attribute something else falsely to Allah for close friendship, but be steadfast, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding double punishment, no helper, as next verse says, dual torment in worldly life and after death, no supporter against Us, if inclined, even little, so seek steadfastness.

**A Reflection On Little Inclination And Steadfastness**

Shay'an qalīlan — a little, little thing, small amount — indicates that even little inclination to disbelievers, small leaning, trust, reliance, towards them, compromising a little, attributing something else falsely a little, would have led to double punishment, no helper, as next verse says, then We would have made you taste dual torment in worldly life and after death, no supporter against Us, as warning, even for Prophet, double punishment if inclined even little, so steadfastness from Allah essential, thabbatnāka, We made you firm, steadfast, stable, so no inclination, not even little, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses, knows best, etc., and as Our way with messengers no change, as messengers made steadfast. The believer who reflects will seek steadfastness from Allah, be firm, stable, not incline to disbelievers even little, not be lured away from revelation, not attribute falsely, but be steadfast, follow most upright, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding double punishment, no helper, Hell, torment fearsome, but Lord Most Forbearing All-Forgiving to those who constantly turn.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:75

> **and then We truly would have made you taste double ˹punishment˺ both in this life and after death, and you would have found no helper against Us.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Then We Would Have Made You Taste Double Punishment In This Life And After Death**

Idhan la-adhaqnāka ḍiʿfa al-ḥayāti wa-ḍiʿfa al-mamāti — then We would have made you taste double of life and double of death, double punishment in this life and after death. Idhan, then, in that case, then, if you had inclined to them a little, had We not made you steadfast. La-adhaqnāka from dh-w-q, to make taste, make experience, from adhaqa, to make taste, make experience, as adhaqnāka We would have made you taste, experience, as taste punishment, experience punishment. Ḍiʿfa from ḍ-ʿ-f, double, twofold, double, from ḍiʿf, double, twofold, as double, twofold, as double punishment. Al-ḥayāti from ḥ-y-y, life, this life, worldly life, from ḥayāh, life. Wa-ḍiʿfa al-mamāti, and double of death, double of after death, double punishment after death, from m-w-t, death, after death, Hereafter, as mamāt, death, after death, Hereafter. Al-Ṭabarī explains that then We truly would have made you taste double punishment both in this life and after death, meaning if you, O Prophet, had inclined to them, disbelievers who tried to lure you away from revelation, hoping attribute something else falsely and taken as close friend, a little, had We not made you steadfast, then We would have made you taste double punishment in this life, worldly, and double punishment after death, Hereafter, double, twofold, severe, as warning, even for Prophet, double punishment if inclined, even little, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses, knows best, etc.

**You Would Have Found No Helper Against Us**

Thumma lā tajidu laka ʿalaynā naṣīran — then you would not find for you against Us helper, supporter. Thumma, then, after double punishment. Lā tajidu from w-j-d, to find, you would not find, as lā tajidū you will not find guardian, avenger, etc. Laka, for you, for yourself. ʿAlaynā, against Us, upon Us, against Allah, Us. Naṣīran from n-ṣ-r, helper, supporter, from naṣara, to help, support, as naṣīr, helper, supporter, as manṣūr supported, and naṣīran helper, supporter, as no helper against Us, against Allah, if He punishes double in life and death, no helper, supporter, to protect, avenge, as only Allah has power, as those claimed besides Him have no power, as verse 56 says, no power, so no helper against Us.

**Why Double Punishment And No Helper If Inclined**

Why double punishment both in this life and after death and no helper against Us if inclined a little? Because luring away from revelation, attributing something else falsely to Allah for close friendship with disbelievers, inclining to them even little, is grave, serious, especially for Prophet, who is favoured, given revelation, wisdom, most upright, Night Journey, etc., so if he inclined, even little, double punishment, twofold, severe, in this life and after death, as warning, as even Prophet would have double punishment if inclined, so steadfastness essential, thabbatnāka, We made you firm, steadfast, so no inclination, not even little, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses, knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and no helper against Us, no supporter, if He punishes, as only He has power.

**Connection To Lure Away And Steadfast And Intimidate And Our Way No Change**

Verse 73 they were about to lure you away from what We revealed hoping attribute something else falsely and taken as close friend. Verse 74 had We not made you steadfast you would have inclined to them a little. Verse 75 then We would have made you taste double punishment in this life and after death and you would have found no helper against Us, showing sequence: luring away, hoping attribute falsely, taken as close friend, had not made steadfast would have inclined little, then double punishment in life and death, no helper against Us, as warning, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses, knows best, etc., and as Our way with messengers no change, as messengers made steadfast, not inclined, etc., as divine law, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, etc.

**Link To Our Way With Messengers And Intimidate To Drive Out Of Land**

Verse 76 they were about to intimidate you to drive you out of land of Mecca, but then they would not have survived after you left except little while. Verse 77 this has been Our way with messengers We sent before you, you will never find any change in Our way, showing that luring away, inclining, double punishment, no helper, intimidating to drive out, are ways of disbelievers against messengers, but Our way with messengers is steadfast, no change, as messengers made steadfast, not lured, not inclined, not intimidated, etc., as divine law, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, etc., and as double punishment would have been if inclined, but Prophet made steadfast, so no double punishment, no helper needed.

**Linguistic Note On Adhaqnaka And Di'f And Hayah And Mamat And Nasiran**

Idhan, then, in that case. Adhaqnāka from dh-w-q, to make taste, experience, from adhaqa. Ḍiʿf from ḍ-ʿ-f, double, twofold, from ḍiʿf. Ḥayāh from ḥ-y-y, life, from ḥayāh. Mamāt from m-w-t, death, after death, from māta, to die, from mamāt, death. Tajidu from w-j-d, to find. Naṣīr from n-ṣ-r, helper, supporter, from naṣara, to help, support. The verse's structure — then We would have made you taste double of life and double of death, then you would not find for you against Us helper — with double punishment in life and death and no helper against Us, shows severity if Prophet had inclined even little to disbelievers who tried to lure away, tempt, hoping attribute falsely, taken as close friend, but Allah made steadfast, so no double punishment, no helper needed, as Prophet protected, steadfast.

**How This Verse Teaches Double Punishment And No Helper If Inclined**

This verse teaches double punishment and no helper if inclined to disbelievers: then We truly would have made you taste double punishment both in this life, worldly, and after death, Hereafter, double, twofold, severe, and you would have found no helper, supporter, against Us, against Allah, if He punishes double, no helper, supporter, to protect, avenge, as only Allah has power, as those claimed besides Him have no power, as verse 56 says, no power, so be steadfast, not incline even little to disbelievers who try to lure away from revelation, hoping attribute something else falsely and taken as close friend, but be steadfast, firm, stable, as Allah made steadfast, thabbatnāka, We made you firm, so no inclination, not even little, as Prophet protected, steadfast, as Lord encompasses, knows best, etc., and as Our way with messengers no change, as messengers made steadfast. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should not incline to disbelievers even little, not be lured away from revelation, not attribute falsely, but be steadfast, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding double punishment in life and death, no helper against Us, Hell, torment fearsome, but Lord Most Forbearing All-Forgiving to those who constantly turn.

**A Reflection On Double And No Helper**

Ḍiʿfa al-ḥayāti wa-ḍiʿfa al-mamāti — double of life and double of death, double punishment in this life and after death — indicates severity, twofold, double, severe, both in worldly life and after death, Hereafter, double punishment, as warning, even for Prophet, double punishment if inclined even little, so steadfastness essential, thabbatnāka, We made you firm, steadfast, stable, so no double punishment. Lā tajidu laka ʿalaynā naṣīran — you would not find for you against Us helper, supporter — indicates no helper, supporter, against Allah if He punishes double, no guardian, no avenger, no helper, as only Allah has power, as those claimed besides Him have no power, as verse 56 says, no power to remove harm or transfer, so no helper against Us, so be steadfast, not incline, follow most upright, wisdom, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, avoiding double punishment, no helper, Hell, as torment fearsome, but Lord Most Forbearing All-Forgiving.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:76

> **They were about to intimidate you to drive you out of the land ˹of Mecca˺, but then they would not have survived after you ˹had left˺ except for a little while.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**They Were About To Intimidate You To Drive You Out Of Land**

Wa-in kādū la-yastafizzūnaka mina al-arḍi li-yukhrijūka minhā — and indeed they were about to intimidate, provoke you to drive you out from land. Wa-in, and indeed, indeed, surely, they were about to. Kādū from k-w-d, to be about to, almost, nearly, as kādū they were about to, almost, as previous kādū lure away, and kādū intimidate. La-yastafizzūnaka from f-z-z, to intimidate, provoke, make to slip, cause to leave, from istafazza, to intimidate, provoke, make to tremble, to cause to leave, as yastafizzūnaka intimidate you, provoke you, to drive you out, as Satan incite whoever you can with voice, istafiz, incite, entice, and yastafizzūnaka intimidate you, provoke you, to drive out. Mina al-arḍi, from land, from earth, from land of Mecca, as arḍ land, earth, as land of Mecca, as Mecca, as land. Li-yukhrijūka from kh-r-j, to drive out, expel, make exit, from akhraja, to drive out, expel, make exit, as yukhrijūka drive you out, expel you, from land, as they wanted to drive Prophet out of Mecca, intimidate to drive out. Minhā, from it, from land. Al-Ṭabarī explains that they, disbelievers, polytheists, were about to intimidate you, provoke you, to drive you out of land of Mecca, as they tried to intimidate Prophet to drive him out, expel him, as they intimidated, provoked, as they wanted to expel, as they did later, as Hijrah, etc., but Allah protected, as Lord encompasses people, knows best, etc., and as Our way with messengers no change, as messengers intimidated to drive out, etc.

**But Then They Would Not Have Survived After You Left Except Little While**

Wa-idhan lā yalbathūna khilāfaka illā qalīlan — and then they would not remain after you, behind you, except little while, short time. Wa-idhan, and then, in that case, then, if they drove you out. Lā yalbathūna from l-b-th, to remain, stay, tarry, as labitha, to remain, stay, as yalbathūna remain, stay, as labithtum remained, as remained only little while, as Day call thinking remained only little while. Khilāfaka from kh-l-f, after you, behind you, after you left, behind you, from khilāf, after, behind, after you, behind you, as after you left, behind you. Illā qalīlan, except little, only little while, short time, from q-l-l, little, small, few, as qalīlan little while, short time, as little while, as worldly life little while, as remained only little while. Al-Ṭabarī explains that then they would not have survived, remained after you left, behind you, except for little while, short time, meaning if they drove you out of land of Mecca, intimidated to drive out, they would not remain, survive after you left, behind you, except little while, short time, then would be destroyed, punished severely, as divine law, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., and as Our way with messengers, when messengers driven out, people destroyed after little while, as history, as Ad, Thamud, etc.

**Why Intimidate To Drive Out And Not Survive Except Little While**

Why intimidate to drive out of land and not survive except little while? Because disbelievers, polytheists, tried to intimidate Prophet to drive him out of Mecca, as they wanted to expel, as they intimidated, provoked, as they were about to, almost, kādū, almost, about to, as they tried to lure away, tempt, hoping attribute falsely, taken as close friend, and intimidate to drive out, as ways against messengers, but Our way with messengers, divine law, is that when messengers driven out, intimidated to drive out, people do not remain, survive after them, behind them, except little while, short time, then destroyed, punished severely, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, as many generations destroyed after Noah, etc., as history, as Ad, Thamud, etc., destroyed after messengers driven out, etc., and as Lord encompasses people, knows best, etc., and as Prophet not keeper over them, etc., but warner, deliverer, steadfast, not lured, not inclined, not intimidated, etc.

**Connection To Lure Away And Steadfast And Double Punishment And Our Way No Change**

Verses 73-75 they were about to lure you away from what We revealed hoping attribute something else falsely and taken as close friend, had We not made you steadfast you would have inclined a little, then double punishment in life and death, no helper against Us. Verse 76 they were about to intimidate you to drive you out of land of Mecca, but then they would not have survived after you left except little while. **Link To Our Way With Messengers And No Change And Prayer And Night Prayer**

**Linguistic Note On Kadu And Yastafizzunaka And Arḍ And Yukhrijuka And Yalbathuna And Khilafaka And Qalilan**

Kādū from k-w-d, to be about to, almost, nearly, from kāda. Yastafizzūnaka from f-z-z, to intimidate, provoke, make to slip, cause to leave, from istafazza. Arḍ from a-r-ḍ, land, earth, from arḍ. Yukhrijūka from kh-r-j, to drive out, expel, from akhraja. Yalbathūna from l-b-th, to remain, stay, tarry, from labitha. Khilāf from kh-l-f, after, behind, from khalafa, to be after, behind. Qalīl from q-l-l, little, small, few, from qalīl. The verse's structure — they were about to intimidate you to drive you out from land, and then they would not remain after you except little — with about to intimidate to drive out from land and then not remain after you except little, shows attempt to intimidate to drive out and consequence not remaining after you except little while, short time, then destroyed, as divine law, Our way with messengers no change.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:78

**Observe Prayer From Decline Of Sun Until Darkness Of Night**

Aqimi al-ṣalāta li-dulūki al-shamsi ilā ghasaqi al-layli — establish prayer from decline of sun until darkness of night. Aqimi from q-w-m, to establish, observe, keep up, from aqāma, to establish, observe, as aqimi establish prayer, observe prayer, as prayer, ṣalāh. Al-ṣalāta from ṣ-l-w, prayer, ṣalāh, formal prayer, as ṣalāh, prayer. Li-dulūki from d-l-k, decline, sinking, from dalaka, to decline, sink, rub, as dulūk, decline, sinking of sun, as sun declines from zenith, as decline of sun, as Dhuhr, Asr, etc., from decline of sun. Al-shamsi from sh-m-s, sun, from shams, sun, as sun, as night and day signs, sun and moon, etc. Ilā ghasaqi from gh-s-q, darkness, gloom, from ghasaq, darkness, gloom, darkness of night, as ghasaq al-layl, darkness of night, gloom of night, as darkness, as night, as layl night. Al-layli from l-y-l, night, as layl night, as night and day signs, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that observe prayer from decline of sun until darkness of night, meaning establish, observe prayers from decline, sinking of sun, Dhuhr, Asr, Maghrib, Isha, until darkness of night, gloom of night, as prayers from decline of sun until darkness, as Dhuhr, Asr, Maghrib, Isha, as time from decline of sun until darkness of night includes those prayers. Al-Qurṭubī says li-dulūki al-shamsi ilā ghasaqi al-layli means from decline of sun until darkness of night, including Dhuhr, Asr, Maghrib, Isha, as prayers in that period.

**And Dawn Prayer Certainly Dawn Prayer Witnessed By Angels**

Wa-qur'āna al-fajri inna qur'āna al-fajri kāna mashhūdan — and recitation of dawn, dawn prayer, indeed recitation of dawn is witnessed. Wa-qur'āna al-fajri from q-r-ʾ, recitation, Qur'an, recitation of dawn, dawn prayer, as Fajr prayer, dawn prayer, as recitation in Fajr, as Qur'an recited at dawn, as Fajr prayer. Kāna mashhūdan from sh-h-d, witnessed, attended, from shahida, to witness, attend, as mashhūd, witnessed, attended, as dawn prayer witnessed by angels, as angels witness, attend dawn prayer, as dawn prayer witnessed by angels, as angels of night and day witness, as hadith says angels of night and day gather at Fajr, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that and dawn prayer, recitation of dawn, indeed dawn prayer, recitation of dawn, is witnessed, attended, by angels, meaning angels witness, attend dawn prayer, Fajr, as dawn prayer witnessed by angels, as angels of night and day witness, as dawn prayer witnessed, attended, as important, as Fajr prayer witnessed.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:80

> **And say, “My Lord! Grant me an honourable entrance and an honourable exit and give me a supporting authority from Yourself.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Say My Lord Grant Me Honourable Entrance And Honourable Exit**

Wa-qul rabbi adkhilnī mudkhala ṣidqin wa-akhrijnī mukhraja ṣidqin — and say, My Lord, make me enter entrance of truth, sincerity, honourable entrance, and make me exit exit of truth, honourable exit. Wa-qul from q-w-l, to say, command to Prophet to say, supplicate. Rabbi from r-b-b, My Lord, My Lord, as rabbi My Lord. Adkhilnī from d-kh-l, to make enter, cause to enter, from adkhala, to make enter, cause to enter, as adkhilnī make me enter, grant me entrance. Mudkhala from d-kh-l, entrance, place of entrance, manner of entrance, from mudkhal, entrance, place of entrance, manner of entrance, as mudkhala ṣidqin entrance of truth, sincerity, honourable entrance, as entrance with truth, sincerity, honourable. Ṣidqin from ṣ-d-q, truth, sincerity, honesty, from ṣidq, truth, sincerity, as ṣidq, truth, sincerity, honourable, as entrance of truth, sincere, honourable. Wa-akhrijnī from kh-r-j, to make exit, cause to exit, from akhraja, to make exit, cause to exit, as akhrijnī make me exit, grant me exit. Mukhraja from kh-r-j, exit, place of exit, manner of exit, from mukhraj, exit, place of exit, manner of exit, as mukhraja ṣidqin exit of truth, sincerity, honourable exit, as exit with truth, sincerity, honourable. Ṣidqin, truth, sincerity, as truth, sincerity, honourable. Al-Ṭabarī explains that say, My Lord, grant me honourable entrance and honourable exit, meaning make me enter entrance of truth, sincerity, honourable entrance, and make me exit exit of truth, honourable exit, as supplication for honourable entrance and exit, truthful, sincere, honourable, as entrance and exit with truth, sincerity, honourable, as Prophet supplicates for honourable entrance and exit, as Hijrah, entrance to Medina, exit from Mecca, etc., as honourable entrance and exit, with truth, sincerity.

**Give Me Supporting Authority From Yourself**

Wa-ijʿal lī min ladunka sulṭānan naṣīran — and make for me from Yourself, from Your presence, authority, supporting, helper. Wa-ijʿal from j-ʿ-l, to make, grant, give, as jaʿalnā We made, etc., and ijʿal make, grant. Lī, for me, to me. Min ladunka, from Yourself, from Your presence, from near You, from ladun, presence, near, from ladunka from Yourself, from Your presence. Sulṭānan from s-l-ṭ, authority, power, proof, from sulṭān, authority, power, as sulṭānan authority, power, as supporting authority, authority that supports, helps. Naṣīran from n-ṣ-r, supporting, helper, helping, from naṣara, to help, support, as naṣīr helper, supporter, and naṣīran supporting, helper, helping, as supporting authority, authority that supports, helps, helper, supporting. Al-Ṭabarī explains that and give me, grant me from Yourself, from Your presence, supporting authority, authority, power, that supports, helps, helper, supporting, as supplication for supporting authority from Allah, from His presence, authority that supports, helps, as Prophet supplicates for supporting authority from Allah, to support, help, as he was about to be driven out of land, intimidated to drive out, etc., and needed supporting authority, as Our way with messengers no change, etc., and as prayer, night prayer, station of praise, honourable entrance and exit, supporting authority, etc.

Because Prophet was about to be intimidated to drive out of land of Mecca, as verse 76 says they were about to intimidate you to drive you out of land, but then they would not survive after you left except little while, and Our way with messengers no change, you will never find any change in Our way, as verse 77 says, and prayer from decline until darkness and dawn witnessed and night prayer additional station of praise, as verses 78-79 say, as part of Our way, divine law, no change, and now supplication for honourable entrance and exit and supporting authority from Yourself, as Prophet supplicates for honourable entrance, entrance of truth, sincere, honourable, and honourable exit, exit of truth, sincere, honourable, and supporting authority from Allah, from His presence, to support, help, as he was about to be driven out, needed honourable entrance to Medina, honourable exit from Mecca, and supporting authority from Allah, to support, help, as Hijrah, etc., and as truth come falsehood vanished, falsehood bound to vanish, as verse 81 says, etc.

Verse 77 Our way with messengers no change, you will never find any change in Our way. Verse 78 observe prayer from decline until darkness and dawn prayer witnessed. ## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:81

> **And declare, “The truth has come and falsehood has vanished. Indeed, falsehood is bound to vanish.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Truth Has Come And Falsehood Vanished**

Wa-qul jā'a al-ḥaqqu wa-zahaqa al-bāṭilu — and say, truth has come and falsehood has vanished, perished. Wa-qul from q-w-l, to say, declare, command to Prophet to declare. Jā'a from j-y-ʾ, to come, arrive, as jā'a truth has come. Al-ḥaqqu from ḥ-q-q, truth, true, right, reality, from ḥaqq, truth, as ḥaqq truth, as previous ḥaqqa decree becomes true, justified, etc., and ḥaqq truth. Wa-zahaqa from z-h-q, to vanish, perish, disappear, from zahaqa, to vanish, perish, disappear, as zahaqa falsehood vanished, perished. Al-bāṭilu from b-ṭ-l, falsehood, vanity, false, from bāṭil, falsehood, vanity, false, opposite of ḥaqq truth. Al-Ṭabarī explains that declare, truth has come and falsehood has vanished, perished, meaning truth, Islam, Qur'an, revelation, has come, arrived, and falsehood, disbelief, shirk, idolatry, has vanished, perished, disappeared, as truth came, falsehood vanished, as when Prophet entered Mecca, truth came, falsehood vanished, as idols fell, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says jā'a al-ḥaqqu wa-zahaqa al-bāṭilu means truth has come, Islam, Qur'an, and falsehood has vanished, perished, as truth came, falsehood vanished, as divine law, as truth vs falsehood.

**Indeed Falsehood Bound To Vanish**

Inna al-bāṭila kāna zahūqan — indeed falsehood is bound to vanish, ever vanishing, perishing. Inna, indeed, surely. Al-bāṭila, falsehood, vanity, false, from b-ṭ-l. Kāna, is, was, indicates it is. Zahūqan from z-h-q, bound to vanish, ever vanishing, perishing, vanishing, from zahaqa, to vanish, perish, from zahūq, bound to vanish, ever vanishing, perishing, as falsehood bound to vanish, ever vanishing, perishing, not stable, not lasting, as falsehood bound to vanish, as truth stable, lasting. Al-Ṭabarī explains that indeed falsehood is bound to vanish, ever vanishing, perishing, meaning falsehood is bound to vanish, ever vanishing, perishing, not stable, not lasting, as it vanishes, perishes, as truth remains, as divine law, as truth has come and falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, as falsehood ever vanishing, as truth ever remaining.

**Why Truth Come Falsehood Vanish And Bound To Vanish**

Why truth has come and falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish? Because honourable entrance and exit and supporting authority from Yourself, as previous verse supplication for honourable entrance and exit and supporting authority, as Hijrah, entrance to Medina, exit from Mecca, with truth, sincerity, honourable, and supporting authority from Allah, leads to truth coming, falsehood vanishing, as truth, Islam, Qur'an, revelation, has come, arrived, and falsehood, disbelief, shirk, idolatry, has vanished, perished, disappeared, as when Prophet entered Mecca, truth came, falsehood vanished, idols fell, etc., and indeed falsehood bound to vanish, ever vanishing, perishing, not stable, not lasting, as falsehood bound to vanish, as truth stable, lasting, as divine law, as Our way with messengers no change, you will never find any change in Our way, as truth come falsehood vanish is part of Our way, divine law, no change, as truth has come and falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, as truth vs falsehood.

Verse 81 declare truth has come and falsehood has vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, showing that honourable entrance and exit and supporting authority lead to truth coming, falsehood vanishing, as truth, Islam, Qur'an, has come, falsehood vanished, as when Prophet entered Mecca, truth came, falsehood vanished, idols fell, etc., and indeed falsehood bound to vanish, ever vanishing, as truth stable, lasting. Qul from q-w-l, to say, declare. Jā'a from j-y-ʾ, to come, arrive, from jā'a. Ḥaqq from ḥ-q-q, truth, true, right, reality, from ḥaqqa, to be true. ## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:82

> **We send down the Quran as a healing and mercy for the believers, but it only increases the wrongdoers in loss.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**We Send Down Quran As Healing And Mercy For Believers**

Wa-nunazzilu mina al-Qur'āni mā huwa shifā'un wa-raḥmatun li-l-mu'minīna — and We send down from Qur'an what is healing and mercy for believers. Wa-nunazzilu from n-z-l, to send down, reveal, gradually, as nunazzilu We send down, reveal, as We send down Quran, as Quran revealed in stages, successive revelations, as verse 106 Quran revealed in stages so you may recite to people at deliberate pace, sent down successive revelations, as nunazzilu send down, reveal. Mina al-Qur'āni, from Qur'an, from Qur'an, part of Qur'an, what is, as from Qur'an, as Qur'an, as healing and mercy. Mā huwa, what it is, what is, that which is. Shifā'un from sh-f-y, healing, cure, from shafā, to heal, cure, from shifā', healing, cure, as healing, cure for hearts, etc. Wa-raḥmatun from r-ḥ-m, mercy, as raḥmah mercy, as healing and mercy, as Qur'an healing and mercy for believers, as mercy, as ever Merciful, as Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, etc. Li-l-mu'minīna from a-m-n, for believers, for those who believe, as mu'minīn believers, as believers, as believers who do good mighty reward, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We send down Quran as healing and mercy for believers, meaning Qur'an, revelation, is healing, cure, for hearts, from ignorance, doubt, etc., and mercy, mercy for believers, as guidance, most upright, wisdom, etc., as healing and mercy for believers, as believers benefit, as Qur'an guides to most upright, gives good news to believers who do good mighty reward, etc., as healing and mercy.

**But It Only Increases Wrongdoers In Loss**

Wa-lā yazīdu al-ẓālimīna illā khasāran — and it does not increase wrongdoers except in loss, ruin. Wa-lā, and not, but. Yazīdu from z-y-d, to increase, add, as yazīdu increases, as previous yazīduhum increases them aversion, defiance, etc., and lā yazīdu does not increase, but increases wrongdoers except loss. Al-ẓālimīna from ẓ-l-m, wrongdoers, oppressors, unjust, from ẓālim, wrongdoer, oppressor, as ẓālimīn wrongdoers, as wrongdoers, as disbelievers, polytheists, etc., as wrongdoers. Illā khasāran from kh-s-r, loss, ruin, perdition, from khasira, to lose, be in loss, from khasār, loss, ruin, as loss, ruin, perdition, as wrongdoers increase in loss, ruin, as Qur'an increases wrongdoers in loss, as they have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, deny resurrection, etc., and varying signs drives farther away, nufūran, aversion, warning increases greatly in defiance, ṭughyānan kabīran, great defiance, and Qur'an increases wrongdoers in loss, as loss.

**Why Healing Mercy For Believers But Loss For Wrongdoers**

Why Qur'an healing and mercy for believers but only increases wrongdoers in loss? Because Qur'an guides to most upright, explains everything in detail, varies signs so they may be mindful, but it only drives them farther away for disbelievers, nufūran, aversion, as they have hidden barrier, veils over hearts unable to comprehend, deafness in ears, turning backs in aversion when Lord alone mentioned, saying bewitched man, calling names, far astray cannot find Right Way, denying resurrection bones and ashes new creation, etc., and human ever hasty, ever ungrateful, etc., and Satan has no authority over faithful servants, sufficient Lord as Guardian, but wrongdoers follow Satan, etc., and truth has come and falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, as truth vs falsehood, and Qur'an healing and mercy for believers, as truth, healing, mercy, but wrongdoers increase in loss, as falsehood, loss, as they disbelieve, deny, etc.

**Connection To Truth Come Falsehood Vanish And Human Response To Favours And Hardship**

Verse 81 truth has come and falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, truth vs falsehood. Verse 82 We send down Quran as healing and mercy for believers but only increases wrongdoers in loss, showing truth, Qur'an, healing mercy for believers, vs falsehood, wrongdoers loss, as truth vs falsehood, healing mercy vs loss, as Qur'an guides to most upright, gives good news to believers mighty reward, warns disbelievers painful punishment, as healing mercy vs loss. The next verse describes human nature when favoured turning away with arrogance and when hardship touches despairing, showing ever ungrateful nature, ever hasty, etc., and as truth come falsehood vanish, healing mercy vs loss, etc.

**Link To Healing And Mercy And Ever Merciful And Most Forbearing All-Forgiving**

Qur'an healing and mercy for believers, as healing, cure for hearts, mercy, as ever Merciful to you, as Lord steers ships for you through sea to seek bounty, ever Merciful, as verse 66 says, and as He is Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, ḥalīman ghafūran, verse 44, and as healing and mercy, as mercy, as ever Merciful, as Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and wrongdoers increase in loss, as loss, ruin, as painful punishment prepared, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Nunazzilu And Shifa' And Rahmah And Yazidu And Zalimina And Khasaran**

Nunazzilu from n-z-l, to send down, reveal, gradually, from nazzala, to send down. Qur'ān from q-r-ʾ, recitation, Qur'an, from qara'a. Shifā' from sh-f-y, healing, cure, from shafā, to heal. Raḥmah from r-ḥ-m, mercy, from raḥima. Mu'minīn from a-m-n, believers, from amina. Yazīdu from z-y-d, to increase, add, from zāda. Ẓālimīn from ẓ-l-m, wrongdoers, oppressors, from ẓalama. Khasār from kh-s-r, loss, ruin, perdition, from khasira, to lose. The verse's structure — We send down from Qur'an what is healing and mercy for believers, but it does not increase wrongdoers except loss — with sending down Qur'an as healing mercy for believers and increasing wrongdoers except loss, shows dual effect: healing mercy for believers, loss for wrongdoers, as truth vs falsehood, as Qur'an guides to most upright, gives good news mighty reward, warns painful punishment, as healing mercy vs loss.

**How This Verse Teaches Quran Healing Mercy For Believers Loss For Wrongdoers**

This verse teaches Quran as healing and mercy for believers but loss for wrongdoers: We send down, reveal Quran as healing, cure, and mercy for believers, for those who believe, as healing for hearts from ignorance, doubt, etc., and mercy, as guidance, most upright, wisdom, etc., as healing and mercy, as believers benefit, follow most upright, mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, but it only increases wrongdoers, disbelievers, polytheists, etc., in loss, ruin, perdition, as they have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, deny resurrection, etc., and varying signs drives farther away, nufūran, aversion, warning increases greatly in defiance, ṭughyānan kabīran, great defiance, and Qur'an increases wrongdoers in loss, as loss, ruin, as painful punishment prepared, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should see Qur'an as healing and mercy, benefit, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, healing, mercy, while wrongdoers increase in loss, ruin, as they disbelieve, deny, have barrier, etc., so avoid being among wrongdoers in loss, be among believers healing mercy.

**A Reflection On Healing And Mercy Vs Loss**

Shifā'un wa-raḥmatun li-l-mu'minīna — healing and mercy for believers — indicates Qur'an, revelation, truth, is healing, cure, for hearts, from ignorance, doubt, disease, and mercy, mercy for believers, as guidance, most upright, wisdom, etc., as healing and mercy, as ever Merciful to you, as Lord steers ships for you through sea to seek bounty, ever Merciful, as He is Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and healing mercy for believers leads to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, station of praise, honourable entrance and exit, supporting authority, truth come falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, etc., as truth, healing mercy. ## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:83

> **When We grant people Our favours, they turn away, acting arrogantly. But when touched with evil, they lose all hope.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**When Grant People Favours They Turn Away Arrogantly**

Wa-idhā anʿamnā ʿalā al-insāni aʿraḍa wa-na'ā bi-jānibihi — and when We grant favours, bestow blessings upon human, he turns away and goes far, with side, acting arrogantly. Wa-idhā, and when. Anʿamnā from n-ʿ-m, to grant favours, bestow blessings, favour, from anʿama, to grant favours, bestow blessings, as anʿamnā We grant favours, bestow blessings, as favours, blessings, as bounty never withheld, some favoured over others, provision abundant or limited, etc., and anʿamnā grant favours. ʿAlā al-insāni, upon human, to human, as insān human, ever hasty, ever ungrateful, etc., as human, as insān. Aʿraḍa from ʿ-r-ḍ, to turn away, avoid, as aʿraḍtum you turned away, as turn away, avoid, as when delivered to shore you turned away, etc., and aʿraḍa turns away, avoids, as turning away from gratitude, from Allah, etc. Wa-na'ā from n-ʾ-y, to go far, keep far, distance, from na'ā, to go far, keep far, to be distant, as na'ā bi-jānibihi, goes far with side, with side, acting arrogantly, turning side, as arrogance, as going far with side, side, as arrogant, as turning away acting arrogantly, going far with side, side, as arrogance, as previous do not walk arrogantly, maraḥan, arrogantly, and become extremely arrogant, ʿuluwwan kabīran, great arrogance, and na'ā bi-jānibihi acting arrogantly, going far with side, as arrogance. Al-Ṭabarī explains that when We grant people, human, Our favours, blessings, bounty, provision, etc., they turn away, avoid, turn away from gratitude, from Allah, and go far with side, acting arrogantly, turning side, going far, distant, arrogantly, as human nature, ever ungrateful, ever hasty, etc., as human turns away arrogantly when favoured, as ungratefulness, as Devil ever ungrateful, etc.

**When Touched With Evil They Lose All Hope**

Wa-idhā massahu al-sharru kāna ya'ūsan — and when evil touches him, he is despairing, loses all hope. Wa-idhā, and when. Massahu from m-s-s, to touch, afflict, befall, as massakumu touches you, hardship touches you at sea, etc., and massahu evil touches him, afflicts him. Al-sharru from sh-r-r, evil, bad, adversity, harm, as sharr evil, as opposite of khayr good, as evil, bad, adversity, harm, as evil touches, afflicts, as hardship, etc. Kāna ya'ūsan from y-ʾ-s, despairing, losing hope, despondent, from ya'isa, to despair, lose hope, from ya'ūs, despairing, losing hope, despondent, as ya'ūsan despairing, losing all hope, despondent, as when evil touches, he despairs, loses hope, as human nature, ever ungrateful, ever hasty, etc., as when touched with evil loses hope, as human despairs, as previous when grant favours turn away arrogantly, when evil touched lose hope, as two extremes, arrogance when favoured, despair when evil touched, as human nature.

**Why Turn Away Arrogantly When Favoured And Despair When Evil Touched**

Why turn away arrogantly when favoured and despair when evil touched? Because human is ever hasty, ʿajūlan, ever hasty, prays for evil as for good, ever hasty, and ever ungrateful, kafūran, ever ungrateful, as humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran, as Devil ever ungrateful, kafūran, and human acts in own way, everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best whose way rightly guided, as verse 84 says, and human nature, when favoured, turns away arrogantly, goes far with side, acting arrogantly, as arrogance, as extremely arrogant, as do not walk arrogantly, etc., and when evil touched, hardship, adversity, despairs, loses all hope, despondent, as human despairs, as previous chapter said when grant favours turn away, when evil touched despair, etc., as human nature, ever hasty, ever ungrateful, etc., and as Qur'an healing mercy for believers but increases wrongdoers in loss, as truth vs falsehood, etc., and as truth come falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, etc.

**Connection To Truth Come Falsehood Vanish And Quran Healing Mercy And Everyone Acts In Own Way**

Verse 81 truth has come and falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, truth vs falsehood. Verse 82 We send down Quran as healing and mercy for believers but only increases wrongdoers in loss, healing mercy vs loss, truth vs falsehood. Verse 83 when We grant people favours they turn away acting arrogantly, but when touched with evil they lose all hope, showing human nature, ever ungrateful, turning away arrogantly when favoured, losing hope when evil touched, as human nature, ever hasty, ever ungrateful, as insān, and as truth come falsehood vanish, healing mercy vs loss, etc., and verse 84 everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best whose way rightly guided, showing that human acts in own way, some turn away arrogantly when favoured, despair when evil touched, some grateful, patient, etc., and Lord knows best whose way rightly guided, as He knows best what is within selves, etc.

**Link To Lord Steers Ships Ever Merciful And Forget Gods At Sea Turn Away At Shore Ever Ungrateful And Warnings**

Verse 66 Lord steers ships for you through sea to seek bounty, ever Merciful. Verse 67 hardship at sea forget all gods except Him, delivers to shore turn away, humankind ever ungrateful, kafūran. Verse 83 when grant favours turn away acting arrogantly, when evil touched lose all hope, ya'ūsan, despairing, showing human nature, ever ungrateful, turning away arrogantly when favoured, despairing when evil touched, as similar to sea hardship and shore turning away, as human nature, ever ungrateful, ever hasty, etc., and as warnings land swallow, storm stones, no guardian, sea return violent storm drowning for denial, no avenger, etc., all warnings for ungratefulness, arrogance, despair, etc.

**Linguistic Note On An'amna And A'rada And Na'a Bi-janibihi And Massahu And Sharr And Ya'usan**

Anʿamnā from n-ʿ-m, to grant favours, bestow blessings, from anʿama. Insān from a-n-s, human, from insān. Aʿraḍa from ʿ-r-ḍ, to turn away, avoid, from aʿraḍa. Na'ā from n-ʾ-y, to go far, keep far, distance, from na'ā. Jānib from j-n-b, side, from jānib. Massahu from m-s-s, to touch, afflict, from massa. Sharr from sh-r-r, evil, bad, adversity, harm, from sharr. Ya'ūs from y-ʾ-s, despairing, losing hope, from ya'isa, to despair. The verse's structure — when We grant favours upon human he turns away and goes far with side, and when evil touches him he is despairing — with favours granted turning away arrogantly going far with side and evil touched despairing losing hope, shows human nature, ever ungrateful, turning away arrogantly when favoured, despairing when evil touched, two extremes, arrogance when favoured, despair when evil, as human nature, ever hasty, ever ungrateful.

**How This Verse Teaches Human Nature Turning Away Arrogantly And Despairing**

This verse teaches human nature, turning away arrogantly when favoured and despairing when evil touched: when We grant people, human, Our favours, blessings, bounty, provision, etc., they turn away, avoid, turn away from gratitude, from Allah, and go far with side, acting arrogantly, turning side, going far, distant, arrogantly, as arrogance, as extremely arrogant, as do not walk arrogantly, etc., but when touched with evil, hardship, adversity, harm, they lose all hope, despair, despondent, ya'ūsan, despairing, losing hope, as human nature, ever ungrateful, ever hasty, etc., as human turns away arrogantly when favoured, despairs when evil touched, two extremes, not moderate, not grateful, patient, as most upright guidance teaches gratitude when favoured, patience when evil touched, not arrogance, despair. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should be grateful when favoured, not turn away arrogantly, go far with side, and patient when evil touched, not despair, lose hope, but hope, trust, as most upright, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, vs wrongdoers loss, etc.

**A Reflection On Arrogance And Despair As Extremes**

Aʿraḍa wa-na'ā bi-jānibihi — turns away and goes far with side, acting arrogantly — indicates arrogance when favoured, turning away from gratitude, going far with side, side, as arrogant, as extremely arrogant, as do not walk arrogantly, maraḥan, arrogantly, you cannot crack earth nor reach mountains height, but human arrogant when favoured, turns away, goes far with side. Ya'ūsan — despairing, losing all hope, despondent — indicates despair when evil touched, losing hope, despondent, as human despairs when evil touched, loses hope, as human ever ungrateful, ever hasty, etc. Both arrogance when favoured and despair when evil touched are extremes, not most upright, not wisdom, most upright is gratitude when favoured, patience when evil touched, moderate, as most upright guidance teaches, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, vs wrongdoers loss, arrogance, despair, as human nature, but believer avoids extremes, follows most upright.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:84

> **Say, ˹O Prophet,˺ “Everyone acts in their own way. But your Lord knows best whose way is rightly guided.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Everyone Acts In Own Way**

Qul kullun yaʿmalu ʿalā shākilatihi — say, everyone acts, works according to his way, likeness, nature. Qul from q-w-l, to say, command to Prophet. Kullun, everyone, each one, each person, from k-l-l, all, every, everyone. Yaʿmalu from ʿ-m-l, to act, do, work, from ʿamila, to act, do, work, as yaʿmalu acts, works, does, as everyone acts, works. ʿAlā shākilatihi from sh-k-l, way, likeness, nature, manner, form, from shākilah, way, likeness, nature, manner, form, as shākilatihi his way, his likeness, his nature, his manner, his form, as everyone acts according to his own way, nature, manner, as human nature, when favoured turns away arrogantly, when evil touched despairs, etc., as everyone acts in own way, according to own nature, manner, as disbelievers act in own way, believers act in own way, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that say, everyone acts in their own way, according to his way, likeness, nature, manner, meaning everyone acts, works according to his own way, nature, manner, as human acts according to own nature, as disbelievers act according to disbelief, arrogance, ungratefulness, etc., believers act according to belief, gratitude, patience, etc., as everyone acts in own way.

**Lord Knows Best Whose Way Rightly Guided**

Fa-rabbukum aʿlamu bi-man huwa ahdā sabīlan — then your Lord knows best who is more guided way, rightly guided. Fa-rabbukum, then your Lord, your Lord, from r-b-b. Aʿlamu, knows best, more knowing, from ʿ-l-m, to know, as aʿlamu knows best, as previous Lord knows best what is within selves, how they listen, you best, those in heavens and earth, etc., and aʿlamu knows best. Bi-man huwa ahdā, with who is more guided, who is rightly guided, more rightly guided, from h-d-y, to guide, be guided, from hadā, to guide, and ahdā, more guided, rightly guided, more rightly guided. Sabīlan from s-b-l, way, path, as sabīl way, path, Right Way, as rightly guided way, Right Way. Al-Ṭabarī explains that your Lord knows best whose way is rightly guided, more rightly guided, meaning Lord knows best who is more guided, rightly guided, whose way, shākilah, is rightly guided, more rightly guided, as He knows best what is within selves, knows best those in heavens and earth, etc., knows best whose way rightly guided, as He is All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best.

**Why Everyone Acts In Own Way And Lord Knows Best Rightly Guided**

Why everyone acts in own way and Lord knows best whose way rightly guided? Because when grant favours human turns away arrogantly, goes far with side, when evil touched despairs, loses hope, as human nature, ever ungrateful, ever hasty, etc., and everyone acts in own way, according to own nature, manner, as disbelievers act in own way, arrogance, ungratefulness, despair, etc., believers act in own way, gratitude, patience, etc., and Lord knows best whose way, shākilah, is rightly guided, more rightly guided, as He knows best what is within selves, knows best those in heavens and earth, etc., knows best whose way rightly guided, as He is All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best, as He knows best how they listen, etc., and as Qur'an guides to most upright, gives good news to believers mighty reward, warns disbelievers painful punishment, as two ways, etc., and as truth come falsehood vanished, healing mercy vs loss, etc., and as everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best rightly guided.

**Connection To When Grant Favours Turn Away Arrogantly Despair When Evil Touched And Quran Healing Mercy**

Verse 83 when grant favours human turns away acting arrogantly, goes far with side, when evil touched despairs, loses hope, ya'ūsan, human nature, ever ungrateful, ever hasty. Verse 84 everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best whose way rightly guided, showing that turning away arrogantly when favoured and despairing when evil touched are ways, shākilah, some act in that way, own way, but Lord knows best whose way rightly guided, more rightly guided, as He knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, etc., and as Qur'an healing mercy for believers but increases wrongdoers in loss, as two ways, healing mercy vs loss, etc., and as truth come falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, as truth vs falsehood, etc., and as everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best rightly guided, as He knows best.

**Link To Lord Knows Best What Is Within Selves And Those In Heavens Earth And How They Listen**

Verse 25 Lord knows best what is within selves, if righteous All-Forgiving to constantly turning. Verse 47 We know best how they listen and what they say privately, when wrongdoers say you follow only bewitched man. Verse 54 Lord knows you best, may have mercy or punish if wills, not sent you as keeper. Verse 55 Lord knows best those in heavens and earth, favoured some prophets above others, to David gave Psalms. Verse 84 Lord knows best whose way rightly guided, showing knowledge theme: Lord knows best what is within selves, how they listen, you best, those in heavens and earth, whose way rightly guided, as He knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best, as He knows best, etc., and as everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best rightly guided.

**Linguistic Note On Kullun And Ya'malu And Shakilatihi And A'lamu And Ahda Sabilan**

Kull from k-l-l, everyone, each, all, from kull. Yaʿmalu from ʿ-m-l, to act, do, work, from ʿamila. Shākilah from sh-k-l, way, likeness, nature, manner, form, from shakala, to be similar, to form, as shākilah way, likeness, nature. Rabb from r-b-b, Lord. Aʿlam from ʿ-l-m, knows best, more knowing. Man huwa, who he is, who is. Ahdā from h-d-y, more guided, rightly guided, from hadā, to guide. Sabīl from s-b-l, way, path, Right Way. The verse's structure — say everyone acts according to his way, then your Lord knows best who is more guided way — with everyone acts in own way and Lord knows best whose way rightly guided, shows that everyone acts according to own nature, manner, but Lord knows best who is rightly guided, more guided, as He knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, as He knows best what is within selves, etc.

**How This Verse Teaches Everyone Acts In Own Way And Lord Knows Best Rightly Guided**

This verse teaches everyone acts in own way, according to own nature, manner, likeness, and Lord knows best whose way is rightly guided, more rightly guided, more guided way, Right Way. Al-Qurṭubī says everyone acts, works according to his own way, shākilah, nature, manner, likeness, as disbelievers act in own way, arrogance, ungratefulness, despair, etc., believers act in own way, gratitude, patience, etc., and Lord knows best whose way, shākilah, is rightly guided, more rightly guided, as He knows best what is within selves, knows best those in heavens and earth, etc., knows best whose way rightly guided, as He is All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best. The practical lesson is to act in rightly guided way, most upright way, that Qur'an guides to, wisdom, that is part of wisdom revealed, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, etc., and know Lord knows best whose way rightly guided, so seek rightly guided way, most upright, not own way that is arrogance, despair, ungratefulness, etc., but rightly guided way, most upright, that Lord knows best.

**A Reflection On Way And Rightly Guided**

Shākilatihi — his way, likeness, nature, manner, form — indicates everyone has own way, nature, manner, according to which he acts, works, as human nature, when favoured turns away arrogantly, when evil touched despairs, etc., as everyone acts in own way, own nature. Ahdā sabīlan — more guided way, rightly guided way, more rightly guided way — indicates that among ways, some more guided, rightly guided, Right Way, more rightly guided, as Lord knows best who is more guided way, rightly guided, as He knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best what is within selves, etc., and as Qur'an guides to most upright, most upright way is rightly guided, more guided, as truth come falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, as truth, most upright, rightly guided, vs falsehood, astray, far astray cannot find Right Way, blind in world blind in Hereafter far more astray, etc. The believer who acts in rightly guided way, most upright way, that Qur'an guides to, wisdom, will be among those Lord knows best as rightly guided, more guided way, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, station of praise, honourable entrance and exit, supporting authority, truth come falsehood vanished, etc., while disbeliever acts in own way that is arrogance, despair, ungratefulness, far astray cannot find Right Way, blind, etc., painful punishment, loss, etc.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:85

> **They ask you ˹O Prophet˺ about the spirit. Say, “Its nature is known only to my Lord, and you ˹O humanity˺ have been given but little knowledge.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**They Ask You About Spirit**

Wa-yas'alūnaka ʿani al-rūḥi — and they ask you about spirit, soul. Wa-yas'alūnaka from s-ʾ-l, to ask, question, as yas'alūnaka they ask you, O Prophet, about spirit, as they ask about spirit, as question. ʿAni al-rūḥi from r-w-ḥ, about spirit, soul, spirit, from rūḥ, spirit, soul, as spirit, soul, as rūḥ, spirit, as spirit, soul, as human spirit, etc., as they ask about rūḥ, spirit, what is spirit, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that they, disbelievers, polytheists, Jews, etc., ask you, O Prophet, about spirit, soul, what is spirit, nature of spirit, etc., as question, test, as they asked about spirit, as they asked about Dhul-Qarnayn, etc., as questions. Al-Qurṭubī says yas'alūnaka ʿani al-rūḥi means they ask you about spirit, soul, what is spirit, nature, etc., as question about spirit, soul, as human spirit, etc.

**Say Its Nature Known Only To My Lord**

Qul al-rūḥu min amri rabbī — say, spirit is from command, affair of my Lord. Qul from q-w-l, to say, answer, command to Prophet to say, answer. Al-rūḥu, spirit, soul, as rūḥ, spirit, soul. Min amri from a-m-r, from command, affair, matter, from amr, command, affair, matter, as from command, affair of my Lord, as spirit from command of Lord, affair of Lord, matter of Lord, as spirit is from command of Lord, not known except to Lord, as its nature known only to Lord. Rabbī, my Lord, from r-b-b, my Lord, as my Lord, as Lord, as Lord knows best, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that say, spirit is from command, affair of my Lord, meaning spirit, soul, is from command, affair, matter of my Lord, from His command, affair, not known except to Him, its nature known only to Lord, as its nature known only to my Lord, as spirit from command of Lord, as affair of Lord, as matter of Lord, as spirit is from amr, command, of Lord, as creation, etc.

**You Have Been Given But Little Knowledge**

Wa-mā ūtītum mina al-ʿilmi illā qalīlan — and you have not been given from knowledge except little, small. Wa-mā, and not, you have not been given. Ūtītum from a-t-y, to be given, given, from ātā, to give, as ūtītum you have been given, you were given, as you have been given from knowledge. Mina al-ʿilmi, from knowledge, from knowledge, as ʿilm knowledge, as knowledge, as sure knowledge, etc., as knowledge, as you have been given but little knowledge, small knowledge, as human knowledge little, small, compared to Lord's knowledge, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best what is within selves, etc., and human knowledge little. Illā qalīlan, except little, only little, small, from q-l-l, little, small, few, as qalīlan little, small, as little knowledge, small knowledge, as you have been given but little knowledge, small knowledge, as human knowledge little, limited, compared to Lord's knowledge, infinite, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that you, O humanity, have been given but little knowledge, small knowledge, meaning human knowledge is little, small, limited, compared to Allah's knowledge, which is vast, infinite, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best, etc., and spirit from command of Lord, nature known only to Lord, you have little knowledge, so do not ask about spirit beyond little knowledge.

**Why Spirit From Command Of Lord And Little Knowledge**

Why spirit is from command of Lord and you have been given but little knowledge? Because they asked about spirit, soul, what is spirit, nature, etc., as question, test, as they asked, and answer: spirit is from command, affair of my Lord, its nature known only to my Lord, and you have been given but little knowledge, small knowledge, limited, compared to Lord's knowledge, vast, infinite, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best what is within selves, how they listen, you best, those in heavens and earth, whose way rightly guided, etc., knows best, and spirit from command of Lord, nature known only to Lord, you have little knowledge, so do not go beyond little knowledge into what is not given, as human knowledge little, limited, as everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best rightly guided, etc., and as Qur'an guides to most upright, explains everything in detail, but spirit from command of Lord, little knowledge given, as part of wisdom, etc.

**Connection To Everyone Acts In Own Way And Lord Knows Best Rightly Guided And If We Willed We Could Take Away What We Revealed**

Verse 84 everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best whose way rightly guided. Verse 85 they ask about spirit, say spirit from command of Lord, you have been given but little knowledge, showing that knowledge little, limited, Lord knows best, etc., and everyone acts in own way, Lord knows best rightly guided, as knowledge little, but Lord knows best, etc. Verse 86-87 if We willed We could have taken away what We revealed to you, then you would find none to guarantee its return from Us, had it not been for mercy of your Lord, indeed His favour upon you immense, showing that revelation, Qur'an, is from Lord, favour immense, mercy, not to be taken away, as spirit from command of Lord, little knowledge given, but revelation, Qur'an, favour immense, mercy, etc.

**Link To Quran Healing Mercy And Truth Come Falsehood Vanish And Human And Jinn Cannot Produce Equal**

Verse 82 Quran healing mercy for believers but increases wrongdoers in loss, verse 81 truth come falsehood vanish, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, verse 88 if all humans and jinn came together to produce equivalent of Quran they could not produce equal, no matter how they supported each other, showing Quran's miraculous nature, inimitability, as healing mercy, truth, vs falsehood bound to vanish, and humans and jinn cannot produce equal, even if supported each other, as Quran inimitable, as revelation, favour immense, mercy, etc., and as spirit from command of Lord, little knowledge given, but Quran, revelation, favour immense, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Rūḥ And Amr And Utiītum And Ilm And Qalilan**

Yas'alūnaka from s-ʾ-l, to ask, question, from sa'ala. Rūḥ from r-w-ḥ, spirit, soul, from rūḥ. Amr from a-m-r, command, affair, matter, from amr. Rabb from r-b-b, Lord. Ūtītum from a-t-y, to be given, from ātā. ʿIlm from ʿ-l-m, knowledge, from ʿalima, to know. Qalīl from q-l-l, little, small, few, from qalīl. The verse's structure — they ask you about spirit, say spirit is from command of my Lord, and you have not been given from knowledge except little — with question about spirit and answer spirit from command of Lord and statement you have been given but little knowledge, shows that spirit from command of Lord, nature known only to Lord, human knowledge little, limited, compared to Lord's knowledge vast, infinite, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, etc.

**How This Verse Teaches Spirit From Command Of Lord And Little Knowledge**

This verse teaches spirit from command of Lord and little knowledge given to humanity: they ask you, O Prophet, about spirit, soul, what is spirit, nature, etc., say, spirit is from command, affair, matter of my Lord, its nature known only to my Lord, from His command, affair, and you, O humanity, have been given but little knowledge, small knowledge, limited, compared to Lord's knowledge, vast, infinite, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best what is within selves, how they listen, you best, those in heavens and earth, whose way rightly guided, etc., knows best, and spirit from command of Lord, nature known only to Lord, you have little knowledge, so do not ask beyond little knowledge, as human knowledge little. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should know spirit from command of Lord, nature known only to Lord, and human knowledge little, small, limited, compared to Allah's knowledge, vast, infinite, so be humble, not arrogant, not follow without sure knowledge, hearing, sight, heart accountable, etc., and seek knowledge, but know little knowledge given, as part of wisdom, most upright, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, etc.

**A Reflection On Little Knowledge And Spirit From Command**

Mā ūtītum mina al-ʿilmi illā qalīlan — you have not been given from knowledge except little, small — indicates human knowledge little, small, limited, compared to Allah's knowledge, vast, infinite, as Lord knows best, All-Aware All-Seeing, knows best what is within selves, etc., and spirit, rūḥ, from command of Lord, amr, command, affair, matter, of Lord, nature known only to Lord, you have little knowledge, so humble, not arrogant, not walk arrogantly, lower wing of humility, etc., and seek knowledge, but know little, as part of most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, vs wrongdoers loss, etc. The believer who knows spirit from command of Lord, you have little knowledge, will be humble, not claim great knowledge, will seek sure knowledge, not follow without knowledge, hearing, sight, heart accountable, will follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, etc., while disbelievers have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, etc., painful punishment prepared.
