## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:86

> **If We willed, We could have certainly taken away what We have revealed to you ˹O Prophet˺—then you would find none to guarantee its return from Us—**

**Expanded Commentary**

**If We Willed We Could Have Taken Away What We Revealed To You**

Wa-la-in shi'nā la-nadhhabanna bi-alladhī awḥaynā ilayka — and if We willed, We would certainly take away, go away with what We revealed to you. Wa-la-in, and if, and indeed if, if We willed, conditional with emphasis, if We willed. Shi'nā from sh-y-ʾ, to will, wish, if We willed, wished, from shā'a, to will. La-nadhhabanna from dh-h-b, to take away, go away with, remove, from adhhaba, to take away, remove, go away with, with emphasis, certainly take away, with nun emphasis, la-nadhhabanna certainly take away, remove. Bi-alladhī, with what, what, that which. Awḥaynā from w-ḥ-y, We revealed, inspired, as awḥā revealed, as awḥaynā We revealed to you, O Prophet, as revelation, Qur'an, wisdom, most upright, as this is part of wisdom revealed to you, as verse 39 says, and as spirit from command of Lord, etc., and as what We revealed to you, Qur'an, revelation, as favour immense, mercy, etc. Ilayka, to you, O Prophet. Al-Ṭabarī explains that if We willed, We could have certainly taken away, removed, gone away with what We revealed to you, O Prophet, Qur'an, revelation, wisdom, most upright, meaning Allah able to take away revelation, Qur'an, if He willed, remove it, take it away, as He is All-Powerful, All-Aware All-Seeing, etc., as He gives abundant or limited provisions, etc., and as He is Most Forbearing All-Forgiving, but torment fearsome, and as He could take away revelation if willed, as warning, as favour, mercy, not to be taken for granted.

**Then You Would Find None To Guarantee Its Return From Us**

Thumma lā tajidu laka bihi ʿalaynā wakīlan — then you would not find for you with it, about it, against Us, guardian, guarantor, keeper. Thumma, then, after taking away. Lā tajidu from w-j-d, to find, you would not find, you would find not. Laka, for you, for yourself. Bihi, with it, about it, concerning it, revelation, Qur'an. ʿAlaynā, against Us, upon Us, against Allah, Us. **Expanded Commentary**

**Had It Not Been For Mercy Of Your Lord**

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:88

**If Humans And Jinn Came Together To Produce Equivalent Of Quran Could Not Produce Equal**

Qul la-in ijtamaʿati al-insu wa-al-jinnu ʿalā an ya'tū bi-mithli hādhā al-Qur'āni lā ya'tūna bi-mithlihi — say, if humans and jinn gathered to bring like this Quran, they would not bring like it. Qul from q-w-l, to say, command to Prophet. La-in, if, indeed if, if, conditional with emphasis, if humans and jinn gathered. Ijtamaʿati from j-m-ʿ, to gather, come together, assemble, from ijtamaʿa, to gather, come together, as ijtamaʿati gathered, came together, assembled, as humans and jinn gathered, came together, assembled, to produce equivalent of Quran. Al-insu from a-n-s, humans, humankind, as ins, humans, as insān human, ever hasty, ever ungrateful, etc., as humans, humankind. Wa-al-jinnu from j-n-n, jinn, hidden beings, jinn, as jinn, as jinn, as jinn, hidden, as jinn, as jinn, as humans and jinn, both, all, to produce equivalent of Quran. ʿAlā an ya'tū, to bring, produce, come with, to produce, bring, from a-t-y, to bring, produce, come with, as ya'tū bring, produce, come with, as produce equivalent of Quran. Bi-mithli from m-th-l, like, equivalent, similar, from mithl, like, equivalent, similar, as mithli hādhā al-Qur'āni, like this Quran, equivalent of this Quran, similar to this Quran, as equivalent, like, similar. Hādhā al-Qur'āni, this Quran, this recitation, this Qur'an, as this Quran, as Quran, revelation, wisdom, most upright, healing mercy, truth, etc., as this Quran, as inimitable, miraculous. Lā ya'tūna bi-mithlihi, they would not bring like it, they could not produce its equal, they would not bring equivalent, similar, like it, from a-t-y, to bring, produce, and mithl, like, equivalent, similar, as they would not bring like it, could not produce equal, as inimitable, miraculous, as Quran inimitable, cannot be produced equal, even if humans and jinn gathered, came together, to produce equivalent.

**No Matter How They Supported Each Other**

Wa-law kāna baʿḍuhum li-baʿḍin ẓahīran — even if some of them were for some supporters, helpers, backers. Wa-law, even if, and if, even if. Kāna, were, was, even if some were. Baʿḍuhum, some of them, some of humans and jinn, some of them. Li-baʿḍin, for some, to some, for others, to others, as some for others, some to others, as supporters, helpers, for each other. Ẓahīran from ẓ-h-r, supporter, helper, backer, from ẓahīr, supporter, helper, backer, from ẓahara, to support, help, back, as ẓahīran supporter, helper, backer, as even if some were supporters for others, helpers, backers, for each other, to produce equivalent of Quran, they could not produce equal, as Quran inimitable, miraculous, even if supported each other, helped each other, backed each other, humans and jinn, all, to produce equivalent, they could not.

**Why Humans And Jinn Cannot Produce Equal Even If Supported Each Other**

Because Quran is revelation, from Lord, favour immense, mercy, healing mercy for believers, truth has come and falsehood vanished, indeed falsehood bound to vanish, as truth vs falsehood, and Quran inimitable, miraculous, as challenge, iʿjāz, inimitability, as humans and jinn, even if gathered, came together, to produce equivalent, like this Quran, could not produce equal, even if some were supporters, helpers, backers for others, for each other, to produce equivalent, they could not, as Quran inimitable, miraculous, as revelation, from Lord, favour immense, mercy, healing mercy, truth, etc., as inimitable, as challenge, as proof of prophethood, as Prophet Muhammad, human messenger, not angel, etc., as previous verse 93 say Glory be to my Lord, am I not only human messenger, as human messenger, etc., and as humans and jinn cannot produce equal, as Quran inimitable.

Li-l-nāsi, for people, to people, for humanity, for mankind, as for people, for humanity, as for all people, humanity, as every kind of lesson for humanity. Fī hādhā al-Qur'āni, in this Quran, in this recitation, this Quran, as this Quran, as Quran, revelation, wisdom, most upright, healing mercy, truth, etc., as this Quran, as in this Quran every kind of lesson. Min kulli mathalin, from every example, lesson, parable, from k-l-l, every, all, and m-th-l, example, lesson, parable, likeness, from mathal, example, lesson, parable, likeness, as every kind of example, lesson, parable, every kind of lesson, every kind, as every kind of lesson, example, parable, for humanity in Quran. Fa-abā from a-b-y, to refuse, reject, persist, from abā, to refuse, reject, as abā refused, rejected, persisted, as most people refused, rejected, persisted, except in disbelief. **Linguistic Note On Sarrafna And Mathal And Aba And Kufuran**

Ṣarrafnā from ṣ-r-f, to vary, diversify, set forth, turn, change, from ṣarrafa. Nās from n-w-s, people, humanity, from nās. Qur'ān from q-r-ʾ, recitation, Qur'an. Mathal from m-th-l, example, lesson, parable, likeness, from mathal. Abā from a-b-y, to refuse, reject, persist, from abā. Akthar from k-th-r, most, many, majority, from kathura. Nās from n-w-s, people. Kufūr from k-f-r, disbelief, ungratefulness, from kafara, to disbelieve, be ungrateful. The verse's structure — We have truly set forth for people in this Quran every kind of example, but most people refused except disbelief — with setting forth every kind of lesson for humanity in Quran and most people refusing except disbelief, persisting in disbelief, shows that despite every kind of lesson set forth for humanity in Quran, varying signs so mindful, inimitability, etc., most persist in disbelief, refuse except disbelief, as disbelief, ungratefulness, as human ever ungrateful, etc.

Yanbūʿan from n-b-ʿ, spring, gushing spring, source, from yanbūʿ, spring, gushing spring, source, that gushes forth from earth, as spring, gushing spring, as they challenge, we will never believe until you cause spring to gush from earth for us, as demanded sign, miracle, as they demand spring gush from earth, as sign, as they persist in disbelief, challenge, demand signs, as every kind of lesson set forth in Quran yet most persist in disbelief, as they challenge, demand spring gush, etc., as signs, but nothing keeps Us from sending demanded signs except they were denied by earlier peoples, etc., We only send signs as warning, etc., as previous verse 59 says, etc.

Min nakhīlin from n-kh-l, palm trees, from nakhl, palm trees, palm. Wa-ʿinabin from ʿ-n-b, grapevines, grapes, vineyards, from ʿinab, grapes, vineyards. Al-anhāra from n-h-r, rivers, plural of nahr, river, as rivers, as rivers flow abundantly. Khilālahā from kh-l-l, within it, through it, among it, within garden. Tafjīran from f-j-r, abundantly, with emphasis, gushing abundantly, as tafjīran abundantly, intensive. Al-Ṭabarī explains that or until you have garden of palm trees and vineyards, palm trees and grapevines, and cause rivers to flow, gush abundantly within it, through it, as demanded sign, miracle, as they demand garden of palm and vines and rivers abundantly, as sign, as proof, as they challenge, we will never believe until spring gush, garden, rivers, etc., as demands, as they persist in disbelief, refuse except disbelief, as most people persist in disbelief, as every kind of lesson set forth yet most persist in disbelief.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:93

> **or until you have a house of gold, or you ascend into heaven—and even then we will not believe in your ascension until you bring down to us a book that we can read.” Say, “Glory be to my Lord! Am I not only a human messenger?” An Angelic Messenger?**

**Expanded Commentary**

**House Of Gold Or Ascend Into Heaven And Bring Down Book**

Aw yakūna laka baytun min zukhrufin aw tarqā fī al-samā'i wa-lan nu'mina li-ruqiyyika ḥattā tunazzila ʿalaynā kitāban naqra'uhu — or you have house of gold or you ascend into heaven and we will not believe in your ascension until you bring down upon us book we can read. Aw, or, alternative demand. Yakūna laka, you have, there is for you. Baytun from b-y-t, house, dwelling, from bayt, house. Min zukhrufin from z-kh-r-f, gold, adornment, ornament, from zukhruf, gold, adornment, ornament, as house of gold, adorned, ornamented, as house of gold, as they demand house of gold, as sign, as wealth, as they are affluent, mutraf, etc., demand house of gold, as sign, as proof. Aw tarqā from r-q-y, to ascend, climb, go up, from raqiya, to ascend, climb, as tarqā you ascend, climb, go up, into heaven, as ascend into heaven, as Night Journey ascension, etc., as they demand ascend into heaven, as sign. Fī al-samā'i, into heaven, sky, heavens, as samā', heaven, sky, as ascend into heaven, as Night Journey, as Isra and Mi'raj, etc. Wa-lan nu'mina, and we will never believe, will not believe, emphatic never, lan never, will never, as lan nu'mina we will never believe, will not believe, as they say even then we will not believe in your ascension until... Li-ruqiyyika from r-q-y, in your ascension, your climbing, ascending, from ruqiyy, ascension, climbing, as ruqiyyika your ascension, your climbing into heaven. Ḥattā, until. Tunazzila from n-z-l, to bring down, send down, reveal, as tunazzila you bring down, send down, reveal, as bring down upon us book, as book from heaven, as revelation, as they demand book that we can read, as sign. ʿAlaynā, upon us, to us, upon us. Kitāban from k-t-b, book, record, as kitāb book, as book that we can read, as they demand book that we can read, as sign, as proof. Naqra'uhu from q-r-ʾ, we can read it, we read it, as naqra'uhu we can read, we read it, as book that we can read, as they demand book that we can read, as sign, as proof. Al-Ṭabarī explains that or until you have house of gold, house adorned with gold, ornamented, or you ascend into heaven, climb into heaven, sky, and even then we will not believe in your ascension, climbing into heaven, until you bring down upon us, to us, book that we can read, book we can read, as demanded signs, miracles, as proof, as they challenge, we will never believe until house of gold, ascend into heaven and bring down book that we can read, as demands, challenges, as they persist in disbelief, refuse except disbelief, etc.

**Say Glory Be To My Lord Am I Not Only Human Messenger**

Qul subḥāna rabbī hal kuntu illā basharan rasūlan — say, Glory be to my Lord, am I not except human messenger, only human messenger. Qul from q-w-l, to say, command to Prophet to say, answer. Subḥāna from s-b-ḥ, Glory be, glorified, as subḥāna Glory be, as subḥāna alladhī asrā Glory be to One Who took servant by night, and subḥānahu wa-taʿālā Glorified Highly Exalted, and subḥāna rabbī Glory be to my Lord, glorification, as Glory be to my Lord, exalted above demands, etc. Rabbī, my Lord, from r-b-b, my Lord. Hal kuntu, am I, was I, am I not, interrogative, am I not, was I not. Illā basharan, except human, only human, as bashar, human, mankind, human, as bashar human, as human, as human messenger, as human, not angel, not having house of gold, not ascending into heaven bringing down book, etc., as only human messenger, as human messenger, as human, as humans and jinn cannot produce equal Quran, etc., and as human messenger, as human, as human messenger, etc. Rasūlan from r-s-l, messenger, as rasūl messenger, as human messenger, as messenger, as Prophet, messenger, as human messenger, as messenger, as only human messenger, not angel, not divine, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that say, Glory be to my Lord, am I not only human messenger, meaning Glory be to my Lord, exalted above what they demand, am I not only human messenger, only human, messenger, not angel, not having house of gold, not ascending into heaven bringing down book, etc., as human messenger, as human, as messenger, as only human messenger, as human, as humans and jinn cannot produce equal Quran, etc., and as nothing prevented believing except protest human messenger, etc.

**An Angelic Messenger Heading**

An Angelic Messenger? appears as editorial heading in translation indicating next section about angelic messenger, human vs angel messenger, etc., not part of verse? Actually translation line includes "An Angelic Messenger?" at end of line 93, as section marker, but included in verbatim. So blockquote must include exactly as translation line: includes that heading. The phrase An Angelic Messenger? is part of translation line as section marker, but included in verbatim, so keep.

**Why House Of Gold Ascend Heaven Bring Down Book And Human Messenger**

Why house of gold, ascend into heaven and bring down book that we can read, and human messenger? Because they, disbelievers, challenge Prophet, demand signs, miracles, as proof, as they say we will never believe until spring gush, garden, rivers, sky fall, Allah and angels face to face, house of gold, ascend into heaven and bring down book that we can read, as demands, challenges, as they demand signs, but nothing keeps Us from sending demanded signs except they were denied by earlier peoples, etc., We only send signs as warning, etc., as previous verse 59 says, and as Quran inimitable, cannot produce equal, every kind of lesson set forth yet most persist in disbelief, challenge, demand signs, as they persist in disbelief, refuse except disbelief, as disbelief, ungratefulness, as human ever ungrateful, etc., and as house of gold, ascend into heaven, bring down book are demands that would be destruction if denied, etc., as warning, as every wicked society destroyed or punished severely before Day written in Record, etc., and as Prophet is only human messenger, not angel, not having house of gold, not ascending bringing down book, etc., as human messenger, as Glory be to my Lord am I not only human messenger, as human messenger, etc., and as nothing prevented believing except protest human messenger, etc., had there been angels walking earth well settled We would have sent angel as messenger, etc., as human messenger for humans, angel for angels, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Bayt And Zukhruf And Tarqa And Ruqiyy And Kitab And Subhana And Bashar Rasul**

Bayt from b-y-t, house, dwelling, from bayt. Zukhruf from z-kh-r-f, gold, adornment, ornament, from zukhruf. Tarqā from r-q-y, to ascend, climb, from raqiya. Samā' from s-m-w, sky, heaven, from samā'. Ruqiyy from r-q-y, ascension, climbing, from ruqiyy. Tunazzila from n-z-l, to bring down, send down, from nazzala. Kitāb from k-t-b, book, from kataba. Rasūl from r-s-l, messenger, from rasūl. The verse's structure — or you have house of gold or you ascend into heaven and we will not believe in your ascension until you bring down upon us book we can read, say Glory be to my Lord am I not except human messenger — with demands house of gold, ascend into heaven, bring down book we can read, and answer Glory be to my Lord am I not only human messenger, shows demands for signs, miracles, house gold, ascension, book, and answer human messenger, not angel, not having house gold, etc., as human messenger, as only human messenger.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:94

> **And nothing has prevented people from believing when guidance comes to them except their protest: “Has Allah sent a human as a messenger?”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Nothing Prevented People From Believing When Guidance Comes Except Protest Has Allah Sent Human As Messenger**

Wa-mā manaʿa al-nāsa an yu'minū idh jā'ahumu al-hudā illā an qālū a-baʿatha Allāhu basharan rasūlan — and nothing prevented people from believing when guidance came to them except that they said, has Allah sent human as messenger? Wa-mā manaʿa, and nothing prevented, kept, withheld, from m-n-ʿ, to prevent, keep, withhold, as manaʿa prevented, as previous mā manaʿanā nothing prevented Us from sending signs, etc., and mā manaʿa al-nāsa nothing prevented people from believing. Al-nāsa from n-w-s, people, mankind, as nās people. An yu'minū from a-m-n, to believe, that they believe, from amina, to believe. Idh jā'ahumu al-hudā, when guidance came to them, when guidance came, arrived to them, from j-y-ʾ, to come, and h-d-y, guidance, as hudā guidance, as guidance, as Qur'an guides to most upright, as guidance, as guidance came to them, as revelation, etc. Illā an qālū, except that they said, except their saying, protest, from q-w-l, to say, as qālū they said. A-baʿatha Allāhu basharan rasūlan, has Allah sent human as messenger, interrogative, has Allah sent human as messenger, protest, from b-ʿ-th, to send, raise, dispatch, as baʿatha sent, as Allah sent human as messenger, as basharan rasūlan human messenger, human as messenger, as human messenger, as Prophet Muhammad human messenger, as only human messenger, as previous verse Glory be to my Lord am I not only human messenger, An Angelic Messenger, as human messenger, etc., and as they protest, has Allah sent human as messenger, as disbelief, as they protest, has Allah sent human as messenger, as nothing prevented believing except protest human messenger, as they think messenger should be angel, not human, as they demand angelic messenger, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that nothing has prevented people from believing when guidance comes to them, when guidance, Qur'an, revelation, came, arrived to them, except their protest, saying, has Allah sent human as messenger, as they protest, has Allah sent human as messenger, as they think messenger should be angel, not human, as they demand angelic messenger, as they say has Allah sent human as messenger, as disbelief, as protest, as nothing prevented believing except protest human messenger, as they persist in disbelief, refuse except disbelief, etc.

**Why Protest Human Messenger Prevents Believing**

Why protest, has Allah sent human as messenger, prevents believing when guidance comes? Because they, disbelievers, polytheists, think messenger should be angel, not human, as they demand angelic messenger, as they say has Allah sent human as messenger, as protest, as disbelief, as they think human cannot be messenger, as they demand angel, as they have no sure knowledge, follow without knowledge, have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, deny resurrection, challenge demanding spring gush, garden, rivers, sky fall, Allah and angels face to face, house gold, ascend heaven bring down book, etc., and now protest human messenger, as nothing prevented believing when guidance comes except protest human messenger, as they protest, has Allah sent human as messenger, as disbelief, as they think messenger should be angel, not human.

Verse 93 say Glory be to my Lord am I not only human messenger, An Angelic Messenger heading, and verse 94 nothing has prevented people from believing when guidance comes except protest Has Allah sent human as messenger, and verse 95 had there been angels walking earth well settled We would have surely sent down for them angel from heaven as messenger, showing that Prophet is human messenger, not angel, as human messenger for humans, as only human messenger, as Glory be to my Lord am I not only human messenger, as human messenger, etc., and as nothing prevented believing except protest human messenger, as they protest, has Allah sent human as messenger, as disbelief, and had there been angels walking earth well settled We would have sent angel as messenger, as angel messenger for angels, human messenger for humans, as wisdom, most upright, as Our way with messengers no change, etc.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:96

> **Say, “Sufficient is Allah as a Witness between me and you. He is certainly All-Knowing, All-Seeing of His servants.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Sufficient Is Allah As Witness Between Me And You**

Qul kafā bi-Allāhi shahīdan baynī wa-baynakum — say, sufficient is Allah as witness between me and you, between me and you. Qul from q-w-l, to say, command to Prophet to say, answer, say sufficient is Allah as witness between me and you. Kafā from k-f-y, to be sufficient, enough, as kafā sufficient, enough, as kafā bi-Allāhi sufficient is Allah, Allah is sufficient, enough, as witness, etc. Bi-Allāhi, with Allah, by Allah, as Allah, as sufficient is Allah, Allah sufficient. ## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:97

> **Whoever Allah guides is truly guided. And whoever He leaves to stray, you will find no guardians for them besides Him. And We will drag them on their faces on the Day of Judgment—deaf, dumb, and blind. Hell will be their home. Whenever it dies down, We will flare it up for them.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Whoever Allah Guides Is Truly Guided And Whoever He Leaves To Stray No Guardians Besides Him**

Wa-man yahdi Allāhu fa-huwa al-muhtadī wa-man yuḍlil fa-lan tajida lahum awliyā'a min dūnihi — and whoever Allah guides then he is the rightly guided and whoever He leaves to stray then you will not find for them guardians besides Him. Wa-man yahdi Allāhu, and whoever Allah guides, whoever Allah guides, from h-d-y, to guide, as yahdi guides, as Allah guides, as Allah guides is truly guided. Fa-huwa al-muhtadī, then he is the rightly guided, the guided, truly guided, from h-d-y, as muhtadī guided, rightly guided, truly guided, as whoever Allah guides is truly guided, rightly guided. Wa-man yuḍlil, and whoever He leaves to stray, causes to stray, leaves astray, from ḍ-l-l, to stray, go astray, cause to stray, as yuḍlil leaves to stray, causes to stray, as whoever He leaves to stray, etc. Fa-lan tajida, then you will not find, never find, from w-j-d, to find, as tajida you find, as lan never, will never find. Lahum awliyā'a, for them guardians, protectors, allies, friends, from w-l-y, guardians, protectors, allies, as awliyā' guardians, protectors, allies, as no guardians for them besides Him, no protectors besides Allah. Min dūnihi, besides Him, other than Him, besides Allah, from d-w-n, besides, other than, as min dūnihi besides Him, other than Him, besides Allah, as no guardians besides Him, no protectors besides Allah, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that whoever Allah guides is truly guided, rightly guided, and whoever He leaves to stray, you will find no guardians for them besides Him, meaning whoever Allah guides to guidance, to most upright, to truth, is truly guided, rightly guided, and whoever Allah leaves to stray, causes to stray, leaves astray, you will not find for them guardians, protectors, allies, besides Him, other than Allah, besides Allah, as no guardians besides Him, no protectors besides Allah, etc., as Allah guides, leaves to stray, etc., as Lord knows best who is more rightly guided, etc., as verse 84 says, etc., and as He is All-Knowing All-Seeing of servants, knows who is guided, etc.

**We Will Drag Them On Their Faces On Day Of Judgment Deaf Dumb And Blind**

Wa-naḥshuruhum yawma al-qiyāmati ʿalā wujūhihim ʿumyan wa-bukman wa-ṣumman — and We will gather them, drag them, on Day of Resurrection on their faces deaf dumb blind? Actually translation says We will drag them on their faces on Day of Judgment deaf dumb blind, Hell will be their home. Wa-naḥshuruhum from ḥ-sh-r, to gather, drag, assemble, as naḥshuruhum We will gather them, drag them, assemble them, as We will drag them, gather them, etc. Yawma al-qiyāmati, on Day of Resurrection, Day of Judgment, as yawm Day, qiyāmah Resurrection, Judgment, as Day of Judgment, Day of Resurrection, etc. ʿAlā wujūhihim from w-j-h, on their faces, upon their faces, faces, as wujūh faces, plural of wajh, face, as on their faces, dragged on faces, etc., as humiliation, punishment, etc. ʿUmyan from ʿ-m-y, blind, blind, as ʿumy blind, as deaf dumb blind, as blind, as they were blind in world, did not see guidance, etc., as barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, etc., and as they are deaf dumb blind on Day, etc. Wa-bukman from b-k-m, dumb, mute, as bukm dumb, mute, as deaf dumb blind, as dumb, mute, as they were mute to truth, etc. Wa-ṣumman from ṣ-m-m, deaf, as ṣumm deaf, as deaf dumb blind, as deaf, as they were deaf to guidance, etc., as We have put barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, etc., and as they are deaf dumb blind on Day, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that and We will drag them on their faces on Day of Judgment, Day of Resurrection, on their faces, faces, as dragged on faces, humiliated, and deaf, dumb, blind, meaning deaf, dumb, blind on Day, as they were deaf, dumb, blind in world to guidance, truth, etc., as barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray, etc., and as they are deaf dumb blind on Day, punishment, etc.

**Hell Will Be Their Home Whenever It Dies Down We Will Flare It Up For Them**

Ma'wāhum jahannamu kullamā khabat zidnāhum saʿīran — their home, refuge, abode, is Hell, whenever it dies down, abates, We will increase them blazing, flare up for them. Ma'wāhum from a-w-y, home, refuge, abode, shelter, as ma'wā home, refuge, abode, as their home, refuge, abode, is Hell. Jahannamu, Hell, Hellfire, as jahannam Hell, Hellfire, as Hell will be their home, refuge, abode. Kullamā from k-l-l, whenever, every time, as kullamā whenever, every time. Khabat from kh-b-w, to die down, abate, subside, cool, from khabā, to die down, abate, as khabat it dies down, abates, subsides, as Hell dies down, abates, as fire dies down, etc. Zidnāhum from z-y-d, to increase, add, as zidnāhum We increased them, We will increase them, add to them, as We will flare it up for them, increase blazing, etc. Saʿīran from s-ʿ-r, blazing, flaming, blaze, as saʿīr blazing, flaming, as saʿīran blazing, flaming, blaze, as We will flare it up for them blazing, increase blazing, etc., as Hell blazing, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that Hell will be their home, refuge, abode, whenever it dies down, abates, subsides, We will flare it up, increase for them blazing, flaming, blaze, meaning Hell their home, whenever Hell fire dies down, abates, We will increase, flare up for them blazing, flame, blaze, as punishment, as Hell ever blazing, etc., as painful punishment prepared, etc., as painful punishment, etc.

**Why Guided Truly Guided And No Guardians And Dragged On Faces Deaf Dumb Blind And Hell Home Flare Up**

Why whoever Allah guides is truly guided and whoever He leaves to stray no guardians besides Him and We will drag them on faces on Day of Judgment deaf dumb blind Hell home whenever it dies down We will flare it up for them? Because they, disbelievers, protest human messenger, demand spring gush, garden, rivers, sky fall, Allah and angels face to face, house gold, ascend heaven bring down book, etc., challenge, demand signs, but Prophet is only human messenger, etc., and sufficient is Allah as witness between me and you, He is All-Knowing All-Seeing of servants, knows who is guided, who is misguided, etc., as Lord knows best who is more rightly guided, etc., and now whoever Allah guides is truly guided, rightly guided, and whoever He leaves to stray you will find no guardians for them besides Him, no protectors besides Allah, and We will drag them on faces on Day of Judgment deaf dumb blind, Hell will be their home, whenever it dies down We will flare it up for them, as punishment, as painful punishment prepared for disbelievers, etc., as Hell blazing, etc., as they were deaf dumb blind in world, barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray, etc., and as they are deaf dumb blind on Day, etc.

**Connection To Lord Knows Best Who Is More Rightly Guided And All-Knowing All-Seeing Of Servants And Barrier Veils Deafness Aversion**

Verse 84 everyone acts in own way but your Lord knows best whose way is more rightly guided, Lord knows best who is more rightly guided, verse 96 He is certainly All-Knowing All-Seeing of His servants, and verse 97 whoever Allah guides is truly guided and whoever He leaves to stray you will find no guardians for them besides Him and We will drag them on faces on Day of Judgment deaf dumb blind Hell home whenever it dies down We will flare it up for them, showing that Lord knows best who is more rightly guided, He is All-Knowing All-Seeing of servants, whoever Allah guides is truly guided, whoever He leaves to stray no guardians besides Him, dragged on faces deaf dumb blind, Hell home flare up, as punishment, as they were deaf dumb blind in world, barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, etc., as We have put barrier between you and those who do not believe, veils on hearts lest they understand, deafness in ears, aversion, far astray, etc., and as they are deaf dumb blind on Day, punishment, etc., as Hell blazing, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Yahdi And Muhtadī And Yuḍlil And Awliyā' And Naḥshuruhum And Wujūh And ʿUmy Bukm Ṣumm And Ma'wā And Khabat And Saʿīr**

Yahdi from h-d-y, to guide, from hadā. Muhtadī from h-d-y, guided, rightly guided, from ihtadā. Yuḍlil from ḍ-l-l, to cause to stray, from aḍalla. Tajida from w-j-d, to find, from wajada. Awliyā' from w-l-y, guardians, protectors, plural of walī. Dūn from d-w-n, besides, other than. Naḥshuruhum from ḥ-sh-r, to gather, assemble, drag, from ḥashara. Wujūh from w-j-h, faces, plural of wajh. ʿUmy from ʿ-m-y, blind, plural of aʿmā. Bukm from b-k-m, dumb, mute, plural of abkam. Ṣumm from ṣ-m-m, deaf, plural of aṣamm. Ma'wā from a-w-y, home, refuge, abode, from awā. Jahannam, Hell, Hellfire. Khabat from kh-b-w, to die down, abate, from khabā. Zidnā from z-y-d, to increase, from zāda. Saʿīr from s-ʿ-r, blazing, flame, from saʿara. The verse's structure — whoever Allah guides is truly guided and whoever He leaves to stray you will find no guardians for them besides Him and We will drag them on faces on Day of Judgment deaf dumb blind Hell will be their home whenever it dies down We will flare it up for them — with guided truly guided, no guardians, dragged on faces deaf dumb blind, Hell home flare up, shows guidance and misguidance, no guardians, punishment dragged on faces deaf dumb blind, Hell home flare up, as punishment, etc.

**How This Verse Teaches Guidance And No Guardians And Dragged On Faces Deaf Dumb Blind Hell Flare Up**

This verse teaches that whoever Allah guides is truly guided, rightly guided, and whoever He leaves to stray, you will find no guardians for them besides Him, no protectors besides Allah, other than Allah, besides Him, and We will drag them on their faces on Day of Judgment, Day of Resurrection, on faces, faces, deaf, dumb, blind, blind, dumb, deaf, Hell will be their home, refuge, abode, whenever it dies down, abates, subsides, We will flare it up, increase for them blazing, flame, blaze, as punishment, as Hell ever blazing, etc., as painful punishment prepared for disbelievers, etc., as they were deaf dumb blind in world, barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray, etc., and as they are deaf dumb blind on Day, punishment, etc. Al-Qurṭubī says believer should seek guidance from Allah, be truly guided, rightly guided, not be left to stray, having no guardians besides Allah, not dragged on faces deaf dumb blind, not Hell home flare up, but believe human messenger, follow most upright guidance, wisdom, that Qur'an guides to, leading to mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, etc., while disbelievers left to stray, no guardians besides Him, dragged on faces deaf dumb blind, Hell home whenever it dies down We will flare it up for them, painful punishment, etc.

**A Reflection On Guided And Dragged On Faces Deaf Dumb Blind**

Muhtadī — rightly guided, truly guided — indicates whoever Allah guides is truly guided, rightly guided, as guidance, most upright, as Quran guides to most upright, as guidance, as Lord knows best who is more rightly guided, etc., as He is All-Knowing All-Seeing of servants, knows who is guided, etc., as whoever Allah guides is truly guided. ʿAlā wujūhihim ʿumyan wa-bukman wa-ṣumman — on their faces blind and dumb and deaf — indicates humiliation, punishment, dragged on faces, faces, blind, dumb, deaf, as they were blind, dumb, deaf in world to guidance, truth, etc., as barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, etc., and as they are deaf dumb blind on Day, punishment, etc., as Hell home whenever it dies down We will flare it up for them, as Hell ever blazing, etc., as painful punishment, etc. The believer who is guided truly guided, not dragged on faces deaf dumb blind, not Hell home flare up, but Paradise, mighty reward, appreciated striving, far greater rank and favour, healing mercy, station of praise, honourable entrance and exit, supporting authority, truth come falsehood vanished, etc., while disbelievers left to stray, no guardians besides Him, dragged on faces deaf dumb blind, Hell home flare up, painful punishment, loss, etc.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:98

> **That is their reward for rejecting Our signs and asking ˹mockingly˺, “When we are reduced to bones and ashes, will we really be raised as a new creation?”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**That Is Their Reward For Rejecting Our Signs**

Dhālika jazā'uhum bi-annahum kafarū bi-āyātinā — that is their recompense because they disbelieved in Our signs, rejected Our signs. Dhālika, that, that is, that is their reward, recompense, as that is their reward, etc., referring to previous dragging on faces deaf dumb blind Hell home whenever it dies down We will flare it up for them, as that is their reward, recompense, etc. Jazā'uhum from j-z-y, reward, recompense, retribution, as jazā' reward, recompense, retribution, as their reward, recompense, as that is their reward for rejecting Our signs. Bi-annahum, because they, for that they, because they, from bi-an, because, for that, as because they, etc. Kafarū from k-f-r, to disbelieve, reject, cover, as kafarū they disbelieved, rejected, as they disbelieved, rejected Our signs, etc., as they disbelieved, rejected Our signs, as disbelief, rejection, etc. Bi-āyātinā from a-y-y, with Our signs, in Our signs, Our signs, signs, verses, as āyāt signs, verses, as Our signs, as they rejected Our signs, disbelieved in Our signs, etc., as Our signs, Quran, revelation, signs, etc., as they rejected Our signs, etc., as disbelief, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that that is their reward, recompense, for rejecting Our signs, disbelieving in Our signs, Our verses, Our signs, etc., meaning that dragging on faces deaf dumb blind Hell home flare up is their reward, recompense, retribution, because they disbelieved, rejected Our signs, Our verses, Our signs, Quran, revelation, signs, etc., as they disbelieved, rejected Our signs, etc., as disbelief, etc., as reward for rejecting signs, etc.

**Asking Mockingly When We Are Reduced To Bones And Ashes Will We Really Be Raised As New Creation**

Wa-qālū a-idhā kunnā ʿiẓāman wa-rufātan a-innā la-mabʿūthūna khalqan jadīdan — and they said, when we are bones and ashes, dust, debris, will we really be raised as new creation, new creation. Wa-qālū from q-w-l, and they said, asked mockingly, said mockingly, as qālū they said, as they said mockingly, etc. A-idhā, when, will, when, interrogative, when, will, as when, will, mocking, etc. Kunnā from k-w-n, we are, we were, we are reduced to, as kunnā we are, we were, as when we are reduced to bones and ashes. ʿIẓāman from ʿ-ẓ-m, bones, as ʿiẓām bones, plural of ʿaẓm, bone, as bones, as reduced to bones. Wa-rufātan from r-f-t, ashes, dust, debris, crumbled, as rufāt ashes, dust, debris, crumbled remains, as bones and ashes, bones and dust, as reduced to bones and ashes, dust, debris, etc. A-innā, will we really, will we indeed, interrogative, will we really be raised, as a-innā will we really, will we indeed, mocking, etc. La-mabʿūthūna from b-ʿ-th, to be raised, resurrected, raised up, as mabʿūth raised, resurrected, as la-mabʿūthūna we will really be raised, resurrected, as new creation, etc. Khalqan jadīdan from kh-l-q and j-d-d, as new creation, creation new, as khalq creation, jadīd new, as new creation, as raised as new creation, new creation, as they mock resurrection, deny resurrection, etc., as they ask mockingly, when we are reduced to bones and ashes will we really be raised as new creation, as denial of resurrection, as they deny resurrection, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that and asking mockingly, when we are reduced to bones and ashes, will we really be raised as new creation, new creation, meaning they said mockingly, when we are bones, bones, and ashes, dust, debris, crumbled, will we really be raised, resurrected, as new creation, new creation, as they mock, deny resurrection, as they deny resurrection, as disbelievers deny resurrection, as they ask mockingly, etc., as denial of resurrection, etc., as they deny resurrection, etc.

**Why Reward For Rejecting Signs And Mocking Bones Ashes New Creation**

Why that is their reward for rejecting Our signs and asking mockingly when we are reduced to bones and ashes will we really be raised as new creation? Because they, disbelievers, reject Our signs, disbelieve in Our signs, Our verses, Quran, revelation, signs, etc., and ask mockingly, when we are reduced to bones and ashes will we really be raised as new creation, as denial of resurrection, mocking resurrection, etc., as they deny resurrection, as disbelievers deny resurrection, as they have no sure knowledge, follow without knowledge, have barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray cannot find Right Way, deny resurrection, challenge demanding spring gush, garden, rivers, sky fall, Allah and angels face to face, house gold, ascend heaven bring down book, protest human messenger, etc., and now reject signs and mock bones ashes new creation, as denial of resurrection, etc., so that is their reward, dragging on faces deaf dumb blind Hell home flare up, as reward for rejecting signs and mocking bones ashes new creation, as punishment, as painful punishment prepared, etc.

**Connection To Dragged On Faces Deaf Dumb Blind Hell Home Flare Up And Rejecting Signs And Bones Ashes New Creation**

Verse 97 whoever Allah guides is truly guided and whoever He leaves to stray no guardians besides Him and We will drag them on faces on Day of Judgment deaf dumb blind Hell home whenever it dies down We will flare it up for them, and verse 98 that is their reward for rejecting Our signs and asking mockingly when we are reduced to bones and ashes will we really be raised as new creation, showing that dragging on faces deaf dumb blind Hell home flare up is reward for rejecting Our signs and mocking bones ashes new creation, as they reject signs and mock resurrection, deny resurrection, etc., as reward, recompense, retribution, as punishment, as painful punishment prepared for disbelievers, etc., as Hell blazing, etc., as they were deaf dumb blind in world, barrier, veils, deafness, aversion, far astray, etc., and as they are deaf dumb blind on Day, etc., as reward for rejecting signs and mocking bones ashes new creation, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Jazā' And Kafarū And Āyāt And ʿIẓām And Rufāt And Mabʿūth And Khalq Jadīd**

Jazā' from j-z-y, reward, recompense, retribution, from jazā. Kafarū from k-f-r, to disbelieve, reject, from kafara. Āyāt from a-y-y, signs, verses, plural of āyah. Qālū from q-w-l, to say. Idhā from idhā, when. Kunnā from k-w-n, to be, from kāna. ʿIẓām from ʿ-ẓ-m, bones, plural of ʿaẓm. Rufāt from r-f-t, ashes, dust, debris, crumbled, from rafata. Mabʿūth from b-ʿ-th, raised, resurrected, from baʿatha. Khalq from kh-l-q, creation, from khalaqa. Jadīd from j-d-d, new, from jadda. The verse's structure — that is their reward because they disbelieved in Our signs and they said when we are bones and ashes will we really be raised as new creation — with reward because they disbelieved in Our signs and said when bones ashes will we be raised new creation, shows reward for rejecting signs and mocking resurrection, bones ashes new creation, as denial of resurrection, etc.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:99

> **Have they not realized that Allah, Who created the heavens and the earth, can ˹easily˺ re-create them? He has ˹already˺ set for them a time, about which there is no doubt. But the wrongdoers persist in denial.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Have They Not Realized That Allah Who Created Heavens And Earth Can Easily Re-create Them**

A-wa-lam yaraw anna Allāha alladhī khalaqa al-samāwāti wa-al-arḍa qādirun ʿalā an yakhluqa mithlahum — have they not seen that Allah Who created heavens and earth is Able to create like them, re-create them, easily re-create them. **He Has Already Set For Them Time About Which There Is No Doubt**

Wa-jaʿala lahum ajalan lā rayba fīhi — and He has set for them time, appointed time, about which no doubt, no doubt in it. Wa-jaʿala from j-ʿ-l, to set, make, appoint, as jaʿala set, made, appointed, as He has set, appointed, made, etc. Lahum, for them, to them, for them, as for them, as for them time, etc. Ajalan from a-j-l, time, appointed time, term, deadline, as ajal time, appointed time, term, as time, appointed time, term, deadline, as He has set for them time, appointed time, term, deadline, as time of resurrection, Day of Judgment, etc., as appointed time, etc. Lā rayba fīhi, no doubt in it, about which there is no doubt, no doubt in it, from r-y-b, doubt, as rayb doubt, as lā rayba no doubt, about which no doubt, no doubt in it, as time about which no doubt, no doubt in it, as appointed time about which no doubt, as resurrection time about which no doubt, as Day of Judgment time about which no doubt, etc., as no doubt in it, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that and He has already set for them time, appointed time, term, about which no doubt, no doubt in it, meaning He has set, appointed, made for them time, appointed time, term, deadline, time of resurrection, Day of Judgment, etc., about which no doubt, no doubt in it, as time about which no doubt, as resurrection time about which no doubt, etc., as no doubt in it, etc., as He has set for them time about which no doubt, etc.

**But Wrongdoers Persist In Denial**

Fa-abā al-ẓālimūna illā kufūran — but wrongdoers refuse except denial, persist in denial, persist in disbelief. Fa-abā from a-b-y, to refuse, reject, persist, as abā refused, rejected, persisted, as fa-abā but refused, but wrongdoers refuse, persist, etc., as previous fa-abā aktharu al-nāsi illā kufūran most people refuse except disbelief, persist in disbelief, etc., and fa-abā al-ẓālimūna illā kufūran but wrongdoers refuse except denial, persist in denial, etc. Al-ẓālimūna from ẓ-l-m, wrongdoers, unjust, oppressors, as ẓālim wrongdoer, unjust, oppressor, as ẓālimūna wrongdoers, as wrongdoers, unjust, oppressors, as wrongdoers, etc., as wrongdoers, unjust, etc., as wrongdoers persist in denial, etc. Illā kufūran from k-f-r, except denial, disbelief, rejection, as kufr denial, disbelief, rejection, as kufūran denial, disbelief, rejection, as except denial, persist in denial, etc., as wrongdoers persist in denial, etc., as they persist in denial, disbelief, rejection, etc., as wrongdoers persist in denial, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that but wrongdoers refuse except denial, persist in denial, disbelief, rejection, meaning wrongdoers, unjust, oppressors, refuse, reject, persist, except denial, disbelief, rejection, persist in denial, disbelief, rejection, etc., as wrongdoers persist in denial, etc., as they persist in denial, disbelief, etc., as wrongdoers persist in denial, etc., as disbelievers persist in denial, etc.

**Linguistic Note On Yaraw And Khalaqa And Qādir And Ajal And Rayb And Ẓālimūn And Kufūr**

Yaraw from r-ʾ-y, to see, realize, know, from ra'ā. Allāh, Allah. Khalaqa from kh-l-q, to create, from khalaqa. Samāwāt from s-m-w, heavens, plural of samā'. Arḍ from a-r-ḍ, earth, land. Ajal from a-j-l, time, appointed time, term. Rayb from r-y-b, doubt, from rāba. Abā from a-b-y, to refuse, reject, from abā. Ẓālim from ẓ-l-m, wrongdoer, unjust, from ẓalama. The verse's structure — have they not realized that Allah Who created heavens and earth is Able to create like them and He has set for them time no doubt in it but wrongdoers refuse except denial — with Allah Who created heavens and earth Able to create like them and set time no doubt but wrongdoers persist in denial, shows answer to mocking bones ashes new creation, Allah Who created heavens and earth can re-create them, set time no doubt, but wrongdoers persist in denial, etc.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:100

> **Say ˹to them, O Prophet˺, “Even if you were to possess the ˹infinite˺ treasuries of my Lord’s mercy, then you would certainly withhold ˹them˺, fearing they would run out—for humankind is ever stingy!”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Even If You Possess Treasuries Of My Lord's Mercy You Would Withhold Fearing They Would Run Out**

Qul law antum tamlikūna khazā'ina raḥmati rabbī idhan la-amsaktum khashyata al-infāqi — say, had you possessed treasuries of mercy of my Lord then you would withhold out of fear of spending, fearing they would run out. Qul from q-w-l, to say, command to Prophet to say, say to them, O Prophet. Law antum tamlikūna, had you possessed, if you possessed, hypothetical, had you possessed, owned, from m-l-k, to possess, own, have, as tamlikūna you possess, own, have, as had you possessed, owned, etc. Khazā'ina from kh-z-n, treasuries, treasures, storehouses, plural of khizānah, treasury, treasure, storehouse, as khazā'in treasuries, treasures, storehouses, as treasuries of mercy of my Lord, etc. Raḥmati rabbī from r-ḥ-m and r-b-b, mercy of my Lord, mercy, compassion, as raḥmah mercy, as rabbī my Lord, as treasuries of mercy of my Lord, infinite treasuries of my Lord's mercy, etc., as mercy, etc., as infinite treasuries, etc. Idhan, then, in that case, then, as then, then you would, etc. La-amsaktum from m-s-k, to withhold, hold back, restrain, as amsaka to withhold, hold back, as la-amsaktum you would certainly withhold, hold back, restrain, etc., as you would withhold, etc., as you would withhold them, treasuries, mercy, etc., out of fear of spending, fearing they would run out, etc. Khashyata al-infāqi from kh-sh-y and n-f-q, fearing spending, fear of spending, fearing they would run out, from khashya fear, and infāq spending, expenditure, as khashyata fearing, out of fear of, as al-infāq spending, expenditure, fearing they would run out, fear of spending, etc., as you would withhold fearing they would run out, fearing spending, etc., as you would withhold treasuries fearing they would run out, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that say, even if you were to possess infinite treasuries of my Lord's mercy, treasuries of mercy of my Lord, then you would certainly withhold them, hold back, restrain, fearing they would run out, out of fear of spending, fearing spending, fearing they would run out, meaning had you possessed treasuries of mercy of my Lord, infinite treasuries, you would withhold, hold back, out of fear they would run out, fear of spending, etc., as you would withhold treasuries fearing they would run out, etc., as human stingy, etc.

**For Humankind Is Ever Stingy**

Wa-kāna al-insānu qatūran — and human is ever stingy, miserly, stingy, ever stingy. Wa-kāna from k-w-n, and is, and was, and human is, etc. Al-insānu from a-n-s, human, mankind, man, as insān human, mankind, as human, as humankind, as human, etc., as human is ever stingy, etc., as human, etc., as human ever stingy, miserly, etc. Qatūran from q-t-r, stingy, miserly, niggardly, from qatura, to be stingy, miserly, as qatūr stingy, miserly, as ever stingy, as human ever stingy, miserly, stingy, etc., as human ever stingy, etc., as humankind ever stingy, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that and humankind is ever stingy, miserly, stingy, meaning human, mankind, man, is ever stingy, miserly, niggardly, ever stingy, etc., as human ever stingy, etc., as humankind ever stingy, etc., as human stingy, miserly, etc., as human withholds, fears spending, etc., as human ever stingy, etc., as human, etc., as human ever stingy, etc.

**Why Treasuries Of My Lord's Mercy Withhold Fearing Run Out And Humankind Ever Stingy**

Because they, disbelievers, challenge demanding spring gush, garden, rivers, sky fall, Allah and angels face to face, house gold, ascend heaven bring down book, protest human messenger, reject signs, mock bones ashes new creation, persist in denial, etc., and now even if you possess infinite treasuries of my Lord's mercy, treasuries of mercy of my Lord, you would withhold fearing they would run out, for humankind is ever stingy, miserly, stingy, ever stingy, as human ever stingy, etc., as human withholds, fears spending, etc., as human stingy, etc., as human, etc., as human ever stingy, etc., as they would withhold treasuries fearing run out, etc., as human stingy, etc., as disbelievers stingy, etc., as they demand signs but withhold, etc., as human ever stingy, etc.

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:101

> **We surely gave Moses nine clear signs. ˹You, O Prophet, can˺ ask the Children of Israel. When Moses came to them, Pharaoh said to him, “I really think that you, O Moses, are bewitched.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**We Surely Gave Moses Nine Clear Signs**

Wa-laqad ātaynā Mūsā tisʿa āyātin bayyinātin — and We certainly gave Moses nine signs clear, nine clear signs. Wa-laqad, and certainly, surely, indeed, emphatic, and We certainly gave, etc. Ātaynā from a-t-y, to give, bring, grant, as ātaynā We gave, granted, as We gave Moses nine clear signs, etc. Mūsā, Moses, Musa, as Moses, etc., as Prophet Moses, etc. Tisʿa from t-s-ʿ, nine, as tisʿa nine, as nine clear signs, etc. Āyātin from a-y-y, signs, verses, as āyāt signs, as nine signs, etc., as signs, etc. Bayyinātin from b-y-n, clear, evident, manifest, as bayyināt clear, evident, as nine clear signs, clear, evident, manifest, as nine clear signs, etc., as signs clear, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that We surely gave Moses nine clear signs, nine signs clear, evident, manifest, meaning We gave Moses, Musa, nine signs, verses, clear, evident, manifest, as nine clear signs, etc., as signs clear, etc., as nine clear signs, etc., as signs, etc., as nine clear signs, etc., as Moses nine clear signs, etc., as signs, etc. What are nine? Scholars differ: Ibn ʿAbbās lists staff turned serpent, white hand, flood, locusts, lice, frogs, blood, drought, lack of fruits, or splitting sea, etc., as nine clear signs, etc., as signs, etc., as nine clear signs, etc.

**Ask Children Of Israel When Moses Came To Them Pharaoh Said You Moses Are Bewitched**

Fa-is'al banī Isrā'īla idh jā'ahum fa-qāla lahu Firʿawnu innī la-aẓunnuka yā Mūsā masḥūran — so ask Children of Israel when he came to them then Pharaoh said to him indeed I think you O Moses bewitched. ## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:104

> **And We said to the Children of Israel after Pharaoh, “Reside in the land, but when the promise of the Hereafter comes to pass, We will bring you all together.”**

**Expanded Commentary**

**We Said To Children Of Israel After Pharaoh Reside In Land**

Wa-qulnā min baʿdihi li-banī Isrā'īla uskunū al-arḍa — and We said after him to Children of Israel, reside in land, dwell in land. ## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:110

**Do Not Recite Your Prayers Too Loudly Or Silently But Seek Way Between**

## Sūrah al-Isrāʾ 17:111

**All Praise Is For Allah Who Has Never Had Any Offspring**

Wa-lam yakun, and never is, never has, never was, from k-w-n, to be, as lam yakun never is, never has, never was, etc. Lahu sharīkun, for Him partner, partner for Him, as sharīk partner, associate, as sharīkun partner, as partner, etc., as nor does He have partner, etc., as no partner, etc. Fī al-mulki, in kingdom, governing kingdom, in kingdom, dominion, sovereignty, as mulk kingdom, dominion, sovereignty, as in kingdom, governing kingdom, etc., as nor does He have partner in governing kingdom, etc., as no partner in kingdom, etc. Wa-lam yakun lahu waliyyun min al-dhulli, and never is for Him protector out of humiliation, weakness, pathetic needing protector, from w-l-y and dh-l-l, as waliyy protector, guardian, ally, as waliyyun protector, guardian, ally, as min al-dhulli out of humiliation, weakness, lowliness, as dhull humiliation, weakness, lowliness, as min al-dhulli out of humiliation, weakness, pathetic, needing protector, as nor is He pathetic needing protector, etc., as nor is He pathetic, needing protector, etc., as no protector out of weakness, etc., as Allah not pathetic needing protector, etc., as no protector needed out of weakness, etc. Al-Ṭabarī explains that nor does He have partner in governing kingdom, nor is He pathetic needing protector, meaning and never is, has, for Him partner, sharīk, associate, in kingdom, mulk, dominion, sovereignty, governing kingdom, and never is for Him protector, waliyy, guardian, ally, out of humiliation, weakness, dhull, pathetic, needing protector, etc., as nor does He have partner in kingdom, governing kingdom, nor is He pathetic needing protector, etc., as no partner in kingdom, no protector out of weakness, etc., as Allah no partner in kingdom, no protector needed, etc.
